     6	interface IVerifierDirect {
     7	    function verifyProof(
     8	        uint256[2] calldata a,
     9	        uint256[2][2] calldata b,
    10	        uint256[2] calldata c,
    11	        uint256[73] calldata input
    12	    )
    13	        external
    14	        view
    15	        returns (bool);
    16	}
    17	
    18	interface IVerifierDigest {
    19	    function verifyProof(
    20	        uint256[2] calldata a,
    21	        uint256[2][2] calldata b,
    22	        uint256[2] calldata c,
    23	        uint256[10] calldata input
    24	    )
    25	        external
    26	        view
    27	        returns (bool);
    28	}
    29	
    30	/*
    31	 * BabyJub affine twisted-Edwards addition.
    32	 *
    33	 * a*x^2 + y^2 = 1 + d*x^2*y^2
    34	 * a = 168700
    35	 * d = 168696
    36	 *
    37	 * This is intentionally instrumented in WP4 because its EVM
    38	 * cost is one of the authorized architecture review gates.
    39	 */
    40	library BabyJubAffine {
    41	    uint256 internal constant P =
    42	        21888242871839275222246405745257275088548364400416034343698204186575808495617;
    43	
    44	    uint256 internal constant A =
    45	        168700;
    46	
    47	    uint256 internal constant D =
    48	        168696;
    49	
    50	    struct Point {
    51	        uint256 x;
    52	        uint256 y;
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    function _submod(
    56	        uint256 x,
    57	        uint256 y
    58	    )
    59	        private
    60	        pure
    61	        returns (uint256)
    62	    {
    63	        return
    64	            x >= y
    65	                ? x - y
    66	                : P - (y - x);
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    /*
    70	     * EIP-198 modular exponentiation precompile.
    71	     *
    72	     * inverse(a) = a^(P-2) mod P
    73	     */
    74	    function _inverse(
    75	        uint256 x
    76	    )
    77	        private
    78	        view
    79	        returns (uint256 out)
    80	    {
    81	        require(
    82	            x != 0,
    83	            "BABYJUB_ZERO_DENOMINATOR"
    84	        );
    85	
    86	        bytes memory input =
    87	            abi.encode(
    88	                uint256(32),
    89	                uint256(32),
    90	                uint256(32),
    91	                x,
    92	                P - 2,
    93	                P
    94	            );
    95	
    96	        bytes memory output =
    97	            new bytes(32);
    98	
    99	        bool ok;
   100	
   101	        assembly ("memory-safe") {
   102	            ok := staticcall(
   103	                gas(),
   104	                5,
   105	                add(input, 32),
   106	                mload(input),
   107	                add(output, 32),
   108	                32
   109	            )
   110	        }
   111	
   112	        require(
   113	            ok,
   114	            "MODEXP_FAILED"
   115	        );
   116	
   117	        out =
   118	            abi.decode(
   119	                output,
   120	                (uint256)
   121	            );
   122	
   123	        require(
   124	            out != 0,
   125	            "BAD_INVERSE"
   126	        );
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    function add(
   130	        Point memory p1,
   131	        Point memory p2
   132	    )
   133	        internal
   134	        view
   135	        returns (Point memory r)
   136	    {
   137	        require(
   138	            p1.x < P &&
   139	            p1.y < P &&
   140	            p2.x < P &&
   141	            p2.y < P,
   142	            "NON_CANONICAL_POINT"
   143	        );
   144	
   145	        uint256 x1x2 =
   146	            mulmod(
   147	                p1.x,
   148	                p2.x,
   149	                P
   150	            );
   151	
   152	        uint256 y1y2 =
   153	            mulmod(
   154	                p1.y,
   155	                p2.y,
   156	                P
   157	            );
   158	
   159	        uint256 x1y2 =
   160	            mulmod(
   161	                p1.x,
   162	                p2.y,
   163	                P
   164	            );
   165	
   166	        uint256 y1x2 =
   167	            mulmod(
   168	                p1.y,
   169	                p2.x,
   170	                P
   171	            );
   172	
   173	        uint256 t =
   174	            mulmod(
   175	                x1x2,
   176	                y1y2,
   177	                P
   178	            );
   179	
   180	        uint256 dt =
   181	            mulmod(
   182	                D,
   183	                t,
   184	                P
   185	            );
   186	
   187	        uint256 numeratorX =
   188	            addmod(
   189	                x1y2,
   190	                y1x2,
   191	                P
   192	            );
   193	
   194	        uint256 numeratorY =
   195	            _submod(
   196	                y1y2,
   197	                mulmod(
   198	                    A,
   199	                    x1x2,
   200	                    P
   201	                )
   202	            );
   203	
   204	        uint256 denominatorX =
   205	            addmod(
   206	                1,
   207	                dt,
   208	                P
   209	            );
   210	
   211	        uint256 denominatorY =
   212	            _submod(
   213	                1,
   214	                dt
   215	            );
   216	
   217	        r.x =
   218	            mulmod(
   219	                numeratorX,
   220	                _inverse(
   221	                    denominatorX
   222	                ),
   223	                P
   224	            );
   225	
   226	        r.y =
   227	            mulmod(
   228	                numeratorY,
   229	                _inverse(
   230	                    denominatorY
   231	                ),
   232	                P
   233	            );
   234	    }
   235	}

   237	abstract contract VoteChainBase {
   238	    uint256 internal constant MAX =
   239	        WP4Profile.MAX_CANDIDATES;
   240	
   241	    uint256 internal constant P =
   242	        WP4Profile.FIELD_MODULUS;
   243	
   244	    enum Phase {
   245	        Registration,
   246	        Active,
   247	        Ended,
   248	        Finalized
   249	    }
   250	
   251	    struct ElectionConfig {
   252	        uint256 eligibleRoot;
   253	
   254	        uint256 electionId;
   255	
   256	        uint256 candidateManifestHash;
   257	
   258	        uint256 candidateCount;
   259

   267	
   268	    address public immutable admin;
   269

   271	
   272	    bool public configured;
   273	
   274	    uint256 public eligibleRoot;
   275	
   276	    uint256 public electionId;
   277	
   278	    uint256 public candidateManifestHash;
   279	
   280	    uint256 public candidateCount;
   281

   283

   285	    uint256 public Yy;
   286	
   287	    uint256 public electionContextHash;
   288	
   289	    uint256 public acceptedBallotCount;
   290	
   291	    /*
   292	     * WP6 threshold-finalization evidence.
   293	     *
   294	     * trusteeTranscriptHash is pinned during Registration.
   295	     * finalizationTranscriptHash is written only after Ended.
   296	     *
   297	     * The full threshold transcript remains public/off-chain;
   298	     * Solidity anchors its canonical SHA-256 hash and the
   299	     * independently verified plaintext aggregate totals.
   300	     */
   301	    bytes32 public trusteeTranscriptHash;
   302	
   303	    bytes32 public finalizationTranscriptHash;
   304	
   305	    uint256[16] internal finalResults;
   306	
   307	    mapping(uint256 => bool)
   308	        public usedNullifier;
   309	
   310	    uint256[16] internal tallyRx;
   311	    uint256[16] internal tallyRy;
   312	    uint256[16] internal tallySx;
   313	    uint256[16] internal tallySy;
   314	
   315	    event ElectionConfigured(
   316	        uint256 indexed electionId,
   317	        uint256 eligibleRoot,
   318	        uint256 candidateManifestHash,
   319	        uint256 candidateCount
   320	    );
   321	
   322	    event ElectionActivated(
   323	        uint256 indexed electionId
   324	    );
   325	
   326	    event ElectionEnded(
   327	        uint256 indexed electionId
   328	    );
   329	
   330	    event TrusteeTranscriptPinned(
   331	        bytes32 indexed transcriptHash
   332	    );
   333	
   334	    event TallyFinalized(
   335	        bytes32 indexed transcriptHash,
   336	        uint256 acceptedBallotCount
   337	    );
   338	
   339	    event EncryptedVoteAccepted(
   340	        uint256 indexed nullifier,
   341	        bytes32 ciphertextHash,
   342	        uint256 acceptedBallotCount
   343	    );
   344	
   345	    constructor() {
   346	        admin =
   347	            msg.sender;
   348	
   349	        phase =
   350	            Phase.Registration;
   351	
   352	        /*
   353	         * BabyJub identity = (0,1)
   354	         */
   355	        for (
   356	            uint256 i = 0;
   357	            i < MAX;
   358	            i++
   359	        ) {
   360	            tallyRy[i] = 1;
   361	            tallySy[i] = 1;
   362	        }
   363	    }
   364	
   365	    modifier onlyAdmin() {
   366	        require(
   367	            msg.sender == admin,
   368	            "ONLY_ADMIN"
   369	        );
   370	
   371	        _;
   372	    }
   373	
   374	    modifier inPhase(
   375	        Phase expected
   376	    ) {
   377	        require(
   378	            phase == expected,
   379	            "WRONG_PHASE"
   380	        );
   381	
   382	        _;
   383	    }
   384	
   385	    function configure(
   386	        ElectionConfig calldata cfg
   387	    )
   388	        external
   389	        onlyAdmin
   390	        inPhase(
   391	            Phase.Registration
   392	        )
   393	    {
   394	        require(
   395	            !configured,
   396	            "ALREADY_CONFIGURED"
   397	        );
   398	
   399	        require(
   400	            cfg.candidateCount >= 1 &&
   401	            cfg.candidateCount <= MAX,
   402	            "BAD_CANDIDATE_COUNT"
   403	        );
   404	
   405	        require(
   406	            cfg.eligibleRoot < P &&
   407	            cfg.candidateManifestHash < P &&
   408	            cfg.contractDomain < P &&
   409	            cfg.Yx < P &&
   410	            cfg.Yy < P &&
   411	            cfg.electionContextHash < P,
   412	            "NON_CANONICAL_CONFIG"
   413	        );
   414	
   415	        require(
   416	            cfg.Yx != 0,
   417	            "IDENTITY_PUBLIC_KEY"
   418	        );
   419	
   420	        eligibleRoot =
   421	            cfg.eligibleRoot;
   422	
   423	        electionId =
   424	            cfg.electionId;
   425	
   426	        candidateManifestHash =
   427	            cfg.candidateManifestHash;
   428	
   429	        candidateCount =
   430	            cfg.candidateCount;
   431	
   432	        contractDomain =
   433	            cfg.contractDomain;
   434	
   435	        Yx =
   436	            cfg.Yx;
   437	
   438	        Yy =
   439	            cfg.Yy;
   440	
   441	        electionContextHash =
   442	            cfg.electionContextHash;
   443	
   444	        configured =
   445	            true;
   446	
   447	        emit ElectionConfigured(
   448	            electionId,
   449	            eligibleRoot,
   450	            candidateManifestHash,
   451	            candidateCount
   452	        );
   453	    }
   454	
   455	    /*
   456	     * Pin the public DKG ceremony transcript before Active.
   457	     *
   458	     * The transcript verifier independently checks that the
   459	     * committed trustee public shares derive the configured Y.
   460	     * Existing WP4/WP5 deployment flows remain ABI-compatible:
   461	     * this is an additional registration-stage call.
   462	     */
   463	    function setTrusteeTranscriptHash(
   464	        bytes32 transcriptHash
   465	    )
   466	        external
   467	        onlyAdmin
   468	        inPhase(
   469	            Phase.Registration
   470	        )
   471	    {
   472	        require(
   473	            configured,
   474	            "NOT_CONFIGURED"
   475	        );
   476	
   477	        require(
   478	            transcriptHash !=
   479	                bytes32(0),
   480	            "ZERO_TRUSTEE_TRANSCRIPT"
   481	        );
   482	
   483	        require(
   484	            trusteeTranscriptHash ==
   485	                bytes32(0),
   486	            "TRUSTEE_TRANSCRIPT_ALREADY_SET"
   487	        );
   488	
   489	        trusteeTranscriptHash =
   490	            transcriptHash;
   491	
   492	        emit TrusteeTranscriptPinned(
   493	            transcriptHash
   494	        );
   495	    }
   496	
   497	    function activate()
   498	        external
   499	        onlyAdmin
   500	        inPhase(
   501	            Phase.Registration
   502	        )
   503	    {
   504	        require(
   505	            configured,
   506	            "NOT_CONFIGURED"
   507	        );
   508	
   509	        phase =
   510	            Phase.Active;
   511	
   512	        emit ElectionActivated(
   513	            electionId
   514	        );
   515	    }
   516	
   517	    function endElection()
   518	        external
   519	        onlyAdmin
   520	        inPhase(
   521	            Phase.Active
   522	        )
   523	    {
   524	        phase =
   525	            Phase.Ended;
   526	
   527	        emit ElectionEnded(
   528	            electionId
   529	        );
   530	    }
   531	
   532	    /*
   533	     * WP6 prototype finalization boundary.
   534	     *
   535	     * Threshold/DLEQ verification is performed by the public
   536	     * transcript verifier before this transaction is submitted.
   537	     * This contract:
   538	     *
   539	     *   - requires Ended,
   540	     *   - requires a registration-stage trustee transcript pin,
   541	     *   - enforces candidate padding,
   542	     *   - enforces sum(totals) == acceptedBallotCount,
   543	     *   - anchors the independently verified transcript hash,
   544	     *   - exposes plaintext totals only in Finalized.
   545	     *
   546	     * The transcript and proofs stay independently re-verifiable.
   547	     */
   548	    function finalizeTally(
   549	        uint256[16] calldata totals,
   550	        bytes32 transcriptHash
   551	    )
   552	        external
   553	        onlyAdmin
   554	        inPhase(
   555	            Phase.Ended
   556	        )
   557	    {
   558	        require(
   559	            trusteeTranscriptHash !=
   560	                bytes32(0),
   561	            "TRUSTEE_TRANSCRIPT_NOT_PINNED"
   562	        );
   563	
   564	        require(
   565	            transcriptHash !=
   566	                bytes32(0),
   567	            "ZERO_FINALIZATION_TRANSCRIPT"
   568	        );
   569	
   570	        uint256 activeSum;
   571	
   572	        for (
   573	            uint256 i = 0;
   574	            i < MAX;
   575	            i++
   576	        ) {
   577	            if (
   578	                i <
   579	                candidateCount
   580	            ) {
   581	                require(
   582	                    totals[i] <=
   583	                        acceptedBallotCount,
   584	                    "RESULT_EXCEEDS_BALLOTS"
   585	                );
   586	
   587	                activeSum +=
   588	                    totals[i];
   589	            } else {
   590	                require(
   591	                    totals[i] == 0,
   592	                    "NONZERO_PADDED_RESULT"
   593	                );
   594	            }
   595	
   596	            finalResults[i] =
   597	                totals[i];
   598	        }
   599	
   600	        require(
   601	            activeSum ==
   602	                acceptedBallotCount,
   603	            "RESULT_SUM_MISMATCH"
   604	        );
   605	
   606	        finalizationTranscriptHash =
   607	            transcriptHash;
   608	
   609	        phase =
   610	            Phase.Finalized;
   611	
   612	        emit TallyFinalized(
   613	            transcriptHash,
   614	            acceptedBallotCount
   615	        );
   616	    }
   617	
   618	    function getFinalResult(
   619	        uint256 slot
   620	    )
   621	        external
   622	        view
   623	        returns (uint256)
   624	    {
   625	        require(
   626	            phase ==
   627	                Phase.Finalized,
   628	            "NOT_FINALIZED"
   629	        );
   630	
   631	        require(
   632	            slot <
   633	                candidateCount,
   634	            "BAD_FINAL_RESULT_SLOT"
   635	        );
   636	
   637	        return
   638	            finalResults[slot];
   639	    }
   640	
   641	    function getTally(
   642	        uint256 slot
   643	    )
   644	        external
   645	        view
   646	        returns (
   647	            uint256 rx,
   648	            uint256 ry,
   649	            uint256 sx,
   650	            uint256 sy
   651	        )
   652	    {
   653	        require(
   654	            slot < MAX,
   655	            "BAD_SLOT"
   656	        );
   657	
   658	        return (
   659	            tallyRx[slot],
   660	            tallyRy[slot],
   661	            tallySx[slot],
   662	            tallySy[slot]
   663	        );
   664	    }
   665	
   666	    function _requireCanonicalCiphertext(
   667	        uint256[16] calldata rx,
   668	        uint256[16] calldata ry,
   669	        uint256[16] calldata sx,
   670	        uint256[16] calldata sy
   671	    )
   672	        internal
   673	        pure
   674	    {
   675	        for (
   676	            uint256 i = 0;
   677	            i < MAX;
   678	            i++
   679	        ) {
   680	            require(
   681	                rx[i] < P &&
   682	                ry[i] < P &&
   683	                sx[i] < P &&
   684	                sy[i] < P,
   685	                "NON_CANONICAL_CIPHERTEXT"
   686	            );
   687	        }
   688	    }
   689	
   690	    function _applyTally(
   691	        uint256[16] calldata rx,
   692	        uint256[16] calldata ry,
   693	        uint256[16] calldata sx,
   694	        uint256[16] calldata sy
   695	    )
   696	        internal
   697	    {
   698	        for (
   699	            uint256 i = 0;
   700	            i < MAX;
   701	            i++
   702	        ) {
   703	            BabyJubAffine.Point memory oldR =
   704	                BabyJubAffine.Point(
   705	                    tallyRx[i],
   706	                    tallyRy[i]
   707	                );
   708	
   709	            BabyJubAffine.Point memory newR =
   710	                BabyJubAffine.Point(
   711	                    rx[i],
   712	                    ry[i]
   713	                );
   714	
   715	            BabyJubAffine.Point memory oldS =
   716	                BabyJubAffine.Point(
   717	                    tallySx[i],
   718	                    tallySy[i]
   719	                );
   720	
   721	            BabyJubAffine.Point memory newS =
   722	                BabyJubAffine.Point(
   723	                    sx[i],
   724	                    sy[i]
   725	                );
   726	
   727	            BabyJubAffine.Point memory sumR =
   728	                BabyJubAffine.add(
   729	                    oldR,
   730	                    newR
   731	                );
   732	
   733	            BabyJubAffine.Point memory sumS =
   734	                BabyJubAffine.add(
   735	                    oldS,
   736	                    newS
   737	                );
   738	
   739	            tallyRx[i] =
   740	                sumR.x;
   741	
   742	            tallyRy[i] =
   743	                sumR.y;
   744	
   745	            tallySx[i] =
   746	                sumS.x;
   747	
   748	            tallySy[i] =
   749	                sumS.y;
   750	        }
   751	    }
   752	
   753	    function _accept(
   754	        uint256 nullifier,
   755	        uint256[16] calldata rx,
   756	        uint256[16] calldata ry,
   757	        uint256[16] calldata sx,
   758	        uint256[16] calldata sy
   759	    )
   760	        internal
   761	    {
   762	        require(
   763	            phase == Phase.Active,
   764	            "NOT_ACTIVE"
   765	        );
   766	
   767	        require(
   768	            !usedNullifier[
   769	                nullifier
   770	            ],
   771	            "NULLIFIER_USED"
   772	        );
   773	
   774	        usedNullifier[
   775	            nullifier
   776	        ] =
   777	            true;
   778	
   779	        _applyTally(
   780	            rx,
   781	            ry,
   782	            sx,
   783	            sy
   784	        );
   785	
   786	        acceptedBallotCount +=
   787	            1;
   788	
   789	        emit EncryptedVoteAccepted(
   790	            nullifier,
   791	            keccak256(
   792	                abi.encode(
   793	                    rx,
   794	                    ry,
   795	                    sx,
   796	                    sy
   797	                )
   798	            ),
   799	            acceptedBallotCount
   800	        );
   801	    }
   802	}
   803	
   804	contract VoteChainDirect is
   805	    VoteChainBase
   806	{
   807	    IVerifierDirect
   808	        public immutable verifier;
   809	
   810	    constructor(
   811	        address verifierAddress
   812	    ) {
   813	        require(
   814	            verifierAddress !=
   815	                address(0),
   816	            "ZERO_VERIFIER"
   817	        );
   818	
   819	        verifier =
   820	            IVerifierDirect(
   821	                verifierAddress
   822	            );
   823	    }
   824	
   825	    function _buildInputs(
   826	        uint256 nullifier,
   827	        uint256[16] calldata rx,
   828	        uint256[16] calldata ry,
   829	        uint256[16] calldata sx,
   830	        uint256[16] calldata sy
   831	    )
   832	        private
   833	        view
   834	        returns (
   835	            uint256[73] memory input
   836	        )
   837	    {
   838	        input[0] =
   839	            eligibleRoot;
   840	
   841	        input[1] =
   842	            electionId;
   843	
   844	        input[2] =
   845	            candidateManifestHash;
   846	
   847	        input[3] =
   848	            candidateCount;
   849	
   850	        input[4] =
   851	            contractDomain;
   852	
   853	        input[5] =
   854	            Yx;
   855	
   856	        input[6] =
   857	            Yy;
   858	
   859	        input[7] =
   860	            electionContextHash;
   861	
   862	        input[8] =
   863	            nullifier;
   864	
   865	        uint256 offset =
   866	            9;
   867	
   868	        for (
   869	            uint256 i = 0;
   870	            i < MAX;
   871	            i++
   872	        ) {
   873	            input[
   874	                offset + i
   875	            ] =
   876	                rx[i];
   877	        }
   878	
   879	        offset += MAX;
   880	
   881	        for (
   882	            uint256 i = 0;
   883	            i < MAX;
   884	            i++
   885	        ) {
   886	            input[
   887	                offset + i
   888	            ] =
   889	                ry[i];
   890	        }
   891	
   892	        offset += MAX;
   893	
   894	        for (
   895	            uint256 i = 0;
   896	            i < MAX;
   897	            i++
   898	        ) {
   899	            input[
   900	                offset + i
   901	            ] =
   902	                sx[i];
   903	        }
   904	
   905	        offset += MAX;
   906	
   907	        for (
   908	            uint256 i = 0;
   909	            i < MAX;
   910	            i++
   911	        ) {
   912	            input[
   913	                offset + i
   914	            ] =
   915	                sy[i];
   916	        }
   917	    }
   918	
   919	    function castVote(
   920	        uint256[2] calldata a,
   921	        uint256[2][2] calldata b,
   922	        uint256[2] calldata c,
   923	        uint256 nullifier,
   924	        uint256[16] calldata rx,
   925	        uint256[16] calldata ry,
   926	        uint256[16] calldata sx,
   927	        uint256[16] calldata sy
   928	    )
   929	        external
   930	    {
   931	        require(
   932	            phase == Phase.Active,
   933	            "NOT_ACTIVE"
   934	        );
   935	
   936	        require(
   937	            !usedNullifier[
   938	                nullifier
   939	            ],
   940	            "NULLIFIER_USED"
   941	        );
   942	
   943	        _requireCanonicalCiphertext(
   944	            rx,
   945	            ry,
   946	            sx,
   947	            sy
   948	        );
   949	
   950	        uint256[73] memory input =
   951	            _buildInputs(
   952	                nullifier,
   953	                rx,
   954	                ry,
   955	                sx,
   956	                sy
   957	            );
   958	
   959	        require(
   960	            verifier.verifyProof(
   961	                a,
   962	                b,
   963	                c,
   964	                input
   965	            ),
   966	            "INVALID_PROOF"
   967	        );
   968	
   969	        _accept(
   970	            nullifier,
   971	            rx,
   972	            ry,
   973	            sx,
   974	            sy
   975	        );
   976	    }
   977	}

   979	contract VoteChainPoseidon is
   980	    VoteChainBase
   981	{
   982	    IVerifierDigest
   983	        public immutable verifier;
   984	
   985	    address
   986	        public immutable poseidon5;
   987	
   988	    constructor(
   989	        address verifierAddress,
   990	        address poseidonAddress
   991	    ) {
   992	        require(
   993	            verifierAddress !=
   994	                address(0),
   995	            "ZERO_VERIFIER"
   996	        );
   997	
   998	        require(
   999	            poseidonAddress !=
  1000	                address(0),
  1001	            "ZERO_POSEIDON"
  1002	        );
  1003	
  1004	        verifier =
  1005	            IVerifierDigest(
  1006	                verifierAddress
  1007	            );
  1008	
  1009	        poseidon5 =
  1010	            poseidonAddress;
  1011	    }
  1012	
  1013	    function _poseidon(
  1014	        uint256[5] memory values
  1015	    )
  1016	        private
  1017	        view
  1018	        returns (uint256)
  1019	    {
  1020	        (
  1021	            bool ok,
  1022	            bytes memory data
  1023	        ) =
  1024	            poseidon5.staticcall(
  1025	                abi.encodeWithSignature(
  1026	                    "poseidon(uint256[5])",
  1027	                    values
  1028	                )
  1029	            );
  1030	
  1031	        require(
  1032	            ok &&
  1033	            data.length >= 32,
  1034	            "POSEIDON_CALL_FAILED"
  1035	        );
  1036	
  1037	        return
  1038	            abi.decode(
  1039	                data,
  1040	                (uint256)
  1041	            );
  1042	    }
  1043	
  1044	    function _binding(
  1045	        uint256[16] calldata rx,
  1046	        uint256[16] calldata ry,
  1047	        uint256[16] calldata sx,
  1048	        uint256[16] calldata sy
  1049	    )
  1050	        private
  1051	        view
  1052	        returns (uint256 state)
  1053	    {
  1054	        state =
  1055	            WP4Profile
  1056	                .CIPHERTEXT_BINDING_DOMAIN;
  1057	
  1058	        for (
  1059	            uint256 i = 0;
  1060	            i < MAX;
  1061	            i++
  1062	        ) {
  1063	            uint256[5] memory values;
  1064	
  1065	            values[0] =
  1066	                state;
  1067	
  1068	            values[1] =
  1069	                rx[i];
  1070	
  1071	            values[2] =
  1072	                ry[i];
  1073	
  1074	            values[3] =
  1075	                sx[i];
  1076	
  1077	            values[4] =
  1078	                sy[i];
  1079	
  1080	            state =
  1081	                _poseidon(
  1082	                    values
  1083	                );
  1084	        }
  1085	    }
  1086	
  1087	    function previewBindingDigest(
  1088	        uint256[16] calldata rx,
  1089	        uint256[16] calldata ry,
  1090	        uint256[16] calldata sx,
  1091	        uint256[16] calldata sy
  1092	    )
  1093	        external
  1094	        view
  1095	        returns (uint256)
  1096	    {
  1097	        _requireCanonicalCiphertext(
  1098	            rx,
  1099	            ry,
  1100	            sx,
  1101	            sy
  1102	        );
  1103	
  1104	        return
  1105	            _binding(
  1106	                rx,
  1107	                ry,
  1108	                sx,
  1109	                sy
  1110	            );
  1111	    }
  1112	
  1113	    function castVote(
  1114	        uint256[2] calldata a,
  1115	        uint256[2][2] calldata b,
  1116	        uint256[2] calldata c,
  1117	        uint256 nullifier,
  1118	        uint256[16] calldata rx,
  1119	        uint256[16] calldata ry,
  1120	        uint256[16] calldata sx,
  1121	        uint256[16] calldata sy
  1122	    )
  1123	        external
  1124	    {
  1125	        require(
  1126	            phase == Phase.Active,
  1127	            "NOT_ACTIVE"
  1128	        );
  1129	
  1130	        require(
  1131	            !usedNullifier[
  1132	                nullifier
  1133	            ],
  1134	            "NULLIFIER_USED"
  1135	        );
  1136	
  1137	        _requireCanonicalCiphertext(
  1138	            rx,
  1139	            ry,
  1140	            sx,
  1141	            sy
  1142	        );
  1143	
  1144	        uint256 digest =
  1145	            _binding(
  1146	                rx,
  1147	                ry,
  1148	                sx,
  1149	                sy
  1150	            );
  1151

  1153	
  1154	        input[0] =
  1155	            eligibleRoot;
  1156	
  1157	        input[1] =
  1158	            electionId;
  1159	
  1160	        input[2] =
  1161	            candidateManifestHash;
  1162	
  1163	        input[3] =
  1164	            candidateCount;
  1165	
  1166	        input[4] =
  1167	            contractDomain;
  1168	
  1169	        input[5] =
  1170	            Yx;
  1171	
  1172	        input[6] =
  1173	            Yy;
  1174	
  1175	        input[7] =
  1176	            electionContextHash;
  1177	
  1178	        input[8] =
  1179	            nullifier;
  1180	
  1181	        input[9] =
  1182	            digest;
  1183	
  1184	        require(
  1185	            verifier.verifyProof(
  1186	                a,
  1187	                b,
  1188	                c,
  1189	                input
  1190	            ),
  1191	            "INVALID_PROOF"
  1192	        );
  1193	
  1194	        _accept(
  1195	            nullifier,
  1196	            rx,
  1197	            ry,
  1198	            sx,
  1199	            sy
  1200	        );
  1201	    }
  1202	}

  1204	contract BabyJubTallyHarness {
  1205	    uint256[16] internal tallyRx;
  1206	    uint256[16] internal tallyRy;
  1207	    uint256[16] internal tallySx;
  1208	    uint256[16] internal tallySy;
  1209	
  1210	    constructor() {
  1211	        for (
  1212	            uint256 i = 0;
  1213	            i < 16;
  1214	            i++
  1215	        ) {
  1216	            tallyRy[i] = 1;
  1217	            tallySy[i] = 1;
  1218	        }
  1219	    }
  1220	
  1221	    function applyBallot(
  1222	        uint256[16] calldata rx,
  1223	        uint256[16] calldata ry,
  1224	        uint256[16] calldata sx,
  1225	        uint256[16] calldata sy
  1226	    )
  1227	        external
  1228	    {
  1229	        for (
  1230	            uint256 i = 0;
  1231	            i < 16;
  1232	            i++
  1233	        ) {
  1234	            BabyJubAffine.Point memory sumR =
  1235	                BabyJubAffine.add(
  1236	                    BabyJubAffine.Point(
  1237	                        tallyRx[i],
  1238	                        tallyRy[i]
  1239	                    ),
  1240	                    BabyJubAffine.Point(
  1241	                        rx[i],
  1242	                        ry[i]
  1243	                    )
  1244	                );
  1245	
  1246	            BabyJubAffine.Point memory sumS =
  1247	                BabyJubAffine.add(
  1248	                    BabyJubAffine.Point(
  1249	                        tallySx[i],
  1250	                        tallySy[i]
  1251	                    ),
  1252	                    BabyJubAffine.Point(
  1253	                        sx[i],
  1254	                        sy[i]
  1255	                    )
  1256	                );
  1257	
  1258	            tallyRx[i] =
  1259	                sumR.x;
  1260	
  1261	            tallyRy[i] =
  1262	                sumR.y;
  1263	
  1264	            tallySx[i] =
  1265	                sumS.x;
  1266	
  1267	            tallySy[i] =
  1268	                sumS.y;
  1269	        }
  1270	    }
  1271	}

   389	    /*
   390	     * Critical compatibility gate:
   391	     * generated Poseidon-5 EVM bytecode must match
   392	     * the exact WP3/Circom hash vector.
   393	     */
   394	    function testPoseidonEvmMatchesCircuitVector()
   395	        public
   396	    {
   397	        address p =
   398	            _deployPoseidon();
   399	
   400	        (
   401	            uint256[5] memory input,
   402	            uint256 expected
   403	        ) =
   404	            WP4Fixture
   405	                .poseidonFirstVector();
   406	
   407	        (
   408	            bool ok,
   409	            bytes memory data
   410	        ) =
   411	            p.staticcall(
   412	                abi.encodeWithSignature(
   413	                    "poseidon(uint256[5])",
   414	                    input
   415	                )
   416	            );
   417	
   418	        require(
   419	            ok &&
   420	            data.length >= 32,
   421	            "POSEIDON_STATICCALL_FAILED"
   422	        );
   423	
   424	        uint256 got =
   425	            abi.decode(
   426	                data,
   427	                (uint256)
   428	            );
   429	
   430	        require(
   431	            got == expected,
   432	            "POSEIDON_EVM_CIRCUIT_MISMATCH"
   433	        );
   434	    }

   658	        public
   659	    {
   660	        (
   661	            VoteChainDirect d,
   662	        ) =
   663	            _newDirect(
   664	                true
   665	            );
   666	
   667	        (
   668	            VoteChainPoseidon p,
   669	            ,
   670	        ) =

   672	                true
   673	            );

   678	        uint256[73] memory di;
   679

   683	            dc,
   684	            di
   685	        ) =

   714	        ) =

   721	                        dc,

   756	            !poseidonOk,
   757	            "POSEIDON_TAMPER_ACCEPTED"
   758	        );
   759	    }

   761	    function testLifecycleAndConfigFreeze()
   762	        public
   763	    {
   764	        (
   765	            VoteChainDirect d,
   766	        ) =
   767	            _newDirect(
   768	                false
   769	            );
   770	
   771	        /*
   772	         * Vote before Active.
   773	         */
   774	        uint256[2] memory a;
   775	        uint256[2][2] memory b;
   776	        uint256[2] memory c;
   777	        uint256[73] memory ignored;
   778	
   779	        (
   780	            a,
   781	            b,
   782	            c,
   783	            ignored
   784	        ) =
   785	            WP4Fixture.directA();
   786	
   787	        (
   788	            uint256 nullifier,
   789	            uint256[16] memory rx,
   790	            uint256[16] memory ry,
   791	            uint256[16] memory sx,
   792	            uint256[16] memory sy
   793	        ) =
   794	            WP4Fixture.ballotA();
   795	
   796	        (
   797	            bool beforeActive,
   798	        ) =
   799	            address(d).call(
   800	                abi.encodeCall(
   801	                    VoteChainDirect.castVote,
   802	                    (
   803	                        a,
   804	                        b,
   805	                        c,
   806	                        nullifier,
   807	                        rx,
   808	                        ry,
   809	                        sx,
   810	                        sy
   811	                    )
   812	                )
   813	            );
   814	
   815	        require(
   816	            !beforeActive,
   817	            "VOTE_BEFORE_ACTIVE"
   818	        );
   819	
   820	        /*
   821	         * Unauthorized activation.
   822	         */
   823	        vm.prank(
   824	            address(0xBEEF)
   825	        );
   826	
   827	        (
   828	            bool unauthorized,
   829	        ) =
   830	            address(d).call(
   831	                abi.encodeWithSignature(
   832	                    "activate()"
   833	                )
   834	            );
   835	
   836	        require(
   837	            !unauthorized,
   838	            "UNAUTHORIZED_ACTIVATE"
   839	        );
   840	
   841	        d.activate();
   842	
   843	        /*
   844	         * Config is frozen.
   845	         */
   846	        VoteChainBase
   847	            .ElectionConfig
   848	            memory cfg =
   849	                _config();
   850	
   851	        (
   852	            bool reconfigured,
   853	        ) =
   854	            address(d).call(
   855	                abi.encodeWithSelector(
   856	                    bytes4(
   857	                        keccak256(
   858	                            "configure((uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256))"
   859	                        )
   860	                    ),
   861	                    cfg
   862	                )
   863	            );
   864	
   865	        require(
   866	            !reconfigured,
   867	            "CONFIG_CHANGED_AFTER_ACTIVE"
   868	        );
   869	
   870	        d.endElection();
   871	
   872	        (
   873	            bool afterEnded,
   874	        ) =
   875	            address(d).call(
   876	                abi.encodeCall(
   877	                    VoteChainDirect.castVote,
   878	                    (
   879	                        a,
   880	                        b,
   881	                        c,
   882	                        nullifier,
   883	                        rx,
   884	                        ry,
   885	                        sx,
   886	                        sy
   887	                    )
   888	                )
   889	            );
   890	
   891	        require(
   892	            !afterEnded,
   893	            "VOTE_AFTER_END"
   894	        );
   895	    }

   967	
   968	    function _calldataIntrinsicGas(
   969	        bytes memory data
   970	    )
   971	        internal
   972	        pure
   973	        returns (uint256 total)
   974	    {
   975	        for (
   976	            uint256 i = 0;
   977	            i < data.length;
   978	            i++
   979	        ) {
   980	            total +=
   981	                data[i] == 0
   982	                    ? 4
   983	                    : 16;
   984	        }
   985	    }
   986	
   987	    function _gasLine(
   988	        string memory metric,
   989	        string memory variant,
   990	        uint256 value,
   991	        string memory unit
   992	    )
   993	        internal
   994	    {
   995	        vm.writeLine(
   996	            "gas/wp4-gas.csv",
   997	            string.concat(
   998	                metric,
   999	                ",",
  1000	                variant,
  1001	                ",",
  1002	                vm.toString(
  1003	                    value
  1004	                ),
  1005	                ",",
  1006	                unit
  1007	            )
  1008	        );
  1009	    }
  1010	
  1011	    function testZZGasGate()
  1012	        public
  1013	    {
  1014	        vm.writeFile(
  1015	            "gas/wp4-gas.csv",
  1016	            "metric,variant,value,unit\n"
  1017	        );
  1018	
  1019	        /*
  1020	         * Verifier deployments.
  1021	         */
  1022	        uint256 g0 =
  1023	            gasleft();
  1024	
  1025	        VoteChainVoteDirectVerifier dv =
  1026	            new VoteChainVoteDirectVerifier();
  1027	
  1028	        uint256 directVerifierDeploy =
  1029	            g0 -
  1030	            gasleft();
  1031	
  1032	        g0 =
  1033	            gasleft();
  1034	
  1035	        VoteChainVoteDigestVerifier pv =
  1036	            new VoteChainVoteDigestVerifier();
  1037	
  1038	        uint256 poseidonVerifierDeploy =
  1039	            g0 -
  1040	            gasleft();
  1041	
  1042	        /*
  1043	         * Poseidon raw creation-code deployment.
  1044	         */
  1045	        bytes memory poseidonCode =
  1046	            vm.readFileBinary(
  1047	                "testdata/poseidon5.creation.bin"
  1048	            );
  1049	
  1050	        g0 =
  1051	            gasleft();
  1052	
  1053	        address poseidon5 =
  1054	            _deployRaw(
  1055	                poseidonCode
  1056	            );
  1057	
  1058	        uint256 poseidonDeploy =
  1059	            g0 -
  1060	            gasleft();
  1061	
  1062	        /*
  1063	         * Voting contracts.
  1064	         */
  1065	        g0 =
  1066	            gasleft();
  1067	
  1068	        VoteChainDirect d =
  1069	            new VoteChainDirect(
  1070	                address(dv)
  1071	            );
  1072	
  1073	        uint256 directContractDeploy =
  1074	            g0 -
  1075	            gasleft();
  1076	
  1077	        g0 =
  1078	            gasleft();
  1079	
  1080	        VoteChainPoseidon p =
  1081	            new VoteChainPoseidon(
  1082	                address(pv),
  1083	                poseidon5
  1084	            );
  1085	
  1086	        uint256 poseidonContractDeploy =
  1087	            g0 -
  1088	            gasleft();
  1089	
  1090	        VoteChainBase
  1091	            .ElectionConfig
  1092	            memory cfg =
  1093	                _config();
  1094	
  1095	        d.configure(
  1096	            cfg
  1097	        );
  1098	
  1099	        p.configure(
  1100	            cfg
  1101	        );
  1102	
  1103	        d.activate();
  1104	        p.activate();
  1105	
  1106	        uint256[2] memory da;
  1107	        uint256[2][2] memory db;
  1108	        uint256[2] memory dc;
  1109	        uint256[73] memory directPublic;
  1110	
  1111	        (
  1112	            da,
  1113	            db,
  1114	            dc,
  1115	            directPublic
  1116	        ) =
  1117	            WP4Fixture.directA();
  1118	
  1119	        uint256[2] memory pa;
  1120	        uint256[2][2] memory pb;
  1121	        uint256[2] memory pc;
  1122	        uint256[10] memory poseidonPublic;
  1123	
  1124	        (
  1125	            pa,
  1126	            pb,
  1127	            pc,
  1128	            poseidonPublic
  1129	        ) =
  1130	            WP4Fixture.digestA();
  1131	
  1132	        (
  1133	            uint256 nullifier,
  1134	            uint256[16] memory rx,
  1135	            uint256[16] memory ry,
  1136	            uint256[16] memory sx,
  1137	            uint256[16] memory sy
  1138	        ) =
  1139	            WP4Fixture.ballotA();
  1140	
  1141	        /*
  1142	         * M12 engineering proxy: verifier execution.
  1143	         */
  1144	        g0 =
  1145	            gasleft();
  1146	
  1147	        bool directVerified =
  1148	            dv.verifyProof(
  1149	                da,
  1150	                db,
  1151	                dc,
  1152	                directPublic
  1153	            );
  1154	
  1155	        uint256 directVerifierGas =
  1156	            g0 -
  1157	            gasleft();
  1158	
  1159	        require(
  1160	            directVerified,
  1161	            "DIRECT_GAS_PROOF"
  1162	        );
  1163	
  1164	        g0 =
  1165	            gasleft();
  1166	
  1167	        bool poseidonVerified =
  1168	            pv.verifyProof(
  1169	                pa,
  1170	                pb,
  1171	                pc,
  1172	                poseidonPublic
  1173	            );
  1174	
  1175	        uint256 poseidonVerifierGas =
  1176	            g0 -
  1177	            gasleft();
  1178	
  1179	        require(
  1180	            poseidonVerified,
  1181	            "POSEIDON_GAS_PROOF"
  1182	        );
  1183	
  1184	        /*
  1185	         * M13 engineering proxy:
  1186	         * Poseidon ciphertext-binding recomputation.
  1187	         *
  1188	         * DIRECT has no separate hash stage; its binding
  1189	         * cost is represented by the larger verifier input.
  1190	         */
  1191	        g0 =
  1192	            gasleft();
  1193	
  1194	        uint256 digest =
  1195	            p.previewBindingDigest(
  1196	                rx,
  1197	                ry,
  1198	                sx,
  1199	                sy
  1200	            );
  1201	
  1202	        uint256 poseidonBindingGas =
  1203	            g0 -
  1204	            gasleft();
  1205	
  1206	        require(
  1207	            digest ==
  1208	                WP4Fixture.bindingA(),
  1209	            "BINDING_GAS_DIGEST"
  1210	        );
  1211	
  1212	        /*
  1213	         * M14 execution proxy: complete accepted vote call.
  1214	         */
  1215	        g0 =
  1216	            gasleft();
  1217	
  1218	        d.castVote(
  1219	            da,
  1220	            db,
  1221	            dc,
  1222	            nullifier,
  1223	            rx,
  1224	            ry,
  1225	            sx,
  1226	            sy
  1227	        );
  1228	
  1229	        uint256 directCastGas =
  1230	            g0 -
  1231	            gasleft();
  1232	
  1233	        g0 =
  1234	            gasleft();
  1235	
  1236	        p.castVote(
  1237	            pa,
  1238	            pb,
  1239	            pc,
  1240	            nullifier,
  1241	            rx,
  1242	            ry,
  1243	            sx,
  1244	            sy
  1245	        );
  1246	
  1247	        uint256 poseidonCastGas =
  1248	            g0 -
  1249	            gasleft();
  1250	
  1251	        /*
  1252	         * M15 deterministic ABI calldata sizes.
  1253	         */
  1254	        bytes memory directCalldata =
  1255	            abi.encodeCall(
  1256	                VoteChainDirect.castVote,
  1257	                (
  1258	                    da,
  1259	                    db,
  1260	                    dc,
  1261	                    nullifier,
  1262	                    rx,

FILE: src/LocalVoting.sol

     1	// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
     2	pragma solidity 0.8.17;
     3	
     4	contract LocalVoting {
     5	    address public electionCommission;
     6	
     7	    enum ElectionState {
     8	        Registration,
     9	        Active,
    10	        Ended
    11	    }
    12	
    13	    ElectionState public state;
    14	
    15	    struct CandidateState {
    16	        bool exists;
    17	        uint256 voteCount;
    18	    }
    19	
    20	    mapping(uint256 => CandidateState) public candidates;
    21	    uint256 public candidateCount;
    22	
    23	    mapping(bytes32 => bool) public voterEligible;
    24	    mapping(bytes32 => bool) public hasVoted;
    25	    uint256 public registeredVoterCount;
    26	
    27	    constructor() {
    28	        electionCommission = msg.sender;
    29	        state = ElectionState.Registration;
    30	    }
    31	
    32	    modifier onlyCommission() {
    33	        require(msg.sender == electionCommission, "Only Election Commission can call this function");
    34	        _;
    35	    }
    36	
    37	    modifier onlyRegistration() {
    38	        require(state == ElectionState.Registration, "Election is not in Registration state");
    39	        _;
    40	    }
    41	
    42	    modifier onlyActive() {
    43	        require(state == ElectionState.Active, "Election is not Active");
    44	        _;
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    function registerCandidate(uint256 candidateId) public onlyCommission onlyRegistration {
    48	        require(!candidates[candidateId].exists, "Candidate ID already registered");
    49	
    50	        candidates[candidateId] = CandidateState({exists: true, voteCount: 0});
    51	        candidateCount++;
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    function registerVoter(bytes32 voterHash) public onlyCommission onlyRegistration {
    55	        require(voterHash != bytes32(0), "Invalid voter hash");
    56	        require(!voterEligible[voterHash], "Voter hash already registered");
    57	
    58	        voterEligible[voterHash] = true;
    59	        registeredVoterCount++;
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    function startElection() public onlyCommission onlyRegistration {
    63	        state = ElectionState.Active;
    64	    }
    65	
    66	    function castVote(uint256 candidateId, bytes32 voterHash) public onlyActive {
    67	        require(candidates[candidateId].exists, "Invalid candidate");
    68	        require(voterEligible[voterHash], "Unrecognised voter hash");
    69	        require(!hasVoted[voterHash], "You have already voted");
    70	
    71	        hasVoted[voterHash] = true;
    72	        candidates[candidateId].voteCount++;
    73	    }
    74	
    75	    function endElection() public onlyCommission onlyActive {
    76	        state = ElectionState.Ended;
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    function getVoteCount(uint256 candidateId) public view returns (uint256) {
    80	        require(candidates[candidateId].exists, "Candidate not found");
    81	        return candidates[candidateId].voteCount;
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    function getElectionState() public view returns (ElectionState) {
    85	        return state;
    86	    }
    87	}

FILE: src/ZKLocalVoting.sol
======================================================================
     1	// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
     2	pragma solidity ^0.8.23;
     3	
     4	import {ISemaphore} from "@semaphore-protocol/contracts/interfaces/ISemaphore.sol";
     5	import {ISemaphoreGroups} from "@semaphore-protocol/contracts/interfaces/ISemaphoreGroups.sol";
     6	
     7	/// @title VoteChain ZK voting contract backed by Semaphore V4.
     8	/// @notice The transaction sender is infrastructure only; voter uniqueness is enforced by Semaphore nullifiers.
     9	contract ZKLocalVoting {
    10	    address public immutable electionCommission;
    11	    uint256 public immutable electionId;
    12	    uint256 public immutable groupId;
    13	    ISemaphore public immutable semaphore;
    14	
    15	    enum ElectionState {
    16	        Registration,
    17	        Active,
    18	        Ended
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    ElectionState public state;
    22	
    23	    struct CandidateState {
    24	        bool exists;
    25	        uint256 voteCount;
    26	    }
    27	
    28	    mapping(uint256 => CandidateState) public candidates;
    29	    mapping(uint256 => bool) public registeredCommitments;
    30	    uint256 public candidateCount;
    31	    uint256 public registeredVoterCount;
    32	
    33	    event CandidateRegistered(uint256 indexed candidateId);
    34	    event VoterCommitmentRegistered(uint256 indexed identityCommitment);
    35	    event ElectionStarted(uint256 indexed electionId, uint256 indexed groupId);
    36	    event ElectionEnded(uint256 indexed electionId);
    37	    event VoteCast(uint256 indexed candidateId, uint256 indexed nullifier);
    38	
    39	    modifier onlyCommission() {
    40	        require(msg.sender == electionCommission, "Only Election Commission can call this function");
    41	        _;
    42	    }
    43	
    44	    modifier onlyRegistration() {
    45	        require(state == ElectionState.Registration, "Election is not in Registration state");
    46	        _;
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    modifier onlyActive() {
    50	        require(state == ElectionState.Active, "Election is not Active");
    51	        _;
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    constructor(ISemaphore semaphore_, uint256 electionId_, uint256 merkleTreeDuration_) {
    55	        require(address(semaphore_) != address(0), "Invalid Semaphore address");
    56	        require(electionId_ != 0, "Invalid election ID");
    57	        require(merkleTreeDuration_ != 0, "Invalid Merkle root duration");
    58	
    59	        electionCommission = msg.sender;
    60	        electionId = electionId_;
    61	        semaphore = semaphore_;
    62	        state = ElectionState.Registration;
    63	
    64	        // The VoteChain contract is the Semaphore group admin, so only the
    65	        // Election Commission path below can mutate membership before activation.
    66	        groupId = semaphore_.createGroup(address(this), merkleTreeDuration_);
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    function registerCandidate(uint256 candidateId) external onlyCommission onlyRegistration {
    70	        require(candidateId != 0, "Invalid candidate ID");
    71	        require(!candidates[candidateId].exists, "Candidate ID already registered");
    72	
    73	        candidates[candidateId] = CandidateState({exists: true, voteCount: 0});
    74	        unchecked {
    75	            candidateCount++;
    76	        }
    77	
    78	        emit CandidateRegistered(candidateId);
    79	    }
    80	
    81	    function registerVoterCommitment(uint256 identityCommitment) external onlyCommission onlyRegistration {
    82	        require(identityCommitment != 0, "Invalid identity commitment");
    83	        require(!registeredCommitments[identityCommitment], "Identity commitment already registered");
    84	
    85	        // Mark first; any downstream revert rolls this state change back atomically.
    86	        registeredCommitments[identityCommitment] = true;
    87	        semaphore.addMember(groupId, identityCommitment);
    88	
    89	        unchecked {
    90	            registeredVoterCount++;
    91	        }
    92	
    93	        emit VoterCommitmentRegistered(identityCommitment);
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    function startElection() external onlyCommission onlyRegistration {
    97	        state = ElectionState.Active;
    98	        emit ElectionStarted(electionId, groupId);
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    function castVote(uint256 candidateId, ISemaphore.SemaphoreProof calldata proof) external onlyActive {
   102	        require(candidates[candidateId].exists, "Invalid candidate");
   103	        require(proof.message == candidateId, "Proof message does not match candidate");
   104	        require(proof.scope == electionId, "Proof scope does not match election");
   105	
   106	        // Semaphore V4 validates membership and records the group-scoped nullifier.
   107	        // A reused nullifier reverts inside Semaphore before the tally is updated.
   108	        semaphore.validateProof(groupId, proof);
   109	
   110	        candidates[candidateId].voteCount++;
   111	        emit VoteCast(candidateId, proof.nullifier);
   112	    }
   113	
   114	    function endElection() external onlyCommission onlyActive {
   115	        state = ElectionState.Ended;
   116	        emit ElectionEnded(electionId);
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    function getVoteCount(uint256 candidateId) external view returns (uint256) {
   120	        require(candidates[candidateId].exists, "Candidate not found");
   121	        return candidates[candidateId].voteCount;
   122	    }
   123	
   124	    function getElectionState() external view returns (ElectionState) {
   125	        return state;
   126	    }
   127	
   128	    function getGroupRoot() external view returns (uint256) {
   129	        return ISemaphoreGroups(address(semaphore)).getMerkleTreeRoot(groupId);
   130	    }
   131	
   132	    function getGroupSize() external view returns (uint256) {
   133	        return ISemaphoreGroups(address(semaphore)).getMerkleTreeSize(groupId);
   134	    }
   135	
   136	    function getCommitmentIndex(uint256 identityCommitment) external view returns (uint256) {
   137	        require(registeredCommitments[identityCommitment], "Identity commitment not registered");
   138	        return ISemaphoreGroups(address(semaphore)).indexOf(groupId, identityCommitment);
   139	    }
   140	}

============================== END FILE ==============================

     1	import mongoose from 'mongoose'

     3	const voterSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
     4	  nationalId: {
     5	    type: String,
     6	    required: [true, 'National ID is required'],
     7	    unique: true,
     8	    trim: true,
     9	    match: [/^\d{10}$/, 'National ID must be exactly 10 digits'],
    10	  },
    11	  name: {
    12	    type: String,
    13	    required: [true, 'Name is required'],
    14	    trim: true,
    15	    minlength: [3, 'Name must be at least 3 characters long'],
    16	    maxlength: [50, 'Name cannot exceed 50 characters'],
    17	    match: [/^[A-Za-z ]+$/, 'Name should contain only letters and spaces'],
    18	  },
    19	  dateOfBirth: {
    20	    type: Date,
    21	    required: [true, 'Date of birth is required'],
    22	    validate: {
    23	      validator(value) {
    24	        if (!value) return false
    25	        const today = new Date()
    26	        let age = today.getFullYear() - value.getFullYear()
    27	        const monthDifference = today.getMonth() - value.getMonth()
    28	        if (monthDifference < 0 || (monthDifference === 0 && today.getDate() < value.getDate())) age -= 1
    29	        return age >= 18 && age <= 120
    30	      },
    31	      message: 'Voter must be between 18 and 120 years old',
    32	    },
    33	  },
    34	  location: {
    35	    type: String,
    36	    required: [true, 'Location is required'],
    37	    trim: true,
    38	    minlength: [2, 'Location must be at least 2 characters long'],
    39	    maxlength: [100, 'Location cannot exceed 100 characters'],
    40	    match: [/^[A-Za-z ,.-]+$/, 'Location can only contain letters, spaces, commas, hyphens, and periods'],
    41	  },
    42	
    43	  // Active VoteChain-ZK identity material. The private Semaphore identity is never
    44	  // stored in plaintext; only its public commitment and authenticated ciphertext are persisted.
    45	  zkIdentityCommitment: {
    46	    type: String,
    47	    trim: true,
    48	    match: [/^\d+$/, 'ZK identity commitment must be a decimal integer string'],
    49	  },
    50	  zkCredential: {
    51	    version: { type: Number },
    52	    iv: { type: String },
    53	    ciphertext: { type: String },
    54	    authTag: { type: String },
    55	  },
    56	  zkRegistrationStatus: {
    57	    type: String,
    58	    enum: ['pending', 'registered', 'failed', 'legacy'],
    59	    default: 'legacy',
    60	  },
    61	  zkGroupIndex: {
    62	    type: Number,
    63	    min: 0,
    64	    validate: {
    65	      validator(value) {
    66	        return value === undefined || value === null || Number.isSafeInteger(value)
    67	      },
    68	      message: 'ZK group index must be a safe integer',
    69	    },
    70	  },
    71	  zkRegistrationTxHash: {
    72	    type: String,
    73	    trim: true,
    74	  },
    75	
    76	  // WP5 custom VoteChain-ZK credential.
    77	  //
    78	  // Kept parallel to the inherited Semaphore fields during migration.
    79	  // The final active path will use this Poseidon commitment and encrypted
    80	  // custom secret after the WP5 local-privacy-worker gate passes.
    81	  wp5IdentityCommitment: {

    83	    trim: true,
    84	    match: [/^\d+$/, 'WP5 identity commitment must be a decimal integer string'],

    92	  wp5RegistrationStatus: {
    93	    type: String,
    94	    enum: ['registered', 'failed'],
    95	  },
    96	  wp5GroupIndex: {
    97	    type: Number,
    98	    min: 0,
    99	  },
   100	
   101	
   102	  // Historical VC-W01/VC-W02 fields. They remain readable for archived baseline
   103	  // records only; new ZK enrollment does not populate or use them.
   104	  voterHash: {
   105	    type: String,
   106	    trim: true,
   107	  },
   108	  encryptedSalt: {
   109	    iv: { type: String },
   110	    encryptedData: { type: String },
   111	    authTag: { type: String },
   112	  },
   113	}, {
   114	  timestamps: true,
   115	  collection: 'voterCollection',
   116	})
   117

   123	    unique: true,
   124	    partialFilterExpression: { voterHash: { $type: 'string' } },

   172	voterSchema.pre('save', function trimStringFields(next) {
   173	  Object.keys(this.schema.paths).forEach((key) => {
   174	    if (this.schema.paths[key].instance === 'String' && this[key]) this[key] = this[key].trim()
   175	  })
   176	  next()
   177	})
   178	
   179	voterSchema.post('save', function duplicateKeyError(error, _doc, next) {
   180	  if (
   181	    (error.name === 'MongoError' ||
   182	      error.name === 'MongoServerError') &&
   183	    error.code === 11000
   184	  ) {
   185	    const duplicate =
   186	      new Error(
   187	        'A voter with duplicate identity or registration-index data already exists',
   188	      )
   189	
   190	    duplicate.name =
   191	      error.name
   192	    duplicate.code =
   193	      error.code
   194	    duplicate.keyPattern =
   195	      error.keyPattern
   196	    duplicate.keyValue =
   197	      error.keyValue
   198	
   199	    next(duplicate)
   200	  } else {
   201	    next(error)
   202	  }
   203	})
   204	
   205	const Voter = mongoose.model('Voter', voterSchema)
   206	export default Voter

FILE: votechain-central-server/controllers/voterController.js

     1	import Voter from '../models/Voter.js'
     2	import { validationResult } from 'express-validator'
     3	import path from 'path'
     4	import { fileURLToPath } from 'url'
     5	import fs from 'fs'
     6	import { spawn } from 'child_process'
     7	import { toSafeVoterResponse, toZkVoterEnrollmentResponse } from '../services/privacyProjection.js'
     8	import { createEncryptedSemaphoreIdentity } from '../services/zk/identityService.js'
     9	import { getContract } from '../utils/contract.js'
    10	import { createWp5Credential, allocateWp5GroupIndex } from '../services/zk/wp5PrivacyService.js'
    11	import { saveVoterWithWp5IndexRetry } from '../services/zk/wp5RegistrationCore.js'
    12	
    13	const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)
    14	const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename)
    15	
    16	const handleTrainFace = async (req, res) => {
    17	  try {
    18	    const { nid } = req.params
    19	    console.log('[Train Face] Starting face training for submitted voter record')
    20	
    21	    const faceRecognitionPath = path.join(__dirname, '../../votechain-face-recognition')
    22	    const datasetPath = path.join(faceRecognitionPath, 'dataset', nid)
    23	
    24	    if (!fs.existsSync(datasetPath)) {
    25	      return res.status(404).json({
    26	        success: false,
    27	        message: 'Face images not found. Please capture face images first.',
    28	      })
    29	    }
    30	
    31	    const imageFiles = fs.readdirSync(datasetPath).filter((file) => file.endsWith('.jpg'))
    32	    if (imageFiles.length === 0) {
    33	      return res.status(404).json({
    34	        success: false,
    35	        message: 'No face images found. Please capture face images first.',
    36	      })
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    console.log(`[Train Face] Found ${imageFiles.length} face images for training`)
    40	
    41	    const trainScript = path.join(faceRecognitionPath, 'train_faces.py')
    42	    const trainProcess = spawn('python', [trainScript], {
    43	      cwd: faceRecognitionPath,
    44	      stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
    45	    })
    46	
    47	    trainProcess.stdout.on('data', () => {})
    48	    trainProcess.stderr.on('data', () => {})
    49	
    50	    trainProcess.on('close', (code) => {
    51	      console.log(`[Train Face] Training process finished with code: ${code}`)
    52	
    53	      if (code !== 0) {
    54	        return res.status(500).json({
    55	          success: false,
    56	          message: 'Face training failed',
    57	          code,
    58	        })
    59	      }
    60	
    61	      return res.json({
    62	        success: true,
    63	        message: 'Face training completed successfully',
    64	      })
    65	    })
    66	
    67	    trainProcess.on('error', () => {
    68	      console.error('[Train Face] Failed to start training process')
    69	      return res.status(500).json({
    70	        success: false,
    71	        message: 'Failed to start training process',
    72	      })
    73	    })
    74	  } catch (_error) {
    75	    console.error('[Train Face] Unexpected training endpoint error')
    76	    return res.status(500).json({
    77	      success: false,
    78	      message: 'Failed to train face model',
    79	    })
    80	  }
    81	}
    82	
    83	const createVoter = async (req, res) => {
    84	  try {
    85	    const errors = validationResult(req)
    86	    if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
    87	      return res.status(400).json({
    88	        success: false,
    89	        message: 'Validation failed',
    90	        errors: errors.array().map((err) => err.msg),
    91	      })
    92	    }
    93	
    94	    const { nationalId, name, dateOfBirth, location } = req.body
    95	
    96	    const existingVoter = await Voter.findOne({ nationalId })
    97	    if (existingVoter) {
    98	      return res.status(400).json({
    99	        success: false,
   100	        message: 'A voter with this National ID already exists',
   101	      })
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    const { identityCommitment, encryptedCredential } = await createEncryptedSemaphoreIdentity()
   105	    const wp5 = await createWp5Credential()
   106	
   107	    const voter =
   108	      await saveVoterWithWp5IndexRetry({
   109	        allocateIndex:
   110	          allocateWp5GroupIndex,
   111	
   112	        saveAtIndex:
   113	          async (
   114	            wp5GroupIndex,
   115	          ) => {
   116	            const candidate =
   117	              new Voter({
   118	                nationalId,
   119	                name,
   120	                dateOfBirth,
   121	                location,
   122	                zkIdentityCommitment:
   123	                  identityCommitment,
   124	                zkCredential:
   125	                  encryptedCredential,
   126	                zkRegistrationStatus:
   127	                  'pending',
   128	                wp5IdentityCommitment:
   129	                  wp5.identityCommitment,
   130	                wp5Credential:
   131	                  wp5.encryptedCredential,
   132	                wp5RegistrationStatus:
   133	                  'registered',
   134	                wp5GroupIndex,
   135	              })
   136	
   137	            await candidate.save()
   138	
   139	            return candidate
   140	          },
   141	      })
   142	
   143	    // The browser receives only the public commitment needed by the Election
   144	    // Commission wallet. The private Semaphore identity remains encrypted server-side.
   145	    const enrollment = toZkVoterEnrollmentResponse(voter)
   146	    return res.status(201).json({
   147	      success: true,
   148	      message: 'Voter ZK identity created; on-chain commitment registration is pending',
   149	      data: enrollment,
   150	    })
   151	  } catch (error) {
   152	    console.error('Voter ZK enrollment failed:', error.message)
   153	
   154	    if (error.name === 'ValidationError') {
   155	      return res.status(400).json({
   156	        success: false,
   157	        message: 'Validation failed',
   158	        errors: Object.values(error.errors).map((err) => err.message),
   159	      })
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    if (error.code === 11000) {
   163	      return res.status(400).json({
   164	        success: false,
   165	        message: 'A voter with this National ID or ZK identity commitment already exists',
   166	      })
   167	    }
   168	
   169	    return res.status(500).json({
   170	      success: false,
   171	      message: 'Error creating voter ZK enrollment',
   172	    })
   173	  }
   174	}
   175	
   176	const updateZkRegistrationStatus = async (req, res) => {
   177	  try {
   178	    const { identityCommitment, registrationStatus, transactionHash } = req.body
   179	    if (!/^\d+$/.test(String(identityCommitment || ''))) {
   180	      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Valid identityCommitment is required' })
   181	    }
   182	    if (!['registered', 'failed'].includes(registrationStatus)) {
   183	      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'registrationStatus must be registered or failed' })
   184	    }
   185	
   186	    const voter = await Voter.findOne({ zkIdentityCommitment: String(identityCommitment) })
   187	    if (!voter) return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Voter enrollment not found' })
   188	
   189	    const contract = getContract()
   190	    const onChain = await contract.registeredCommitments(BigInt(identityCommitment))
   191	
   192	    if (registrationStatus === 'registered') {
   193	      if (!onChain) {
   194	        return res.status(409).json({ success: false, message: 'Commitment is not registered in the active ZK contract' })
   195	      }
   196	      const groupIndex = Number(await contract.getCommitmentIndex(BigInt(identityCommitment)))
   197	      voter.zkRegistrationStatus = 'registered'
   198	      voter.zkGroupIndex = groupIndex
   199	      if (transactionHash) voter.zkRegistrationTxHash = String(transactionHash)
   200	    } else {
   201	      // Never let an API caller downgrade a commitment that the blockchain already
   202	      // confirms as a member. Blockchain membership is the authority boundary.
   203	      if (onChain) {
   204	        return res.status(409).json({ success: false, message: 'Commitment is already registered on-chain and cannot be marked failed' })
   205	      }
   206	      voter.zkRegistrationStatus = 'failed'
   207	      voter.zkGroupIndex = undefined
   208	      if (transactionHash) voter.zkRegistrationTxHash = String(transactionHash)
   209	    }
   210	
   211	    await voter.save()
   212	    return res.json({
   213	      success: true,
   214	      data: {
   215	        identityCommitment: voter.zkIdentityCommitment,
   216	        registrationStatus: voter.zkRegistrationStatus,
   217	        groupIndex: voter.zkGroupIndex ?? null,
   218	      },
   219	    })
   220	  } catch (error) {
   221	    console.error('Voter ZK registration reconciliation failed:', error.message)
   222	    return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to reconcile voter commitment registration' })
   223	  }
   224	}
   225	
   226	const updateVoter = async (req, res) => {
   227	  try {
   228	    const voter = await Voter.findById(req.query.id)
   229	    if (!voter) {
   230	      return res.status(404).json({ message: 'Voter not found' })
   231	    }
   232	
   233	    const { nationalId, name, dateOfBirth, location } = req.body
   234	
   235	    voter.nationalId = nationalId || voter.nationalId
   236	    voter.name = name || voter.name
   237	    voter.dateOfBirth = dateOfBirth || voter.dateOfBirth
   238	    voter.location = location || voter.location
   239	
   240	    await voter.save()
   241	
   242	    return res.json({
   243	      message: 'Voter updated successfully',
   244	      voter: toSafeVoterResponse(voter),
   245	    })
   246	  } catch (error) {
   247	    console.error('Voter update failed:', error.message)
   248	    return res.status(500).json({ message: 'Error updating voter' })
   249	  }
   250	}
   251	
   252	const deleteVoter = async (req, res) => {
   253	  try {
   254	    const voter = await Voter.findById(req.query.id)
   255	    if (!voter) {
   256	      return res.status(404).json({ message: 'Voter not found' })
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    if (
   260	      voter.zkRegistrationStatus === 'registered' ||
   261	      voter.wp5RegistrationStatus === 'registered'
   262	    ) {
   263	      return res.status(409).json({
   264	        message:
   265	          'A voter with an active election credential cannot be deleted from the election record',
   266	      })
   267	    }
   268	
   269	    await voter.deleteOne()
   270	    return res.json({ message: 'Voter deleted successfully' })
   271	  } catch (error) {
   272	    console.error('Voter deletion failed:', error.message)
   273	    return res.status(500).json({ message: 'Error deleting voter' })
   274	  }
   275	}
   276	
   277	export default {
   278	  createVoter,
   279	  updateZkRegistrationStatus,
   280	  updateVoter,
   281	  deleteVoter,
   282	  handleTrainFace,
   283	}

    24	function lifecycleStatus(
    25	  error,
    26	) {
    27	  if (
    28	    error.code ===
    29	      "ELECTION_NOT_FOUND"
    30	  ) {
    31	    return 404
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  if (
    35	    [
    36	      "INVALID_DKG_TRANSCRIPT",
    37	      "DKG_CEREMONY_MISMATCH",
    38	    ].includes(
    39	      error.code,
    40	    )
    41	  ) {
    42	    return 400
    43	  }
    44	
    45	  if (
    46	    [
    47	      "ELECTION_IN_PROGRESS",
    48	      "ELECTION_NOT_IN_REGISTRATION",
    49	      "ELECTION_ADMIN_MISMATCH",
    50	      "ELECTION_CANNOT_START",
    51	      "TRUSTEE_TRANSCRIPT_REQUIRED",
    52	      "START_IN_PROGRESS",
    53	      "CURRENT_ELECTION_MISMATCH",
    54	      "SNAPSHOT_NOT_READY",
    55	      "CHAIN_ID_MISMATCH",
    56	      "DEPLOYER_ADMIN_MISMATCH",
    57	      "DIRECT_ADMIN_MISMATCH",
    58	      "DIRECT_VERIFIER_MISMATCH",
    59	      "CONTRACT_CONFIG_MISMATCH",
    60	      "TRUSTEE_TRANSCRIPT_MISMATCH",
    61	      "DIRECT_PHASE_INVALID",
    62	      "CHAIN_STATE_LOST",
    63	    ].includes(
    64	      error.code,
    65	    )
    66	  ) {
    67	    return 409
    68	  }
    69	
    70	  return 500
    71	}

   103	      )

   106	        success: false,
   107	        message:
   108	          "Failed to fetch election data",
   109	      })
   110	    }
   111	  }

   347	          status === 500

   349	            : error.message,
