     1	import express from 'express'
     2	import { body, param } from 'express-validator'
     3	import validate from '../middleware/validate.js'
     4	import { requireElectionRegistrationOpen } from '../middleware/electionRegistration.js'
     5	import {
     6	  requireWp5AdminMutation,
     7	} from '../middleware/adminRequestSecurity.js'
     8	import Candidate from '../models/Candidate.js'
     9	import path from 'path'
    10	import { fileURLToPath } from 'url'
    11	import fs from 'fs'
    12	import {
    13	  createCandidate,
    14	  setCandidateRegistrationStatus,
    15	} from '../services/candidateService.js'
    16	import { toCandidateWorkflowResponse } from '../services/privacyProjection.js'
    17	
    18	const router = express.Router()
    19	
    20	router.use(
    21	  requireWp5AdminMutation,
    22	)
    23	const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)
    24	const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename)
    25	
    26	const candidateMetadataValidation = [
    27	  body('nationalId').isString().matches(/^\d{10}$/).withMessage('National ID must be exactly 10 digits'),
    28	  body('name').isString().trim().isLength({ min: 3, max: 50 }).matches(/^[A-Za-z ]+$/),
    29	  body('party').isString().trim().isLength({ min: 2, max: 40 }).matches(/^[A-Za-z ]+$/),
    30	  body('location').isString().trim().isLength({ min: 2, max: 100 }).matches(/^[A-Za-z ,.-]+$/),
    31	  body('age').isInt({ min: 18, max: 120 }),
    32	]
    33	
    34	router.post('/register', requireElectionRegistrationOpen, candidateMetadataValidation, validate, async (req, res) => {
    35	  try {
    36	    const candidate = await createCandidate(req.body)
    37	
    38	    res.status(201).json({
    39	      success: true,
    40	      message: 'Candidate metadata created; blockchain registration is pending',
    41	      data: toCandidateWorkflowResponse(candidate),
    42	    })
    43	  } catch (error) {
    44	    if (error.code === 'DUPLICATE_NID' || error.code === 11000) {
    45	      return res.status(409).json({ success: false, message: 'A candidate with this National ID already exists' })
    46	    }
    47	
    48	    if (error.name === 'ValidationError') {
    49	      return res.status(400).json({
    50	        success: false,
    51	        message: 'Validation failed',
    52	        errors: Object.values(error.errors).map((validationError) => validationError.message),
    53	      })
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    console.error('Candidate creation failed:', error.message)
    57	    return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to create candidate metadata' })
    58	  }
    59	})
    60	
    61	router.patch('/:candidateId/registration-status', requireElectionRegistrationOpen, [
    62	  param('candidateId').isInt({ min: 1 }).withMessage('candidateId must be a positive integer'),
    63	  body('registrationStatus').isIn(['pending', 'registered', 'failed']),
    64	], validate, async (req, res) => {
    65	  try {
    66	    const candidate = await setCandidateRegistrationStatus(
    67	      Number(req.params.candidateId),
    68	      req.body.registrationStatus,
    69	    )
    70	
    71	    return res.json({
    72	      success: true,
    73	      data: {
    74	        candidateId: candidate.candidateId,
    75	        registrationStatus: candidate.registrationStatus,
    76	      },
    77	    })
    78	  } catch (error) {
    79	    if (error.code === 'NOT_FOUND') {
    80	      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Candidate not found' })
    81	    }
    82	    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: error.message })
    83	  }
    84	})
    85	
    86	router.put('/:id', requireElectionRegistrationOpen, [
    87	  param('id').isMongoId().withMessage('Invalid candidate ID'),
    88	  body('name').optional().trim().notEmpty(),
    89	  body('party').optional().trim().notEmpty(),
    90	  body('nationalId').optional().matches(/^\d{10}$/),
    91	  body('age').optional().isInt({ min: 18, max: 120 }),
    92	  body('location').optional().trim().notEmpty(),
    93	], validate, async (req, res) => {
    94	  try {
    95	    const allowed = ['name', 'party', 'nationalId', 'age', 'location']
    96	    const update = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(req.body).filter(([key]) => allowed.includes(key)))
    97	    const candidate = await Candidate.findByIdAndUpdate(req.params.id, update, { new: true, runValidators: true })
    98	    if (!candidate) return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Candidate not found' })
    99	    return res.json({ success: true, data: toCandidateWorkflowResponse(candidate) })
   100	  } catch (error) {
   101	    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: error.message })
   102	  }
   103	})
   104	
   105	
   106	router.delete('/:id', requireElectionRegistrationOpen, [param('id').isMongoId().withMessage('Invalid candidate ID')], validate, async (req, res) => {
   107	  try {
   108	    const candidate = await Candidate.findByIdAndDelete(req.params.id)
   109	    if (!candidate) return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Candidate not found' })
   110	    return res.json({ success: true, message: 'Candidate deleted successfully' })
   111	  } catch (error) {
   112	    return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to delete candidate' })
   113	  }
   114	})
   115	
   116	router.post('/train-face/:nid', async (req, res) => {
   117	  try {
   118	    const { nid } = req.params
   119	    const faceRecognitionPath = path.join(__dirname, '../../votechain-face-recognition')
   120	    const datasetPath = path.join(faceRecognitionPath, 'dataset', nid)
   121	
   122	    if (!fs.existsSync(datasetPath)) {
   123	      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Face images not found. Please capture face images first.' })
   124	    }
   125	
   126	    const imageFiles = fs.readdirSync(datasetPath).filter((file) => file.endsWith('.jpg'))
   127	    if (imageFiles.length === 0) {
   128	      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'No face images found. Please capture face images first.' })
   129	    }
   130	
   131	    const { spawn } = await import('child_process')
   132	    const trainScript = path.join(faceRecognitionPath, 'train_faces.py')
   133	    const trainProcess = spawn('python', [trainScript], { cwd: faceRecognitionPath, stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'] })
   134	    trainProcess.stdout.on('data', () => {})
   135	    trainProcess.stderr.on('data', () => {})
   136	    trainProcess.on('close', (code) => {
   137	      if (code !== 0) {
   138	        return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Face training failed', code })
   139	      }
   140	      return res.json({ success: true, message: 'Face training completed successfully' })
   141	    })
   142	    trainProcess.on('error', () => res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to start training process' }))
   143	  } catch (_error) {
   144	    return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to train face model' })
   145	  }
   146	})
   147	
   148	export default router

     2	  existsSync,
     3	  mkdirSync,
     4	  readFileSync,
     5	  unlinkSync,
     6	  writeFileSync,
     7	} from 'node:fs'

    10	  dirname,
    11	  join,
    12	  resolve,
    13	} from 'node:path'
    14	
    15	import {
    16	  createHash,
    17	} from 'node:crypto'

    20	  getAddress,
    21	  isAddress,
    22	  verifyMessage,
    23	} from 'ethers'

    29	export const ADMIN_AUTH_SCHEME =
    30	  'VoteChainAdmin'
    31	
    32	export const ADMIN_AUTH_VERSION =
    33	  'VoteChain-ZK Admin Request v1'
    34	
    35	export const ADMIN_REQUEST_MAX_AGE_MS =
    36	  60_000
    37	
    38	export const ADMIN_REQUEST_MAX_FUTURE_MS =
    39	  10_000
    40	
    41	const NONCE_PATTERN =
    42	  /^[0-9a-f]{32}$/
    43	
    44	const SIGNATURE_PATTERN =
    45	  /^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{130}$/
    46	
    47	function normalizeJson(
    48	  value,
    49	) {
    50	  if (
    51	    Array.isArray(value)
    52	  ) {
    53	    return value.map(
    54	      normalizeJson,
    55	    )
    56	  }
    57	
    58	  if (
    59	    value &&
    60	    typeof value === 'object'
    61	  ) {
    62	    return Object.fromEntries(
    63	      Object
    64	        .keys(value)
    65	        .sort()
    66	        .map(
    67	          key => [
    68	            key,
    69	            normalizeJson(
    70	              value[key],
    71	            ),
    72	          ],
    73	        ),
    74	    )
    75	  }
    76	
    77	  return value
    78	}
    79	
    80	export function canonicalAdminJson(
    81	  value,
    82	) {
    83	  return JSON.stringify(
    84	    normalizeJson(
    85	      value === undefined
    86	        ? null
    87	        : value,
    88	    ),
    89	  )
    90	}
    91	
    92	export function hashAdminBody(
    93	  value,
    94	) {
    95	  return createHash(
    96	    'sha256',
    97	  )
    98	    .update(
    99	      canonicalAdminJson(
   100	        value,
   101	      ),
   102	      'utf8',
   103	    )
   104	    .digest(
   105	      'hex',
   106	    )
   107	}

   109	export function buildAdminRequestMessage({
   110	  audience,
   111	  address,
   112	  method,
   113	  path,
   114	  timestamp,
   115	  nonce,
   116	  bodyHash,
   117	}) {
   118	  return [
   119	    ADMIN_AUTH_VERSION,
   120	
   121	    `audience:${
   122	      String(
   123	        audience,
   124	      ).toLowerCase()
   125	    }`,
   126	
   127	    `address:${
   128	      String(
   129	        address,
   130	      ).toLowerCase()
   131	    }`,
   132	
   133	    `method:${
   134	      String(
   135	        method,
   136	      ).toUpperCase()
   137	    }`,
   138	
   139	    `path:${
   140	      String(path)
   141	    }`,
   142	
   143	    `timestamp:${
   144	      String(timestamp)
   145	    }`,
   146	
   147	    `nonce:${
   148	      String(
   149	        nonce,
   150	      ).toLowerCase()
   151	    }`,
   152	
   153	    `body-sha256:${
   154	      String(
   155	        bodyHash,
   156	      ).toLowerCase()
   157	    }`,
   158	  ].join('\n')
   159	}
   160	
   161	/*
   162	 * Persistent atomic replay store.
   163	 *
   164	 * A successful admin signature reserves one nonce by atomically creating
   165	 * a nonce file with flag "wx". This survives a backend restart and also
   166	 * prevents two server processes sharing the same directory from accepting
   167	 * the same nonce concurrently.
   168	 *
   169	 * Files contain only random nonce metadata and expiry time.
   170	 */
   171	export function createFileReplayStore(
   172	  directory =
   173	    process.env
   174	      .ADMIN_REPLAY_CACHE_DIR
   175	      ?.trim() ||
   176	    resolve(
   177	      process.cwd(),
   178	      '.runtime',
   179	      'admin-request-nonces',
   180	    ),
   181	) {
   182	  let cleaned = false
   183	
   184	  function ensureDirectory() {
   185	    mkdirSync(
   186	      directory,
   187	      {
   188	        recursive: true,
   189	      },
   190	    )
   191	  }
   192	
   193	  function cleanup(
   194	    now,
   195	  ) {
   196	    if (cleaned) {
   197	      return
   198	    }
   199	
   200	    cleaned = true
   201	    ensureDirectory()
   202	
   203	    /*
   204	     * Cleanup is deliberately opportunistic. We only need to remove a
   205	     * stale file when its random nonce happens to be reused.
   206	     * Random 128-bit nonces make that already negligible.
   207	     */
   208	    void now
   209	  }
   210	
   211	  function reserve(
   212	    nonce,
   213	    expiresAt,
   214	    now,
   215	  ) {
   216	    cleanup(now)
   217	
   218	    const file =
   219	      join(
   220	        directory,
   221	        `${nonce}.json`,
   222	      )
   223	
   224	    const payload =
   225	      JSON.stringify({
   226	        nonce,
   227	        expiresAt,
   228	      })
   229	
   230	    try {
   231	      writeFileSync(
   232	        file,
   233	        payload,
   234	        {
   235	          flag: 'wx',
   236	          mode: 0o600,
   237	        },
   238	      )
   239	
   240	      return true
   241	    } catch (error) {
   242	      if (
   243	        error?.code !==
   244	          'EEXIST'
   245	      ) {
   246	        throw error
   247	      }
   248	    }
   249	
   250	    /*
   251	     * An expired nonce artifact can be removed and retried. This is mostly
   252	     * maintenance hygiene; a legitimate 128-bit nonce collision is not
   253	     * expected in practice.
   254	     */
   255	    try {
   256	      const existing =
   257	        JSON.parse(
   258	          readFileSync(
   259	            file,
   260	            'utf8',
   261	          ),
   262	        )
   263	
   264	      if (
   265	        Number(
   266	          existing.expiresAt,
   267	        ) <
   268	        Number(now)
   269	      ) {
   270	        unlinkSync(
   271	          file,
   272	        )
   273	
   274	        writeFileSync(
   275	          file,
   276	          payload,
   277	          {
   278	            flag: 'wx',
   279	            mode: 0o600,
   280	          },
   281	        )
   282	
   283	        return true
   284	      }
   285	    } catch (error) {
   286	      if (
   287	        !existsSync(file)
   288	      ) {
   289	        throw error
   290	      }
   291	    }
   292	
   293	    return false
   294	  }
   295	
   296	  return {
   297	    directory,
   298	    reserve,
   299	  }
   300	}
   301	
   302	let cachedAdminContextPromise =
   303	  null
   304	
   305	async function resolveWp5AdminContext() {
   306	  if (
   307	    !cachedAdminContextPromise
   308	  ) {
   309	    cachedAdminContextPromise =
   310	      (
   311	        async () => {
   312	          const contract =
   313	            getContract()
   314	
   315	          const [
   316	            admin,
   317	            audience,
   318	          ] =
   319	            await Promise.all([
   320	              contract.electionCommission(),
   321	              contract.getAddress(),
   322	            ])
   323	
   324	          return {
   325	            admin:
   326	              getAddress(
   327	                admin,
   328	              ),
   329	
   330	            audience:
   331	              getAddress(
   332	                audience,
   333	              ),
   334	          }
   335	        }
   336	      )()
   337	  }
   338	
   339	  try {
   340	    return await cachedAdminContextPromise
   341	  } catch (error) {
   342	    cachedAdminContextPromise =
   343	      null
   344	
   345	    throw error
   346	  }
   347	}
   348	
   349	function parseAuthorization(
   350	  header,
   351	) {
   352	  if (
   353	    typeof header !==
   354	      'string'
   355	  ) {
   356	    return null
   357	  }
   358	
   359	  const prefix =
   360	    `${ADMIN_AUTH_SCHEME} `
   361	
   362	  if (
   363	    !header.startsWith(
   364	      prefix,
   365	    )
   366	  ) {
   367	    return null
   368	  }
   369	
   370	  const token =
   371	    header
   372	      .slice(
   373	        prefix.length,
   374	      )
   375	      .trim()
   376	
   377	  const parts =
   378	    token.split('.')
   379	
   380	  if (
   381	    parts.length !==
   382	      4
   383	  ) {
   384	    return null
   385	  }
   386	
   387	  const [
   388	    address,
   389	    timestamp,
   390	    nonce,
   391	    signature,
   392	  ] = parts
   393	
   394	  if (
   395	    !isAddress(address) ||
   396	    !/^\d{10,16}$/.test(
   397	      timestamp,
   398	    ) ||
   399	    !NONCE_PATTERN.test(
   400	      nonce,
   401	    ) ||
   402	    !SIGNATURE_PATTERN.test(
   403	      signature,
   404	    )
   405	  ) {
   406	    return null
   407	  }
   408	
   409	  return {
   410	    address:
   411	      getAddress(
   412	        address,
   413	      ),
   414	
   415	    timestamp:
   416	      Number(
   417	        timestamp,
   418	      ),
   419	
   420	    nonce:
   421	      nonce.toLowerCase(),
   422	
   423	    signature,
   424	  }
   425	}
   426	
   427	export function createAdminRequestVerifier({
   428	  resolveAdminContext =
   429	    resolveWp5AdminContext,
   430	
   431	  replayStore =
   432	    createFileReplayStore(),
   433	
   434	  now =
   435	    () =>
   436	      Date.now(),
   437	
   438	  maxAgeMs =
   439	    ADMIN_REQUEST_MAX_AGE_MS,
   440	
   441	  maxFutureMs =
   442	    ADMIN_REQUEST_MAX_FUTURE_MS,
   443	} = {}) {
   444	  return async function requireAdminRequest(
   445	    req,
   446	    res,
   447	    next,
   448	  ) {
   449	    const parsed =
   450	      parseAuthorization(
   451	        req.get(
   452	          'authorization',
   453	        ),
   454	      )
   455	
   456	    if (!parsed) {
   457	      return res.status(401).json({
   458	        success: false,
   459	        message:
   460	          'A valid administrator wallet signature is required',
   461	      })
   462	    }
   463	
   464	    const current =
   465	      Number(
   466	        now(),
   467	      )
   468	
   469	    if (
   470	      !Number.isSafeInteger(
   471	        parsed.timestamp,
   472	      ) ||
   473	      parsed.timestamp <
   474	        current -
   475	          maxAgeMs ||
   476	      parsed.timestamp >
   477	        current +
   478	          maxFutureMs
   479	    ) {
   480	      return res.status(401).json({
   481	        success: false,
   482	        message:
   483	          'Administrator request signature is expired or not yet valid',
   484	      })
   485	    }
   486	
   487	    let context
   488	
   489	    try {
   490	      context =
   491	        await resolveAdminContext()
   492	    } catch {
   493	      return res.status(503).json({
   494	        success: false,
   495	        message:
   496	          'Administrator authority is temporarily unavailable',
   497	      })
   498	    }
   499	
   500	    if (
   501	      !context ||
   502	      !isAddress(
   503	        context.admin,
   504	      ) ||
   505	      !isAddress(
   506	        context.audience,
   507	      )
   508	    ) {
   509	      return res.status(503).json({
   510	        success: false,
   511	        message:
   512	          'Administrator authority is not configured correctly',
   513	      })
   514	    }
   515	
   516	    const expectedAdmin =
   517	      getAddress(
   518	        context.admin,
   519	      )
   520	
   521	    const audience =
   522	      getAddress(
   523	        context.audience,
   524	      )
   525	
   526	    if (
   527	      parsed.address
   528	        .toLowerCase() !==
   529	      expectedAdmin
   530	        .toLowerCase()
   531	    ) {
   532	      return res.status(403).json({
   533	        success: false,
   534	        message:
   535	          'The signing wallet is not the election administrator',
   536	      })
   537	    }
   538	
   539	    const bodyHash =
   540	      hashAdminBody(
   541	        req.body,
   542	      )
   543	
   544	    const message =
   545	      buildAdminRequestMessage({
   546	        audience,
   547	        address:
   548	          parsed.address,
   549	        method:
   550	          req.method,
   551	        path:
   552	          req.originalUrl,
   553	        timestamp:
   554	          parsed.timestamp,
   555	        nonce:
   556	          parsed.nonce,
   557	        bodyHash,
   558	      })
   559	
   560	    let recovered
   561	
   562	    try {
   563	      recovered =
   564	        getAddress(
   565	          verifyMessage(
   566	            message,
   567	            parsed.signature,
   568	          ),
   569	        )
   570	    } catch {
   571	      return res.status(401).json({
   572	        success: false,
   573	        message:
   574	          'Administrator request signature is invalid',
   575	      })
   576	    }
   577	
   578	    if (
   579	      recovered
   580	        .toLowerCase() !==
   581	      expectedAdmin
   582	        .toLowerCase()
   583	    ) {
   584	      return res.status(401).json({
   585	        success: false,
   586	        message:
   587	          'Administrator request signature is invalid',
   588	      })
   589	    }
   590	
   591	    const expiresAt =
   592	      parsed.timestamp +
   593	      maxAgeMs +
   594	      maxFutureMs
   595	
   596	    let reserved
   597	
   598	    try {
   599	      reserved =
   600	        replayStore.reserve(
   601	          parsed.nonce,
   602	          expiresAt,
   603	          current,
   604	        )
   605	    } catch {
   606	      return res.status(503).json({
   607	        success: false,
   608	        message:
   609	          'Administrator replay protection is temporarily unavailable',
   610	      })
   611	    }
   612	
   613	    if (!reserved) {
   614	      return res.status(409).json({
   615	        success: false,
   616	        message:
   617	          'Administrator request replay was rejected',
   618	      })
   619	    }
   620	
   621	    req.voteChainAdmin = {
   622	      address:
   623	        expectedAdmin,
   624	
   625	      audience,
   626	
   627	      timestamp:
   628	        parsed.timestamp,
   629	
   630	      nonce:
   631	        parsed.nonce,
   632	    }
   633	
   634	    return next()
   635	  }
   636	}
   637	
   638	export const requireWp5AdminRequest =
   639	  createAdminRequestVerifier()
   640	
   641	export function requireWp5AdminMutation(
   642	  req,
   643	  res,
   644	  next,
   645	) {
   646	  if (
   647	    req.method === 'GET' ||
   648	    req.method === 'HEAD' ||
   649	    req.method === 'OPTIONS'
   650	  ) {
   651	    return next()
   652	  }
   653	
   654	  return requireWp5AdminRequest(
   655	    req,
   656	    res,
   657	    next,
   658	  )
   659	}

============================== END FILE ==============================

FILE: votechain-central-server/services/elections/electionLifecycleService.js
======================================================================
     1	import {
     2	  randomBytes,
     3	} from "node:crypto"
     4	
     5	import Election from "../../models/Election.js"
     6	import {
     7	  getProvider,
     8	} from "../../utils/contract.js"
     9	
    10	const CURRENT_STATUSES = [
    11	  "registration",
    12	  "starting",
    13	  "active",
    14	  "ended",
    15	]
    16	
    17	function electionInProgressError() {
    18	  const error =
    19	    new Error(
    20	      "An unfinished election already exists",
    21	    )
    22	
    23	  error.code =
    24	    "ELECTION_IN_PROGRESS"
    25	
    26	  return error
    27	}
    28	
    29	function generateElectionId() {
    30	  while (true) {
    31	    const value =
    32	      BigInt(
    33	        "0x" +
    34	        randomBytes(31)
    35	          .toString("hex"),
    36	      )
    37	
    38	    if (value !== 0n) {
    39	      return value.toString()
    40	    }
    41	  }
    42	}
    43	
    44	export async function getCurrentElection() {
    45	  return Election
    46	    .findOne({
    47	      status: {
    48	        $in: CURRENT_STATUSES,
    49	      },
    50	    })
    51	    .sort({
    52	      createdAt: -1,
    53	    })
    54	}
    55	
    56	export async function getRegistrationState() {
    57	  const election =
    58	    await getCurrentElection()
    59	
    60	  if (!election) {
    61	    return {
    62	      open: true,
    63	      election: null,
    64	    }
    65	  }
    66	
    67	  return {
    68	    open:
    69	      election.status ===
    70	      "registration",
    71	    election,
    72	  }
    73	}
    74	
    75	export async function createElectionRecord({
    76	  adminAddress,
    77	}) {
    78	  const current =
    79	    await getCurrentElection()
    80	
    81	  if (current) {
    82	    throw electionInProgressError()
    83	  }
    84	
    85	  const network =
    86	    await getProvider()
    87	      .getNetwork()
    88	
    89	  for (
    90	    let attempt = 0;
    91	    attempt < 3;
    92	    attempt += 1
    93	  ) {
    94	    try {
    95	      return await Election.create({
    96	        electionId:
    97	          generateElectionId(),
    98	
    99	        status:
   100	          "registration",
   101	
   102	        lifecycleSlot:
   103	          "current",
   104	
   105	        chainId:
   106	          network.chainId
   107	            .toString(),
   108	
   109	        adminAddress:
   110	          String(adminAddress),
   111	      })
   112	    } catch (error) {
   113	      if (
   114	        error?.code === 11000 &&
   115	        error?.keyPattern
   116	          ?.lifecycleSlot
   117	      ) {
   118	        throw electionInProgressError()
   119	      }
   120	
   121	      if (
   122	        error?.code === 11000 &&
   123	        error?.keyPattern
   124	          ?.electionId
   125	      ) {
   126	        continue
   127	      }
   128	
   129	      throw error
   130	    }
   131	  }
   132	
   133	  throw new Error(
   134	    "Could not allocate a unique election identifier",
   135	  )
   136	}

============================== END FILE ==============================

     1	import Election from "../../models/Election.js"
     2	import ElectionMembership from "../../models/ElectionMembership.js"
     3	import Voter from "../../models/Voter.js"
     4	import Candidate from "../../models/Candidate.js"

     6	import {
     7	  buildWp5MerkleSnapshot,
     8	  hashCandidateManifest,
     9	} from "../zk/wp5PrivacyService.js"
    10	
    11	function lifecycleError(
    12	  code,
    13	  message,
    14	) {
    15	  const error =
    16	    new Error(message)
    17	
    18	  error.code =
    19	    code
    20	
    21	  return error
    22	}
    23	
    24	async function acquireSnapshotElection() {
    25	  const now =
    26	    new Date()
    27	
    28	  let election =
    29	    await Election.findOneAndUpdate(
    30	      {
    31	        lifecycleSlot: "current",
    32	        status: "registration",
    33	      },
    34	      {
    35	        $set: {
    36	          status: "starting",
    37	          startStage: "snapshotting",
    38	          snapshotLockedAt: now,
    39	        },
    40	        $unset: {
    41	          failureReason: 1,
    42	        },
    43	      },
    44	      {
    45	        new: true,
    46	      },
    47	    )
    48	
    49	  if (election) {
    50	    return {
    51	      election,
    52	      resumed: false,
    53	    }
    54	  }
    55	
    56	  election =
    57	    await Election.findOne({
    58	      lifecycleSlot: "current",
    59	      status: "starting",
    60	    })
    61	
    62	  if (!election) {
    63	    throw lifecycleError(
    64	      "NO_REGISTRATION_ELECTION",
    65	      "No election is available to start",
    66	    )
    67	  }
    68	
    69	  if (
    70	    [
    71	      "snapshot_ready",
    72	      "deployed",
    73	      "configured",
    74	      "trustees_pinned",
    75	      "active",
    76	    ].includes(
    77	      election.startStage,
    78	    )
    79	  ) {
    80	    return {
    81	      election,
    82	      resumed: true,
    83	      alreadyReady: true,
    84	    }
    85	  }
    86	
    87	  if (
    88	    election.startStage ===
    89	      "snapshotting" &&
    90	    !election.failureReason
    91	  ) {
    92	    throw lifecycleError(
    93	      "START_IN_PROGRESS",
    94	      "Election start is already in progress",
    95	    )
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  election.startStage =
    99	    "snapshotting"
   100	
   101	  election.failureReason =
   102	    undefined
   103	
   104	  await election.save()
   105	
   106	  return {
   107	    election,
   108	    resumed: true,
   109	  }
   110	}
   111	
   112	export async function prepareElectionSnapshot() {
   113	  const acquired =
   114	    await acquireSnapshotElection()
   115	
   116	  const election =
   117	    acquired.election
   118	
   119	  if (acquired.alreadyReady) {
   120	    return {
   121	      election,
   122	      resumed: true,
   123	      alreadyReady: true,
   124	    }
   125	  }
   126	
   127	  try {
   128	    const [
   129	      voters,
   130	      candidates,
   131	    ] =
   132	      await Promise.all([
   133	        Voter
   134	          .find({
   135	            wp5RegistrationStatus:
   136	              "registered",
   137	            wp5IdentityCommitment: {
   138	              $type: "string",
   139	            },
   140	          })
   141	          .sort({
   142	            _id: 1,
   143	          })
   144	          .select({
   145	            _id: 1,
   146	            wp5IdentityCommitment: 1,
   147	          })
   148	          .lean(),
   149	
   150	        Candidate
   151	          .find({
   152	            registrationStatus:
   153	              "registered",
   154	            candidateId: {
   155	              $exists: true,
   156	            },
   157	          })
   158	          .sort({
   159	            candidateId: 1,
   160	          })
   161	          .select({
   162	            _id: 1,
   163	            candidateId: 1,
   164	            name: 1,
   165	            party: 1,
   166	            location: 1,
   167	          })
   168	          .lean(),
   169	      ])
   170	
   171	    if (voters.length < 1) {
   172	      throw lifecycleError(
   173	        "NO_ELIGIBLE_VOTERS",
   174	        "Cannot start an election without eligible voters",
   175	      )
   176	    }
   177	
   178	    if (candidates.length < 1) {
   179	      throw lifecycleError(
   180	        "NO_REGISTERED_CANDIDATES",
   181	        "Cannot start an election without registered candidates",
   182	      )
   183	    }
   184	
   185	    const memberships =
   186	      voters.map(
   187	        (
   188	          voter,
   189	          merkleIndex,
   190	        ) => ({
   191	          election:
   192	            election._id,
   193	
   194	          voter:
   195	            voter._id,
   196	
   197	          identityCommitment:
   198	            String(
   199	              voter.wp5IdentityCommitment,
   200	            ),
   201	
   202	          merkleIndex,
   203	        }),
   204	      )
   205	
   206	    await ElectionMembership
   207	      .deleteMany({
   208	        election:
   209	          election._id,
   210	      })
   211	
   212	    await ElectionMembership
   213	      .insertMany(
   214	        memberships,
   215	        {
   216	          ordered: true,
   217	        },
   218	      )
   219	
   220	    const merkleRecords =
   221	      memberships.map(
   222	        membership => ({
   223	          wp5GroupIndex:
   224	            membership.merkleIndex,
   225	
   226	          wp5IdentityCommitment:
   227	            membership.identityCommitment,
   228	        }),
   229	      )
   230	
   231	    const merkleSnapshot =
   232	      await buildWp5MerkleSnapshot(
   233	        merkleRecords,
   234	      )
   235	
   236	    const candidateIds =
   237	      candidates.map(
   238	        candidate =>
   239	          Number(
   240	            candidate.candidateId,
   241	          ),
   242	      )
   243	
   244	    const candidateManifest =
   245	      await hashCandidateManifest(
   246	        candidateIds,
   247	      )
   248	
   249	    election.eligibleRoot =
   250	      merkleSnapshot.root
   251	
   252	    election.eligibleVoterCount =
   253	      memberships.length
   254	
   255	    election.candidateManifestHash =
   256	      candidateManifest.toString()
   257	
   258	    election.candidateCount =
   259	      candidates.length
   260	
   261	    election.candidateSnapshot =
   262	      candidates.map(
   263	        (
   264	          candidate,
   265	          slot,
   266	        ) => ({
   267	          candidateRef:
   268	            candidate._id,
   269	
   270	          candidateId:
   271	            Number(
   272	              candidate.candidateId,
   273	            ),
   274	
   275	          slot,
   276	
   277	          name:
   278	            candidate.name,
   279	
   280	          party:
   281	            candidate.party,
   282	
   283	          location:
   284	            candidate.location,
   285	        }),
   286	      )
   287	
   288	    election.startStage =
   289	      "snapshot_ready"
   290	
   291	    election.failureReason =
   292	      undefined
   293	
   294	    await election.save()
   295	
   296	    return {
   297	      election,
   298	      resumed:
   299	        acquired.resumed,
   300	      alreadyReady: false,
   301	    }
   302	  } catch (error) {
   303	    election.failureReason =
   304	      error.message
   305	
   306	    await election.save()
   307	
   308	    throw error
   309	  }
   310	}

     1	import Election from "../../models/Election.js"
     2	import ElectionMembership from "../../models/ElectionMembership.js"
     3	
     4	export async function getActiveElectionMembership(
     5	  voter,
     6	) {
     7	  const election =
     8	    await Election.findOne({
     9	      lifecycleSlot: "current",
    10	      status: "active",
    11	    })
    12	
    13	  if (!election) {
    14	    throw new Error(
    15	      "No active election membership snapshot is available",
    16	    )
    17	  }
    18	
    19	  const membership =
    20	    await ElectionMembership.findOne({
    21	      election: election._id,
    22	      voter: voter._id,
    23	    })
    24	      .select({
    25	        identityCommitment: 1,
    26	        merkleIndex: 1,
    27	      })
    28	      .lean()
    29	
    30	  if (!membership) {
    31	    throw new Error(
    32	      "This voter is not eligible for the active election",
    33	    )
    34	  }
    35	
    36	  if (
    37	    String(
    38	      membership.identityCommitment,
    39	    ) !==
    40	    String(
    41	      voter.wp5IdentityCommitment,
    42	    )
    43	  ) {
    44	    throw new Error(
    45	      "Election membership commitment does not match voter credential",
    46	    )
    47	  }
    48	
    49	  const memberships =
    50	    await ElectionMembership
    51	      .find({
    52	        election: election._id,
    53	      })
    54	      .sort({
    55	        merkleIndex: 1,
    56	      })
    57	      .select({
    58	        identityCommitment: 1,
    59	        merkleIndex: 1,
    60	      })
    61	      .lean()
    62	
    63	  if (
    64	    election.eligibleVoterCount !==
    65	      undefined &&
    66	    election.eligibleVoterCount !==
    67	      null &&
    68	    memberships.length !==
    69	      Number(
    70	        election.eligibleVoterCount,
    71	      )
    72	  ) {
    73	    throw new Error(
    74	      "Election membership snapshot count does not match the frozen election record",
    75	    )
    76	  }
    77	
    78	  memberships.forEach(
    79	    (
    80	      record,
    81	      expectedIndex,
    82	    ) => {
    83	      if (
    84	        Number(
    85	          record.merkleIndex,
    86	        ) !==
    87	        expectedIndex
    88	      ) {
    89	        throw new Error(
    90	          `Election Merkle indices are invalid; expected ${expectedIndex}, got ${record.merkleIndex}`,
    91	        )
    92	      }
    93	    },
    94	  )
    95	
    96	  return {
    97	    election,
    98	    index:
    99	      Number(
   100	        membership.merkleIndex,
   101	      ),
   102	
   103	    records:
   104	      memberships.map(
   105	        record => ({
   106	          wp5GroupIndex:
   107	            Number(
   108	              record.merkleIndex,
   109	            ),
   110	
   111	          wp5IdentityCommitment:
   112	            String(
   113	              record.identityCommitment,
   114	            ),
   115	        }),
   116	      ),
   117	  }
   118	}

     1	import Election from "../../models/Election.js"
     2	
     3	export async function getActiveElectionCandidates() {
     4	  const election =
     5	    await Election.findOne({
     6	      lifecycleSlot: "current",
     7	      status: "active",
     8	    })
     9	      .select({
    10	        electionId: 1,
    11	        candidateSnapshot: 1,
    12	        candidateCount: 1,
    13	        candidateManifestHash: 1,
    14	      })
    15	      .lean()
    16	
    17	  if (!election) {
    18	    throw new Error(
    19	      "No active election candidate snapshot is available",
    20	    )
    21	  }
    22	
    23	  const snapshot =
    24	    [...(election.candidateSnapshot || [])]
    25	      .sort(
    26	        (a, b) =>
    27	          Number(a.slot) -
    28	          Number(b.slot),
    29	      )
    30	
    31	  snapshot.forEach(
    32	    (
    33	      candidate,
    34	      expectedSlot,
    35	    ) => {
    36	      if (
    37	        Number(candidate.slot) !==
    38	        expectedSlot
    39	      ) {
    40	        throw new Error(
    41	          `Election candidate slots are invalid; expected ${expectedSlot}, got ${candidate.slot}`,
    42	        )
    43	      }
    44	    },
    45	  )
    46	
    47	  if (
    48	    snapshot.length !==
    49	    Number(
    50	      election.candidateCount,
    51	    )
    52	  ) {
    53	    throw new Error(
    54	      "Election candidate snapshot count does not match the frozen election record",
    55	    )
    56	  }
    57	
    58	  return {
    59	    election,
    60	
    61	    candidates:
    62	      snapshot.map(
    63	        candidate => ({
    64	          candidateId:
    65	            Number(
    66	              candidate.candidateId,
    67	            ),
    68	
    69	          name:
    70	            candidate.name,
    71	
    72	          party:
    73	            candidate.party,
    74	
    75	          location:
    76	            candidate.location,
    77	
    78	          slot:
    79	            Number(
    80	              candidate.slot,
    81	            ),
    82	        }),
    83	      ),
    84	  }
    85	}

    56	function lifecycleError(
    57	  code,
    58	  message,
    59	) {

    61	    new Error(
    62	      message,
    63	    )

    71	function artifactBytecode(
    72	  artifact,
    73	  label,
    74	) {
    75	  const bytecode =
    76	    artifact
    77	      ?.bytecode
    78	      ?.object
    79	
    80	  if (
    81	    !bytecode ||
    82	    bytecode === "0x"
    83	  ) {
    84	    throw lifecycleError(
    85	      "DEPLOYMENT_ARTIFACT_MISSING",
    86	      `${label} deployment bytecode is unavailable`,
    87	    )
    88	  }
    89	
    90	  return bytecode
    91	}
    92	
    93	async function requireCode(
    94	  provider,
    95	  address,
    96	  label,
    97	) {
    98	  const code =
    99	    await provider
   100	      .getCode(
   101	        address,
   102	      )
   103	
   104	  if (
   105	    !code ||
   106	    code === "0x"
   107	  ) {
   108	    throw lifecycleError(
   109	      "CHAIN_STATE_LOST",
   110	      `${label} address exists in MongoDB but has no bytecode on the connected chain`,
   111	    )
   112	  }
   113	}
   114	
   115	function requireSnapshot(
   116	  election,
   117	) {
   118	  if (
   119	    !election.eligibleRoot ||
   120	    !election.candidateManifestHash ||
   121	    !Number.isSafeInteger(
   122	      Number(
   123	        election.candidateCount,
   124	      ),
   125	    ) ||
   126	    Number(
   127	      election.candidateCount,
   128	    ) < 1
   129	  ) {
   130	    throw lifecycleError(
   131	      "SNAPSHOT_NOT_READY",
   132	      "Election snapshot is not ready for contract configuration",
   133	    )
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  if (
   137	    !election.trusteeTranscript ||
   138	    !election.trusteeTranscriptHash ||
   139	    !election.Yx ||
   140	    !election.Yy
   141	  ) {
   142	    throw lifecycleError(
   143	      "TRUSTEE_TRANSCRIPT_REQUIRED",
   144	      "A verified WP6 trustee transcript is required before election activation",
   145	    )
   146	  }
   147	}
   148	
   149	function contractDomain() {
   150	  const raw =
   151	    wp3Profile
   152	      .testContractDomain ??
   153	    wp3Profile
   154	      .devContractDomain
   155	
   156	  if (
   157	    raw === undefined ||
   158	    raw === null
   159	  ) {
   160	    throw lifecycleError(
   161	      "WP5_PROFILE_INVALID",
   162	      "WP5 contract domain is missing from the build profile",
   163	    )
   164	  }
   165	
   166	  return BigInt(
   167	    raw,
   168	  )
   169	}
   170	
   171	function electionContextDomain() {
   172	  const raw =
   173	    appProfile
   174	      ?.domains
   175	      ?.electionContext ??
   176	    wp3Profile
   177	      ?.domains
   178	      ?.electionContext
   179	
   180	  if (
   181	    raw === undefined ||
   182	    raw === null
   183	  ) {
   184	    throw lifecycleError(
   185	      "WP5_PROFILE_INVALID",
   186	      "WP5 election-context domain is missing from the profile",
   187	    )
   188	  }
   189	
   190	  return BigInt(
   191	    raw,
   192	  )
   193	}
   194	
   195	async function ensureDeployments({
   196	  election,
   197	  provider,
   198	  signer,
   199	}) {
   200	  const directArtifact =
   201	    getWp5ContractArtifact()
   202	
   203	  if (
   204	    election.verifierAddress
   205	  ) {
   206	    await requireCode(
   207	      provider,
   208	      election.verifierAddress,
   209	      "WP5 verifier",
   210	    )
   211	  } else {
   212	    const verifierFactory =
   213	      new ContractFactory(
   214	        verifierArtifact.abi,
   215	        artifactBytecode(
   216	          verifierArtifact,
   217	          "WP5 verifier",
   218	        ),
   219	        signer,
   220	      )
   221	
   222	    const verifier =
   223	      await verifierFactory
   224	        .deploy()
   225	
   226	    await verifier
   227	      .waitForDeployment()
   228	
   229	    election.verifierAddress =
   230	      await verifier
   231	        .getAddress()
   232	
   233	    await election.save()
   234	  }
   235	
   236	  if (
   237	    election.directContractAddress
   238	  ) {
   239	    await requireCode(
   240	      provider,
   241	      election.directContractAddress,
   242	      "VoteChainDirect",
   243	    )
   244	  } else {
   245	    const directFactory =
   246	      new ContractFactory(
   247	        directArtifact.abi,
   248	        artifactBytecode(
   249	          directArtifact,
   250	          "VoteChainDirect",
   251	        ),
   252	        signer,
   253	      )
   254	
   255	    const direct =
   256	      await directFactory
   257	        .deploy(
   258	          election
   259	            .verifierAddress,
   260	        )
   261	
   262	    await direct
   263	      .waitForDeployment()
   264	
   265	    election.directContractAddress =
   266	      await direct
   267	        .getAddress()
   268	
   269	    election.startStage =
   270	      "deployed"
   271	
   272	    await election.save()
   273	  }
   274	
   275	  return new Contract(
   276	    election.directContractAddress,
   277	    directArtifact.abi,
   278	    signer,
   279	  )
   280	}
   281	
   282	async function verifyConfiguredState({
   283	  direct,
   284	  config,
   285	}) {
   286	  const [
   287	    eligibleRoot,
   288	    electionId,
   289	    manifest,
   290	    count,
   291	    domain,
   292	    Yx,
   293	    Yy,
   294	    contextHash,
   295	  ] =
   296	    await Promise.all([
   297	      direct.eligibleRoot(),
   298	      direct.electionId(),
   299	      direct.candidateManifestHash(),
   300	      direct.candidateCount(),
   301	      direct.contractDomain(),
   302	      direct.Yx(),
   303	      direct.Yy(),
   304	      direct.electionContextHash(),
   305	    ])
   306	
   307	  const actual = [
   308	    eligibleRoot,
   309	    electionId,
   310	    manifest,
   311	    count,
   312	    domain,
   313	    Yx,
   314	    Yy,
   315	    contextHash,
   316	  ].map(
   317	    value =>
   318	      BigInt(
   319	        value,
   320	      ),
   321	  )
   322	
   323	  const expected =
   324	    config.map(
   325	      value =>
   326	        BigInt(
   327	          value,
   328	        ),
   329	    )
   330	
   331	  for (
   332	    let index = 0;
   333	    index <
   334	      expected.length;
   335	    index += 1
   336	  ) {
   337	    if (
   338	      actual[index] !==
   339	      expected[index]
   340	    ) {
   341	      throw lifecycleError(
   342	        "CONTRACT_CONFIG_MISMATCH",
   343	        `VoteChainDirect configuration mismatch at tuple index ${index}`,
   344	      )
   345	    }
   346	  }
   347	}
   348	
   349	export async function activateElectionOnChain(
   350	  election,
   351	) {
   352	  requireSnapshot(
   353	    election,
   354	  )
   355	
   356	  const provider =
   357	    getWp5Provider()
   358	
   359	  try {
   360	    const network =
   361	      await provider
   362	        .getNetwork()
   363	
   364	    if (
   365	      election.chainId &&
   366	      String(
   367	        network.chainId,
   368	      ) !==
   369	        String(
   370	          election.chainId,
   371	        )
   372	    ) {
   373	      throw lifecycleError(
   374	        "CHAIN_ID_MISMATCH",
   375	        "Election chainId does not match the connected RPC network",
   376	      )
   377	    }
   378	
   379	    const signer =
   380	      await provider
   381	        .getSigner(
   382	          0,
   383	        )
   384	
   385	    const signerAddress =
   386	      getAddress(
   387	        await signer
   388	          .getAddress(),
   389	      )
   390	
   391	    const expectedAdmin =
   392	      getAddress(
   393	        election.adminAddress,
   394	      )
   395	
   396	    if (
   397	      signerAddress !==
   398	      expectedAdmin
   399	    ) {
   400	      throw lifecycleError(
   401	        "DEPLOYER_ADMIN_MISMATCH",
   402	        "RPC signer 0 is not the administrator that created this election",
   403	      )
   404	    }
   405	
   406	    const direct =
   407	      await ensureDeployments({
   408	        election,
   409	        provider,
   410	        signer,
   411	      })
   412	
   413	    const directAdmin =
   414	      getAddress(
   415	        await direct.admin(),
   416	      )
   417	
   418	    if (
   419	      directAdmin !==
   420	      expectedAdmin
   421	    ) {
   422	      throw lifecycleError(
   423	        "DIRECT_ADMIN_MISMATCH",
   424	        "VoteChainDirect admin does not match the election administrator",
   425	      )
   426	    }
   427	
   428	    const verifierAddress =
   429	      getAddress(
   430	        await direct.verifier(),
   431	      )
   432	
   433	    if (
   434	      verifierAddress !==
   435	      getAddress(
   436	        election
   437	          .verifierAddress,
   438	      )
   439	    ) {
   440	      throw lifecycleError(
   441	        "DIRECT_VERIFIER_MISMATCH",
   442	        "VoteChainDirect points to an unexpected verifier",
   443	      )
   444	    }
   445	
   446	    const domain =
   447	      contractDomain()
   448	
   449	    const D_CTX =
   450	      electionContextDomain()
   451	
   452	    const contextHash =
   453	      await wp5Hash([
   454	        D_CTX,
   455	        BigInt(
   456	          election.electionId,
   457	        ),
   458	        BigInt(
   459	          election
   460	            .candidateManifestHash,
   461	        ),
   462	        BigInt(
   463	          election.candidateCount,
   464	        ),
   465	        domain,
   466	        BigInt(
   467	          election.Yx,
   468	        ),
   469	        BigInt(
   470	          election.Yy,
   471	        ),
   472	      ])
   473	
   474	    const config = [
   475	      String(
   476	        election.eligibleRoot,
   477	      ),
   478	      String(
   479	        election.electionId,
   480	      ),
   481	      String(
   482	        election
   483	          .candidateManifestHash,
   484	      ),
   485	      String(
   486	        election.candidateCount,
   487	      ),
   488	      domain.toString(),
   489	      String(
   490	        election.Yx,
   491	      ),
   492	      String(
   493	        election.Yy,
   494	      ),
   495	      contextHash.toString(),
   496	    ]
   497	
   498	    election.contractDomain =
   499	      domain.toString()
   500	
   501	    election.electionContextHash =
   502	      contextHash.toString()
   503	
   504	    await election.save()
   505	
   506	    const configured =
   507	      Boolean(
   508	        await direct
   509	          .configured(),
   510	      )
   511	
   512	    if (!configured) {
   513	      const tx =
   514	        await direct
   515	          .configure(
   516	            config,
   517	          )
   518	
   519	      await tx.wait()
   520	    }
   521	
   522	    await verifyConfiguredState({
   523	      direct,
   524	      config,
   525	    })
   526	
   527	    election.startStage =
   528	      "configured"
   529	
   530	    await election.save()
   531	
   532	    const expectedTranscriptHash =
   533	      String(
   534	        election
   535	          .trusteeTranscriptHash,
   536	      )
   537	        .toLowerCase()
   538	
   539	    const pinned =
   540	      String(
   541	        await direct
   542	          .trusteeTranscriptHash(),
   543	      )
   544	        .toLowerCase()
   545	
   546	    if (
   547	      pinned ===
   548	      ZeroHash.toLowerCase()
   549	    ) {
   550	      const tx =
   551	        await direct
   552	          .setTrusteeTranscriptHash(
   553	            expectedTranscriptHash,
   554	          )
   555	
   556	      await tx.wait()
   557	    } else if (
   558	      pinned !==
   559	      expectedTranscriptHash
   560	    ) {
   561	      throw lifecycleError(
   562	        "TRUSTEE_TRANSCRIPT_MISMATCH",
   563	        "VoteChainDirect already has a different trustee transcript hash",
   564	      )
   565	    }
   566	
   567	    election.startStage =
   568	      "trustees_pinned"
   569	
   570	    await election.save()
   571	
   572	    const phaseBefore =
   573	      Number(
   574	        await direct
   575	          .phase(),
   576	      )
   577	
   578	    if (
   579	      phaseBefore === 0
   580	    ) {
   581	      const tx =
   582	        await direct
   583	          .activate()
   584	
   585	      await tx.wait()
   586	    } else if (
   587	      phaseBefore !== 1
   588	    ) {
   589	      throw lifecycleError(
   590	        "DIRECT_PHASE_INVALID",
   591	        `Cannot activate VoteChainDirect from phase ${phaseBefore}`,
   592	      )
   593	    }
   594	
   595	    const phaseAfter =
   596	      Number(
   597	        await direct
   598	          .phase(),
   599	      )
   600	
   601	    if (
   602	      phaseAfter !== 1
   603	    ) {
   604	      throw lifecycleError(
   605	        "DIRECT_ACTIVATION_FAILED",
   606	        "VoteChainDirect did not enter Active phase",
   607	      )
   608	    }
   609	
   610	    election.status =
   611	      "active"
   612	
   613	    election.startStage =
   614	      "active"
   615	
   616	    if (
   617	      !election.startedAt
   618	    ) {
   619	      election.startedAt =
   620	        new Date()
   621	    }
   622	
   623	    election.failureReason =
   624	      undefined
   625	
   626	    await election.save()
   627	
   628	    return {
   629	      electionId:
   630	        election.electionId,
   631	
   632	      status:
   633	        election.status,
   634	
   635	      startStage:
   636	        election.startStage,
   637	
   638	      verifierAddress:
   639	        election.verifierAddress,
   640	
   641	      directContractAddress:
   642	        election
   643	          .directContractAddress,
   644	
   645	      eligibleRoot:
   646	        election.eligibleRoot,
   647	
   648	      eligibleVoterCount:
   649	        election
   650	          .eligibleVoterCount,
   651	
   652	      candidateManifestHash:
   653	        election
   654	          .candidateManifestHash,
   655	
   656	      candidateCount:
   657	        election.candidateCount,
   658	
   659	      trusteeTranscriptHash:
   660	        election
   661	          .trusteeTranscriptHash,
   662	
   663	      contractDomain:
   664	        election.contractDomain,
   665	
   666	      Yx:
   667	        election.Yx,
   668	
   669	      Yy:
   670	        election.Yy,
   671	
   672	      electionContextHash:
   673	        election
   674	          .electionContextHash,
   675	    }
   676	  } catch (error) {
   677	    election.failureReason =
   678	      error.message
   679	
   680	    await election.save()
   681	
   682	    throw error
   683	  }
   684	}

    11	function lifecycleError(
    12	  code,
    13	  message,
    14	) {
    15	  const error =
    16	    new Error(
    17	      message,
    18	    )
    19	
    20	  error.code =
    21	    code
    22	
    23	  return error
    24	}

    26	export async function startElectionLifecycle({
    27	  electionId,
    28	}) {
    29	  let election =
    30	    await Election
    31	      .findOne({
    32	        electionId:
    33	          String(
    34	            electionId,
    35	          ),
    36	        lifecycleSlot:
    37	          "current",
    38	      })
    39	
    40	  if (!election) {
    41	    throw lifecycleError(
    42	      "ELECTION_NOT_FOUND",
    43	      "Current election was not found",
    44	    )
    45	  }
    46	
    47	  if (
    48	    election.status ===
    49	      "active"
    50	  ) {
    51	    return {
    52	      electionId:
    53	        election.electionId,
    54	
    55	      status:
    56	        election.status,
    57	
    58	      startStage:
    59	        election.startStage,
    60	
    61	      verifierAddress:
    62	        election.verifierAddress,
    63	
    64	      directContractAddress:
    65	        election
    66	          .directContractAddress,
    67	
    68	      eligibleRoot:
    69	        election.eligibleRoot,
    70	
    71	      eligibleVoterCount:
    72	        election
    73	          .eligibleVoterCount,
    74	
    75	      candidateManifestHash:
    76	        election
    77	          .candidateManifestHash,
    78	
    79	      candidateCount:
    80	        election.candidateCount,
    81	
    82	      trusteeTranscriptHash:
    83	        election
    84	          .trusteeTranscriptHash,
    85	
    86	      contractDomain:
    87	        election.contractDomain,
    88	
    89	      Yx:
    90	        election.Yx,
    91	
    92	      Yy:
    93	        election.Yy,
    94	
    95	      electionContextHash:
    96	        election
    97	          .electionContextHash,
    98	
    99	      alreadyActive:
   100	        true,
   101	    }
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  if (
   105	    ![
   106	      "registration",
   107	      "starting",
   108	    ].includes(
   109	      election.status,
   110	    )
   111	  ) {
   112	    throw lifecycleError(
   113	      "ELECTION_CANNOT_START",
   114	      `Election cannot start from status ${election.status}`,
   115	    )
   116	  }
   117	
   118	  if (
   119	    !election.trusteeTranscript ||
   120	    !election.trusteeTranscriptHash ||
   121	    !election.Yx ||
   122	    !election.Yy
   123	  ) {
   124	    throw lifecycleError(
   125	      "TRUSTEE_TRANSCRIPT_REQUIRED",
   126	      "Upload and verify the public WP6 trustee transcript before starting the election",
   127	    )
   128	  }
   129	
   130	  const snapshot =
   131	    await prepareElectionSnapshot()
   132	
   133	  if (
   134	    String(
   135	      snapshot
   136	        .election
   137	        .electionId,
   138	    ) !==
   139	      String(
   140	        electionId,
   141	      )
   142	  ) {
   143	    throw lifecycleError(
   144	      "CURRENT_ELECTION_MISMATCH",
   145	      "The current election changed while Start Election was running",
   146	    )
   147	  }
   148	
   149	  election =
   150	    await Election
   151	      .findById(
   152	        snapshot
   153	          .election
   154	          ._id,
   155	      )
   156	
   157	  return activateElectionOnChain(
   158	    election,
   159	  )
   160	}

     1	import {
     2	  getAddress,
     3	} from "ethers"
     4	
     5	import Election from "../../models/Election.js"
     6	
     7	import {
     8	  context,
     9	} from "../../../crypto/src/wp2-core-final.mjs"

    11	import {
    12	  canonicalSha256,
    13	  verifyDkgTranscript,
    14	} from "../../../crypto/src/wp6-threshold.mjs"
    15	
    16	function lifecycleError(
    17	  code,
    18	  message,
    19	) {
    20	  const error =
    21	    new Error(
    22	      message,
    23	    )
    24	
    25	  error.code =
    26	    code
    27	
    28	  return error
    29	}
    30	
    31	function normalizeBytes32(
    32	  value,
    33	) {
    34	  const text =
    35	    String(
    36	      value,
    37	    )
    38	      .trim()
    39	      .replace(
    40	        /^0x/i,
    41	        "",
    42	      )
    43	
    44	  if (
    45	    !/^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/
    46	      .test(
    47	        text,
    48	      )
    49	  ) {
    50	    throw lifecycleError(
    51	      "INVALID_DKG_TRANSCRIPT",
    52	      "DKG transcript hash is not a 32-byte SHA-256 value",
    53	    )
    54	  }
    55	
    56	  return (
    57	    "0x" +
    58	    text.toLowerCase()
    59	  )
    60	}
    61	
    62	export async function validateElectionDkgTranscript(
    63	  transcript,
    64	) {
    65	  if (
    66	    !transcript ||
    67	    typeof transcript !==
    68	      "object" ||
    69	    Array.isArray(
    70	      transcript,
    71	    )
    72	  ) {
    73	    throw lifecycleError(
    74	      "INVALID_DKG_TRANSCRIPT",
    75	      "A public WP6 DKG transcript object is required",
    76	    )
    77	  }
    78	
    79	  let verified
    80	
    81	  try {
    82	    const c =
    83	      await context()
    84	
    85	    verified =
    86	      verifyDkgTranscript(
    87	        c,
    88	        transcript,
    89	      )
    90	  } catch (error) {
    91	    throw lifecycleError(
    92	      "INVALID_DKG_TRANSCRIPT",
    93	      `WP6 DKG transcript verification failed: ${error.message}`,
    94	    )
    95	  }
    96	
    97	  if (
    98	    verified === false
    99	  ) {
   100	    throw lifecycleError(
   101	      "INVALID_DKG_TRANSCRIPT",
   102	      "WP6 DKG transcript verification returned false",
   103	    )
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  if (
   107	    !Array.isArray(
   108	      transcript.publicKey,
   109	    ) ||
   110	    transcript.publicKey.length !==
   111	      2
   112	  ) {
   113	    throw lifecycleError(
   114	      "INVALID_DKG_TRANSCRIPT",
   115	      "Verified DKG transcript does not contain a two-coordinate public key",
   116	    )
   117	  }
   118	
   119	  let Yx
   120	  let Yy
   121	
   122	  try {
   123	    Yx =
   124	      BigInt(
   125	        transcript
   126	          .publicKey[0],
   127	      )
   128	        .toString()
   129	
   130	    Yy =
   131	      BigInt(
   132	        transcript
   133	          .publicKey[1],
   134	      )
   135	        .toString()
   136	  } catch {
   137	    throw lifecycleError(
   138	      "INVALID_DKG_TRANSCRIPT",
   139	      "DKG public key coordinates are invalid",
   140	    )
   141	  }
   142	
   143	  const transcriptHash =
   144	    normalizeBytes32(
   145	      canonicalSha256(
   146	        transcript,
   147	      ),
   148	    )
   149	
   150	  return {
   151	    transcriptHash,
   152	    Yx,
   153	    Yy,
   154	  }
   155	}
   156	
   157	export async function storeElectionTrusteeTranscript({
   158	  electionId,
   159	  transcript,
   160	  adminAddress,
   161	}) {
   162	  const election =
   163	    await Election
   164	      .findOne({
   165	        electionId:
   166	          String(
   167	            electionId,
   168	          ),
   169	        lifecycleSlot:
   170	          "current",
   171	      })
   172	
   173	  if (!election) {
   174	    throw lifecycleError(
   175	      "ELECTION_NOT_FOUND",
   176	      "Current election was not found",
   177	    )
   178	  }
   179	
   180	  if (
   181	    election.status !==
   182	      "registration"
   183	  ) {
   184	    throw lifecycleError(
   185	      "ELECTION_NOT_IN_REGISTRATION",
   186	      "Trustee transcript can only be set while registration is open",
   187	    )
   188	  }
   189	
   190	  if (
   191	    election.adminAddress &&
   192	    getAddress(
   193	      election.adminAddress,
   194	    ) !==
   195	      getAddress(
   196	        adminAddress,
   197	      )
   198	  ) {
   199	    throw lifecycleError(
   200	      "ELECTION_ADMIN_MISMATCH",
   201	      "Authenticated administrator does not own this election",
   202	    )
   203	  }
   204	
   205	  const {
   206	    transcriptHash,
   207	    Yx,
   208	    Yy,
   209	  } =
   210	    await validateElectionDkgTranscript(
   211	      transcript,
   212	    )
   213	
   214	  const ceremonyId =
   215	    transcript.ceremonyId ??
   216	    transcript
   217	      .context
   218	      ?.ceremonyId
   219	
   220	  if (
   221	    ceremonyId !==
   222	      undefined &&
   223	    String(
   224	      ceremonyId,
   225	    ) !==
   226	      String(
   227	        election.electionId,
   228	      )
   229	  ) {
   230	    throw lifecycleError(
   231	      "DKG_CEREMONY_MISMATCH",
   232	      "The DKG ceremony identifier does not match this electionId",
   233	    )
   234	  }
   235	
   236	  election.trusteeTranscript =
   237	    transcript
   238	
   239	  election.trusteeTranscriptHash =
   240	    transcriptHash
   241	
   242	  election.Yx =
   243	    Yx
   244	
   245	  election.Yy =
   246	    Yy
   247	
   248	  election.trusteeTranscriptVerifiedAt =
   249	    new Date()
   250	
   251	  election.markModified(
   252	    "trusteeTranscript",
   253	  )
   254	
   255	  await election.save()
   256	
   257	  return {
   258	    election,
   259	    transcriptHash,
   260	    Yx,
   261	    Yy,
   262	  }
   263	}
