    36	let poseidonPromise
    37	
    38	async function poseidon() {
    39	  if (!poseidonPromise) poseidonPromise = buildPoseidon()
    40	  return poseidonPromise
    41	}
    42	
    43	export async function wp5Hash(values) {
    44	  const p = await poseidon()
    45	  return BigInt(
    46	    p.F.toObject(
    47	      p(values.map((value) => BigInt(value))),
    48	    ),
    49	  )
    50	}
    51	
    52	function wp5SecretKey(secret = process.env.WP5_CREDENTIAL_SECRET_KEY) {
    53	  if (!secret) {
    54	    throw new Error('WP5_CREDENTIAL_SECRET_KEY is missing')
    55	  }
    56	  if (Buffer.byteLength(secret, 'utf8') !== 32) {
    57	    throw new Error('WP5_CREDENTIAL_SECRET_KEY must be exactly 32 bytes')
    58	  }
    59	  return secret
    60	}
    61	
    62	function randomFieldSecret() {
    63	  const raw = BigInt(`0x${crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex')}`)
    64	  return (raw % (FIELD - 1n)) + 1n
    65	}
    66	
    67	export async function commitmentFromWp5Secret(secret) {
    68	  return wp5Hash([D_ID, BigInt(secret)])
    69	}
    70	
    71	export async function allocateWp5GroupIndex() {
    72	  const highest = await Voter
    73	    .findOne({ wp5GroupIndex: { $type: 'number' } })
    74	    .sort({ wp5GroupIndex: -1 })
    75	    .select({ wp5GroupIndex: 1 })
    76	    .lean()
    77	
    78	  const next = highest?.wp5GroupIndex === undefined
    79	    ? 0
    80	    : Number(highest.wp5GroupIndex) + 1
    81	
    82	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(next) || next < 0 || next >= CAPACITY) {
    83	    throw new Error('WP5 eligible voter capacity exhausted')
    84	  }
    85	
    86	  return next
    87	}
    88	
    89	export async function createWp5Credential(options = {}) {
    90	  const secret = options.secret === undefined
    91	    ? randomFieldSecret()
    92	    : BigInt(options.secret)
    93	
    94	  if (secret <= 0n || secret >= FIELD) {
    95	    throw new Error('WP5 secret must be a nonzero BN254 field element')
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  const commitment = await commitmentFromWp5Secret(secret)
    99	
   100	  const encryptedCredential = encryptZkCredential(
   101	    secret.toString(),
   102	    commitment.toString(),
   103	    wp5SecretKey(options.credentialKey),
   104	  )
   105	
   106	  return {
   107	    secret: secret.toString(),
   108	    identityCommitment: commitment.toString(),
   109	    encryptedCredential,
   110	  }
   111	}
   112	
   113	async function zeroHashes() {
   114	  const zeros = [0n]
   115	
   116	  for (let level = 0; level < DEPTH; level += 1) {
   117	    zeros.push(
   118	      await wp5Hash([
   119	        zeros[level],
   120	        zeros[level],
   121	      ]),
   122	    )
   123	  }
   124	
   125	  return zeros
   126	}
   127	
   128	function normalizeRecords(records) {
   129	  const normalized = records
   130	    .map((record) => ({
   131	      index: Number(record.wp5GroupIndex),
   132	      commitment: BigInt(record.wp5IdentityCommitment),
   133	    }))
   134	    .sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index)
   135	
   136	  normalized.forEach((record, expectedIndex) => {
   137	    if (
   138	      !Number.isSafeInteger(record.index) ||
   139	      record.index !== expectedIndex
   140	    ) {
   141	      throw new Error(
   142	        `WP5 Merkle indices must be contiguous from zero; expected ${expectedIndex}, got ${record.index}`,
   143	      )
   144	    }
   145	  })
   146	
   147	  if (normalized.length > CAPACITY) {
   148	    throw new Error('WP5 Merkle capacity exceeded')
   149	  }
   150	
   151	  return normalized
   152	}
   153	
   154	export async function buildWp5MerkleSnapshot(records, targetIndex = null) {
   155	  const normalized = normalizeRecords(records)
   156	  const zeros = await zeroHashes()
   157	
   158	  let current = new Map(
   159	    normalized.map((record) => [
   160	      record.index,
   161	      record.commitment,
   162	    ]),
   163	  )
   164	
   165	  const siblings = []
   166	  const pathBits = []
   167	  let cursor = targetIndex
   168	
   169	  if (
   170	    cursor !== null &&
   171	    (
   172	      !Number.isSafeInteger(cursor) ||
   173	      cursor < 0 ||
   174	      cursor >= normalized.length
   175	    )
   176	  ) {
   177	    throw new Error('Invalid WP5 witness index')
   178	  }
   179	
   180	  for (let level = 0; level < DEPTH; level += 1) {
   181	    if (cursor !== null) {
   182	      const bit = cursor & 1
   183	      const siblingIndex = bit === 0 ? cursor + 1 : cursor - 1
   184	
   185	      pathBits.push(String(bit))
   186	      siblings.push(
   187	        String(
   188	          current.get(siblingIndex) ?? zeros[level],
   189	        ),
   190	      )
   191	
   192	      cursor = Math.floor(cursor / 2)
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    const parents = new Set(
   196	      [...current.keys()].map((index) => Math.floor(index / 2)),
   197	    )
   198	
   199	    const next = new Map()
   200	
   201	    for (const parent of parents) {
   202	      const left = current.get(parent * 2) ?? zeros[level]
   203	      const right = current.get(parent * 2 + 1) ?? zeros[level]
   204	
   205	      next.set(
   206	        parent,
   207	        await wp5Hash([left, right]),
   208	      )
   209	    }
   210	
   211	    current = next
   212	  }
   213	
   214	  const root = current.get(0) ?? zeros[DEPTH]
   215	
   216	  return {
   217	    root: root.toString(),
   218	    size: normalized.length,
   219	    depth: DEPTH,
   220	    siblings,
   221	    pathBits,
   222	  }
   223	}
   224	
   225	async function registeredWp5Voters() {
   226	  return Voter
   227	    .find({
   228	      wp5RegistrationStatus: 'registered',
   229	      wp5IdentityCommitment: { $type: 'string' },
   230	      wp5GroupIndex: { $type: 'number' },
   231	    })
   232	    .sort({ wp5GroupIndex: 1 })
   233	    .select({
   234	      wp5IdentityCommitment: 1,
   235	      wp5GroupIndex: 1,
   236	    })
   237	    .lean()
   238	}
   239	
   240	export async function witnessForWp5Voter(voter) {
   241	  if (
   242	    voter?.wp5RegistrationStatus !== "registered" ||
   243	    !voter?.wp5IdentityCommitment
   244	  ) {
   245	    throw new Error(
   246	      "Voter does not have an active WP5 custom credential",
   247	    )
   248	  }
   249	
   250	  const {
   251	    election,
   252	    index,
   253	    records,
   254	  } =
   255	    await getActiveElectionMembership(
   256	      voter,
   257	    )
   258	
   259	  const snapshot =
   260	    await buildWp5MerkleSnapshot(
   261	      records,
   262	      index,
   263	    )
   264	
   265	  if (
   266	    !election.eligibleRoot ||
   267	    BigInt(snapshot.root) !==
   268	      BigInt(
   269	        election.eligibleRoot,
   270	      )
   271	  ) {
   272	    throw new Error(
   273	      "Election membership Merkle root does not match the frozen election snapshot",
   274	    )
   275	  }
   276	
   277	  return {
   278	    identityCommitment:
   279	      String(
   280	        voter.wp5IdentityCommitment,
   281	      ),
   282	
   283	    index,
   284	
   285	    eligibleRoot:
   286	      snapshot.root,
   287	
   288	    siblings:
   289	      snapshot.siblings,
   290	
   291	    pathBits:
   292	      snapshot.pathBits,
   293	  }
   294	}
   295	
   296	export async function hashCandidateManifest(candidateIds) {
   297	  const ids = candidateIds.map(
   298	    (candidateId) => BigInt(candidateId),
   299	  )
   300	
   301	  if (
   302	    ids.length < 1 ||
   303	    ids.length > Number(profile.maxCandidates)
   304	  ) {
   305	    throw new Error(
   306	      'Candidate manifest length is outside the frozen WP5 range',
   307	    )
   308	  }
   309	
   310	  if (
   311	    WP5_MANIFEST_ALGORITHM ===
   312	    'POSEIDON_DOMAIN_PLUS_IDS_V1'
   313	  ) {
   314	    return wp5Hash([
   315	      D_MANIFEST,
   316	      ...ids,
   317	    ])
   318	  }
   319	
   320	  if (
   321	    WP5_MANIFEST_ALGORITHM ===
   322	    'POSEIDON_INDEXED_CHAIN_V1'
   323	  ) {
   324	    let state = await wp5Hash([
   325	      D_MANIFEST,
   326	      BigInt(ids.length),
   327	    ])
   328	
   329	    for (
   330	      let index = 0;
   331	      index < ids.length;
   332	      index += 1
   333	    ) {
   334	      state = await wp5Hash([
   335	        state,
   336	        BigInt(index),
   337	        ids[index],
   338	      ])
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    return state
   342	  }
   343	
   344	  throw new Error(
   345	    `Unsupported WP5 candidate-manifest algorithm: ${WP5_MANIFEST_ALGORITHM}`,
   346	  )
   347	}
   348	
   349	export async function getRegisteredWp5Candidates() {
   350	  const candidates = await Candidate
   351	    .find({
   352	      registrationStatus: 'registered',
   353	      candidateId: { $exists: true },
   354	    })
   355	    .sort({ candidateId: 1 })
   356	    .select({
   357	      candidateId: 1,
   358	      name: 1,
   359	      party: 1,
   360	      location: 1,
   361	    })
   362	    .lean()
   363	
   364	  return candidates.map((candidate) => ({
   365	    candidateId: Number(candidate.candidateId),
   366	    name: candidate.name,
   367	    party: candidate.party,
   368	    location: candidate.location,
   369	  }))
   370	}
   371	
   372	function validateClientJwk(jwk) {
   373	  if (
   374	    !jwk ||
   375	    jwk.kty !== 'EC' ||
   376	    jwk.crv !== 'P-256' ||
   377	    typeof jwk.x !== 'string' ||
   378	    typeof jwk.y !== 'string' ||
   379	    'd' in jwk
   380	  ) {
   381	    throw new Error('A public P-256 credential-release key is required')
   382	  }
   383	
   384	  return jwk
   385	}
   386	
   387	async function sealCredentialForClient({
   388	  plaintextSecret,
   389	  clientPublicJwk,
   390	  identityCommitment,
   391	  electionId,
   392	  expiresAt,
   393	}) {
   394	  const subtle = crypto.webcrypto.subtle
   395	  const clientPublicKey = await subtle.importKey(
   396	    'jwk',
   397	    validateClientJwk(clientPublicJwk),
   398	    { name: 'ECDH', namedCurve: 'P-256' },
   399	    false,
   400	    [],
   401	  )
   402	
   403	  const serverKeyPair = await subtle.generateKey(
   404	    { name: 'ECDH', namedCurve: 'P-256' },
   405	    true,
   406	    ['deriveBits'],
   407	  )
   408	
   409	  const shared = await subtle.deriveBits(
   410	    {
   411	      name: 'ECDH',
   412	      public: clientPublicKey,
   413	    },
   414	    serverKeyPair.privateKey,
   415	    256,
   416	  )
   417	
   418	  const salt = crypto.randomBytes(32)
   419	  const iv = crypto.randomBytes(12)
   420	  const info = Buffer.from(
   421	    'VoteChain-ZK/WP5/credential-release/v1',
   422	    'utf8',
   423	  )
   424	
   425	  const ikm = await subtle.importKey(
   426	    'raw',
   427	    shared,
   428	    'HKDF',
   429	    false,
   430	    ['deriveKey'],
   431	  )
   432	
   433	  const aesKey = await subtle.deriveKey(
   434	    {
   435	      name: 'HKDF',
   436	      hash: 'SHA-256',
   437	      salt,
   438	      info,
   439	    },
   440	    ikm,
   441	    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
   442	    false,
   443	    ['encrypt'],
   444	  )
   445	
   446	  const aad = Buffer.from(
   447	    [
   448	      'VoteChain-ZK/WP5/v1',
   449	      identityCommitment,
   450	      electionId,
   451	      String(expiresAt),
   452	    ].join('|'),
   453	    'utf8',
   454	  )
   455	
   456	  const ciphertext = await subtle.encrypt(
   457	    {
   458	      name: 'AES-GCM',
   459	      iv,
   460	      additionalData: aad,
   461	    },
   462	    aesKey,
   463	    Buffer.from(String(plaintextSecret), 'utf8'),
   464	  )
   465	
   466	  return {
   467	    version: 1,
   468	    keyAgreement: 'ECDH-P256-HKDF-SHA256',
   469	    contentEncryption: 'AES-256-GCM',
   470	    serverPublicKey: await subtle.exportKey(
   471	      'jwk',
   472	      serverKeyPair.publicKey,
   473	    ),
   474	    salt: salt.toString('base64'),
   475	    iv: iv.toString('base64'),
   476	    aad: aad.toString('base64'),
   477	    ciphertext: Buffer.from(ciphertext).toString('base64'),
   478	    expiresAt,
   479	  }
   480	}
   481	
   482	export async function authorizeWp5Voter({
   483	  voter,
   484	  clientPublicKey,
   485	  now = Date.now(),
   486	}) {
   487	  if (!voter) {
   488	    throw new Error('Biometrically verified voter record is required')
   489	  }
   490	
   491	  if (
   492	    voter.wp5RegistrationStatus !== 'registered' ||
   493	    !voter.wp5IdentityCommitment ||
   494	    !voter.wp5Credential
   495	  ) {
   496	    throw new Error(
   497	      'Voter requires WP5 custom-credential enrollment before private voting',
   498	    )
   499	  }
   500	
   501	  const {
   502	    election: activeElection,
   503	    contract,
   504	  } =
   505	    await getActiveWp5Contract()
   506	
   507	  const [
   508	    phase,
   509	    electionId,
   510	    eligibleRoot,
   511	  ] = await Promise.all([
   512	    contract.phase(),
   513	    contract.electionId(),
   514	    contract.eligibleRoot(),
   515	  ])
   516	
   517	  if (
   518	    String(
   519	      activeElection.electionId,
   520	    ) !==
   521	      String(
   522	        electionId,
   523	      )
   524	  ) {
   525	    throw new Error(
   526	      "Active election record does not match the configured Direct contract",
   527	    )
   528	  }
   529	
   530	  if (Number(phase) !== 1) {
   531	    throw new Error(
   532	      'WP5 credential release is only available while the election is Active',
   533	    )
   534	  }
   535	
   536	  const witness = await witnessForWp5Voter(voter)
   537	
   538	  if (BigInt(witness.eligibleRoot) !== BigInt(eligibleRoot)) {
   539	    throw new Error(
   540	      'WP5 local Merkle root does not match the frozen contract root',
   541	    )
   542	  }
   543	
   544	  const plaintextSecret = decryptZkCredential(
   545	    voter.wp5Credential,
   546	    voter.wp5IdentityCommitment,
   547	    wp5SecretKey(),
   548	  )
   549	
   550	  const recomputedCommitment =
   551	    await commitmentFromWp5Secret(plaintextSecret)
   552	
   553	  if (
   554	    recomputedCommitment.toString() !==
   555	    String(voter.wp5IdentityCommitment)
   556	  ) {
   557	    throw new Error('WP5 credential/commitment mismatch')
   558	  }
   559	
   560	  const expiresAt = now + 2 * 60 * 1000
   561	
   562	  const credentialEnvelope =
   563	    await sealCredentialForClient({
   564	      plaintextSecret,
   565	      clientPublicJwk: clientPublicKey,
   566	      identityCommitment:
   567	        String(voter.wp5IdentityCommitment),
   568	      electionId: String(electionId),
   569	      expiresAt,
   570	    })
   571	
   572	  return {
   573	    version: 'VoteChain-ZK-WP5-Authorization-v1',
   574	    electionId: String(electionId),
   575	    expiresAt,
   576	    membership: witness,
   577	    credentialEnvelope,
   578	  }
   579	}
   580	
   581	export async function getWp5PublicConfig() {
   582	  const {
   583	    election: contractElection,
   584	    contract,
   585	  } =
   586	    await getActiveWp5Contract()
   587	
   588	  const {
   589	    election: activeElection,
   590	    candidates,
   591	  } =
   592	    await getActiveElectionCandidates()
   593	
   594	  if (
   595	    String(
   596	      contractElection._id,
   597	    ) !==
   598	      String(
   599	        activeElection._id,
   600	      )
   601	  ) {
   602	    throw new Error(
   603	      "Contract election and candidate snapshot refer to different elections",
   604	    )
   605	  }
   606	
   607	  const [
   608	    phase,
   609	    configured,
   610	    eligibleRoot,
   611	    electionId,
   612	    candidateManifestHash,
   613	    candidateCount,
   614	    contractDomain,
   615	    Yx,
   616	    Yy,
   617	    electionContextHash,
   618	    acceptedBallotCount,
   619	  ] = await Promise.all([
   620	    contract.phase(),
   621	    contract.configured(),
   622	    contract.eligibleRoot(),
   623	    contract.electionId(),
   624	    contract.candidateManifestHash(),
   625	    contract.candidateCount(),
   626	    contract.contractDomain(),
   627	    contract.Yx(),
   628	    contract.Yy(),
   629	    contract.electionContextHash(),
   630	    contract.acceptedBallotCount(),
   631	  ])
   632	
   633	  if (
   634	    String(activeElection.electionId || "") &&
   635	    String(activeElection.electionId) !==
   636	      String(electionId)
   637	  ) {
   638	    throw new Error(
   639	      "Active election record does not match the configured contract electionId",
   640	    )
   641	  }
   642	
   643	  const candidateIds =
   644	    candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.candidateId)
   645	
   646	  const localManifest =
   647	    await hashCandidateManifest(candidateIds)
   648	
   649	  if (Boolean(configured)) {
   650	    if (
   651	      BigInt(candidateCount) !== BigInt(candidateIds.length) ||
   652	      BigInt(candidateManifestHash) !== localManifest
   653	    ) {
   654	      throw new Error(
   655	        'WP5 candidate manifest does not match the configured contract',
   656	      )
   657	    }
   658	  }
   659	
   660	  return {
   661	    directContractAddress:
   662	      contractElection
   663	        .directContractAddress,
   664	
   665	    profile: 'VoteChain-ZK-WP5-v1',
   666	    phase: Number(phase),
   667	    configured: Boolean(configured),
   668	    acceptedBallotCount: String(acceptedBallotCount),
   669	    candidates,
   670	    manifestAlgorithm: WP5_MANIFEST_ALGORITHM,
   671	    config: {
   672	      eligibleRoot: String(eligibleRoot),
   673	      electionId: String(electionId),
   674	      candidateManifestHash: String(candidateManifestHash),
   675	      candidateCount: String(candidateCount),
   676	      contractDomain: String(contractDomain),
   677	      Yx: String(Yx),
   678	      Yy: String(Yy),
   679	      electionContextHash: String(electionContextHash),
   680	    },
   681	  }
   682	}

    77	export async function getActiveWp5Contract() {
    78	  const election =
    79	    await Election
    80	      .findOne({
    81	        lifecycleSlot:
    82	          "current",
    83	        status:
    84	          "active",
    85	        directContractAddress: {
    86	          $type: "string",
    87	        },
    88	      })
    89	      .select({
    90	        electionId: 1,
    91	        chainId: 1,
    92	        directContractAddress: 1,
    93	      })
    94	
    95	  if (
    96	    !election ||
    97	    !election
    98	      .directContractAddress
    99	      ?.trim()
   100	  ) {
   101	    throw new Error(
   102	      "No active election Direct contract is available",
   103	    )
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  const provider =
   107	    getWp5Provider()
   108	
   109	  const network =
   110	    await provider
   111	      .getNetwork()
   112	
   113	  if (
   114	    election.chainId &&
   115	    String(
   116	      network.chainId,
   117	    ) !==
   118	      String(
   119	        election.chainId,
   120	      )
   121	  ) {
   122	    throw new Error(
   123	      "Active election chainId does not match the connected RPC network",
   124	    )
   125	  }
   126	
   127	  const address =
   128	    election
   129	      .directContractAddress
   130	      .trim()
   131	
   132	  const code =
   133	    await provider
   134	      .getCode(
   135	        address,
   136	      )
   137	
   138	  if (
   139	    !code ||
   140	    code === "0x"
   141	  ) {
   142	    throw new Error(
   143	      "The active election Direct contract is not deployed on the connected chain",
   144	    )
   145	  }
   146	
   147	  return {
   148	    election,
   149	
   150	    contract:
   151	      new Contract(
   152	        address,
   153	        abi,
   154	        provider,
   155	      ),
   156	  }
   157	}

   163	export function getWp5ContractArtifact() {
   164	  if (
   165	    !directArtifact ||
   166	    !Array.isArray(
   167	      directArtifact.abi,
   168	    ) ||
   169	    !directArtifact
   170	      .bytecode
   171	      ?.object
   172	  ) {
   173	    throw new Error(
   174	      "VoteChainDirect Foundry artifact is unavailable",
   175	    )
   176	  }
   177	
   178	  return directArtifact
   179	}

============================== END FILE ==============================

     1	import test from 'node:test'
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict'
     3	import fs from 'node:fs'
     4	import {
     5	  hashCandidateManifest,
     6	} from '../services/zk/wp5PrivacyService.js'
     7	
     8	const appProfile = JSON.parse(
     9	  fs.readFileSync(
    10	    new URL(
    11	      '../../crypto/config/wp5-application-profile.json',
    12	      import.meta.url,
    13	    ),
    14	    'utf8',
    15	  ),
    16	)
    17	
    18	test(
    19	  'WP5 K16 manifest matches frozen compatibility profile',
    20	  async () => {
    21	    assert.equal(
    22	      appProfile.candidateManifest.algorithm,
    23	      'POSEIDON_INDEXED_CHAIN_V1',
    24	    )
    25	
    26	    const candidateIds =
    27	      Array.from(
    28	        { length: 16 },
    29	        (_value, index) =>
    30	          101 + index,
    31	      )
    32	
    33	    const actual =
    34	      await hashCandidateManifest(
    35	        candidateIds,
    36	      )
    37	
    38	    assert.equal(
    39	      actual.toString(),
    40	      String(
    41	        appProfile
    42	          .candidateManifest
    43	          .manifest16,
    44	      ),
    45	    )
    46	  },
    47	)
    48	
    49	test(
    50	  'WP5 indexed manifest is order-sensitive',
    51	  async () => {
    52	    const a =
    53	      await hashCandidateManifest(
    54	        [101, 102, 103, 104],
    55	      )
    56	
    57	    const b =
    58	      await hashCandidateManifest(
    59	        [101, 103, 102, 104],
    60	      )
    61	
    62	    assert.notEqual(
    63	      a.toString(),
    64	      b.toString(),
    65	    )
    66	  },
    67	)

FILE: votechain-central-server/server.js
======================================================================
     1	import express from 'express'
     2	import mongoose from 'mongoose'
     3	import cors from 'cors'
     4	import helmet from 'helmet'
     5	import rateLimit from 'express-rate-limit'
     6	import voterRoutes from './routes/voterRoutes.js'
     7	import candidateRoutes from './routes/candidateRoutes.js'
     8	import electionRoutes from './routes/electionRoutes.js'
     9	import voteRoutes from './routes/voteRoutes.js'
    10	import biometricRoutes from './routes/biometricRoutes.js'
    11	import blockchainRoutes from './routes/blockchainRoutes.js'
    12	import wp5Routes from './routes/wp5Routes.js'
    13	import { getServerConfig } from './config/env.js'
    14	
    15	const { port, mongoUri, frontendOrigins } = getServerConfig()
    16	const app = express()
    17	
    18	app.use(helmet({
    19	  contentSecurityPolicy: {
    20	    directives: {
    21	      defaultSrc: ["'self'"],
    22	      connectSrc: ["'self'", ...frontendOrigins],
    23	      scriptSrc: ["'self'", "'unsafe-inline'", "'unsafe-eval'"],
    24	      styleSrc: ["'self'", "'unsafe-inline'"],
    25	      imgSrc: ["'self'", 'data:', 'https:'],
    26	      fontSrc: ["'self'"],
    27	    },
    28	  },
    29	}))
    30	
    31	app.use(rateLimit({
    32	  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,
    33	  max: 100,
    34	}))
    35	
    36	app.use(cors({
    37	  origin: frontendOrigins.length > 0 ? frontendOrigins : false,
    38	  methods: ['GET', 'POST', 'PATCH', 'PUT', 'DELETE'],
    39	  allowedHeaders: ['Content-Type', 'Authorization'],
    40	  credentials: true,
    41	}))
    42	
    43	app.use(express.json({
    44	  limit: '16kb',
    45	  strict: true,
    46	}))
    47	app.use(express.urlencoded({
    48	  extended: true,
    49	  limit: '16kb',
    50	  parameterLimit: 50,
    51	}))
    52	
    53	mongoose.connect(mongoUri)
    54	  .then(() => console.log('MongoDB connected'))
    55	  .catch((error) => console.error('MongoDB connection error:', error.message))
    56	
    57	app.use('/api/voters', voterRoutes)
    58	app.use('/api/candidates', candidateRoutes)
    59	app.use('/api/elections', electionRoutes)
    60	app.use('/api/votes', voteRoutes)
    61	app.use('/api/biometric', biometricRoutes)
    62	app.use('/api/blockchain', blockchainRoutes)
    63	app.use('/api/wp5', wp5Routes)
    64	
    65	app.use((error, _req, res, _next) => {
    66	  if (
    67	    error?.type === 'entity.too.large' ||
    68	    Number(error?.status) === 413
    69	  ) {
    70	    return res.status(413).json({
    71	      success: false,
    72	      message: 'Request body is too large',
    73	    })
    74	  }
    75	
    76	  console.error(
    77	    '[Server] unhandled request error:',
    78	    error?.message || 'unknown error',
    79	  )
    80	
    81	  return res.status(500).json({
    82	    success: false,
    83	    message: 'Something went wrong!',
    84	  })
    85	})
    86	
    87	app.listen(port, () => {
    88	  console.log(`Server running on port ${port}`)
    89	})

    50	function mongoUri() {
    51	  const explicitKey =
    52	    process.env
    53	      .VOTECHAIN_MONGO_ENV_KEY
    54	      ?.trim()
    55	
    56	  if (explicitKey) {
    57	    const explicitValue =
    58	      process.env[
    59	        explicitKey
    60	      ]
    61	
    62	    if (
    63	      !explicitValue ||
    64	      !/^mongodb(?:\+srv)?:\/\//i
    65	        .test(
    66	          explicitValue,
    67	        )
    68	    ) {
    69	      throw new Error(
    70	        `VOTECHAIN_MONGO_ENV_KEY points to an invalid MongoDB URI variable: ${explicitKey}`,
    71	      )
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    return {
    75	      key:
    76	        explicitKey,
    77	
    78	      value:
    79	        explicitValue,
    80	    }
    81	  }
    82	
    83	
    84	  const candidates =
    85	    Object
    86	      .entries(
    87	        process.env,
    88	      )
    89	      .filter(
    90	        (
    91	          [
    92	            _key,
    93	            value,
    94	          ],
    95	        ) =>
    96	          /^mongodb(?:\+srv)?:\/\//i
    97	            .test(
    98	              String(
    99	                value ||
   100	                "",
   101	              ),
   102	            ),
   103	      )
   104	
   105	
   106	  if (
   107	    candidates.length ===
   108	      0
   109	  ) {
   110	    throw new Error(
   111	      "No MongoDB URI-valued environment variable was found",
   112	    )
   113	  }
   114	
   115	
   116	  if (
   117	    candidates.length >
   118	      1
   119	  ) {
   120	    throw new Error(
   121	      "Multiple MongoDB URI environment variables were found. Set VOTECHAIN_MONGO_ENV_KEY to one of: " +
   122	      candidates
   123	        .map(
   124	          ([key]) =>
   125	            key,
   126	        )
   127	        .join(", "),
   128	    )
   129	  }
   130	
   131	
   132	  return {
   133	    key:
   134	      candidates[0][0],
   135	
   136	    value:
   137	      candidates[0][1],
   138	  }
   139	}

   142	async function connectDatabase() {
   143	  const selected =
   144	    mongoUri()
   145	
   146	  await mongoose.connect(
   147	    selected.value,
   148	  )

   150	  console.log(
   151	    "MONGODB_CONNECTED=YES",
   152	  )
   153	
   154	  console.log(
   155	    `MONGODB_ENV_KEY=${selected.key}`,
   156	  )
   157	}

   160	async function localAdmin() {
   161	  const provider =
   162	    getProvider()
   163	
   164	  const legacy =
   165	    getContract()
   166	
   167	  const [
   168	    electionCommission,
   169	    signer,
   170	  ] =
   171	    await Promise.all([
   172	      legacy
   173	        .electionCommission(),
   174	
   175	      provider
   176	        .getSigner(
   177	          0,
   178	        ),
   179	    ])
   180	
   181	
   182	  const admin =
   183	    getAddress(
   184	      electionCommission,
   185	    )
   186	
   187	  const signerAddress =
   188	    getAddress(
   189	      await signer
   190	        .getAddress(),
   191	    )
   192	
   193	
   194	  console.log(
   195	    `LEGACY_ADMIN=${admin}`,
   196	  )
   197	
   198	  console.log(
   199	    `LOCAL_DEPLOYER=${signerAddress}`,
   200	  )
   201	
   202	  console.log(
   203	    `LOCAL_DEPLOYER_IS_ADMIN=${signerAddress === admin}`,
   204	  )
   205	
   206	
   207	  if (
   208	    signerAddress !==
   209	      admin
   210	  ) {
   211	    throw new Error(
   212	      "Anvil signer 0 is not the legacy Election Commission admin",
   213	    )
   214	  }
   215	
   216	
   217	  return admin
   218	}

   226	async function counts() {
   227	  const [
   228	    eligibleVoters,
   229	    registeredCandidates,
   230	  ] =
   231	    await Promise.all([
   232	      Voter.countDocuments({
   233	        wp5RegistrationStatus:
   234	          "registered",
   235	
   236	        wp5IdentityCommitment: {
   237	          $type:
   238	            "string",
   239	        },
   240	      }),
   241	
   242	      Candidate.countDocuments({
   243	        registrationStatus:
   244	          "registered",
   245	
   246	        candidateId: {
   247	          $exists:
   248	            true,
   249	        },
   250	      }),
   251	    ])
   252	
   253	
   254	  return {
   255	    eligibleVoters,
   256	    registeredCandidates,
   257	  }
   258	}

   261	async function printElection(
   262	  election,
   263	) {
   264	  if (!election) {
   265	    console.log(
   266	      "CURRENT_ELECTION=NONE",
   267	    )
   268	
   269	    return
   270	  }
   271	
   272	
   273	  console.log(
   274	    `ELECTION_ID=${election.electionId}`,
   275	  )
   276	
   277	  console.log(
   278	    `ELECTION_STATUS=${election.status}`,
   279	  )
   280	
   281	  console.log(
   282	    `START_STAGE=${election.startStage || "NONE"}`,
   283	  )
   284	
   285	  console.log(
   286	    `CHAIN_ID=${election.chainId || "NONE"}`,
   287	  )
   288	
   289	  console.log(
   290	    `TRUSTEE_TRANSCRIPT=${election.trusteeTranscriptHash ? "READY" : "NOT_READY"}`,
   291	  )
   292	
   293	  console.log(
   294	    `DIRECT_CONTRACT=${election.directContractAddress || "NOT_DEPLOYED"}`,
   295	  )
   296	
   297	  console.log(
   298	    `VERIFIER_CONTRACT=${election.verifierAddress || "NOT_DEPLOYED"}`,
   299	  )
   300	}

   303	async function commandCreate() {
   304	  const admin =
   305	    await localAdmin()
   306	
   307	  let election =
   308	    await current()
   309	
   310	
   311	  if (election) {
   312	    console.log(
   313	      "ELECTION_CREATE=SKIPPED_CURRENT_EXISTS",
   314	    )
   315	
   316	    await printElection(
   317	      election,
   318	    )
   319	
   320	    return
   321	  }
   322	
   323	
   324	  /*
   325	   * Release only terminal archived records from the unique
   326	   * lifecycle slot. This does not modify their election data.
   327	   */
   328	  await Election.updateMany(
   329	    {
   330	      lifecycleSlot:
   331	        "current",
   332	
   333	      status: {
   334	        $in: [
   335	          "finalized",
   336	          "failed",
   337	        ],
   338	      },
   339	    },
   340	    {
   341	      $unset: {
   342	        lifecycleSlot:
   343	          1,
   344	      },
   345	    },
   346	  )
   347	
   348	
   349	  election =
   350	    await createElectionRecord({
   351	      adminAddress:
   352	        admin,
   353	    })
   354	
   355	
   356	  console.log(
   357	    "ELECTION_CREATE=PASS",
   358	  )
   359	
   360	  await printElection(
   361	    election,
   362	  )
   363	}

   387	async function commandStoreTranscript() {
   388	  const admin =
   389	    await localAdmin()
   390	
   391	  const election =
   392	    await current()
   393	
   394	
   395	  if (!election) {
   396	    throw new Error(
   397	      "No current election exists",
   398	    )
   399	  }
   400	
   401	
   402	  if (
   403	    election.status !==
   404	      "registration"
   405	  ) {
   406	    throw new Error(
   407	      `Cannot store DKG transcript while election status is ${election.status}`,
   408	    )
   409	  }
   410	
   411	
   412	  const file =
   413	    resolve(
   414	      process.cwd(),
   415	      "..",
   416	      "crypto",
   417	      ".local",
   418	      "wp6-elections",
   419	      String(
   420	        election.electionId,
   421	      ),
   422	      "public",
   423	      "dkg-public-transcript.json",
   424	    )
   425	
   426	
   427	  const transcript =
   428	    JSON.parse(
   429	      await readFile(
   430	        file,
   431	        "utf8",
   432	      ),
   433	    )
   434	
   435	
   436	  const result =
   437	    await storeElectionTrusteeTranscript({
   438	      electionId:
   439	        election.electionId,
   440	
   441	      transcript,
   442	
   443	      adminAddress:
   444	        admin,
   445	    })
   446	
   447	
   448	  console.log(
   449	    "TRUSTEE_TRANSCRIPT_STORE=PASS",
   450	  )
   451	
   452	  console.log(
   453	    `ELECTION_ID=${result.election.electionId}`,
   454	  )
   455	
   456	  console.log(
   457	    `TRANSCRIPT_HASH=${result.transcriptHash}`,
   458	  )
   459	
   460	  console.log(
   461	    `PUBLIC_KEY_X=${result.Yx}`,
   462	  )
   463	
   464	  console.log(
   465	    `PUBLIC_KEY_Y=${result.Yy}`,
   466	  )
   467	}

   469	
   470	async function commandPreflight() {
   471	  const admin =
   472	    await localAdmin()
   473	
   474	  const election =
   475	    await current()
   476	
   477	
   478	  if (!election) {
   479	    throw new Error(
   480	      "No current election exists",
   481	    )
   482	  }
   483	
   484	
   485	  const result =
   486	    await counts()
   487	
   488	
   489	  console.log(
   490	    `ELECTION_ID=${election.electionId}`,
   491	  )
   492	
   493	  console.log(
   494	    `ELECTION_STATUS=${election.status}`,
   495	  )
   496	
   497	  console.log(
   498	    `ELIGIBLE_VOTERS=${result.eligibleVoters}`,
   499	  )
   500	
   501	  console.log(
   502	    `REGISTERED_CANDIDATES=${result.registeredCandidates}`,
   503	  )
   504	
   505	  console.log(
   506	    `TRUSTEE_TRANSCRIPT=${election.trusteeTranscriptHash ? "READY" : "NOT_READY"}`,
   507	  )
   508	
   509	  console.log(
   510	    `ADMIN=${admin}`,
   511	  )
   512	
   513	
   514	  const ready =
   515	    election.status ===
   516	      "registration" &&
   517	    result.eligibleVoters >
   518	      0 &&
   519	    result.registeredCandidates >
   520	      0 &&
   521	    Boolean(
   522	      election
   523	        .trusteeTranscriptHash,
   524	    ) &&
   525	    Boolean(
   526	      election
   527	        .trusteeTranscript,
   528	    )
   529	
   530	
   531	  console.log(
   532	    `START_PREFLIGHT=${ready ? "PASS" : "BLOCKED"}`,
   533	  )
   534	
   535	
   536	  if (!ready) {
   537	    if (
   538	      result.eligibleVoters <
   539	        1
   540	    ) {
   541	      console.log(
   542	        "BLOCK_REASON=NO_ELIGIBLE_VOTERS",
   543	      )
   544	    }
   545	
   546	    if (
   547	      result.registeredCandidates <
   548	        1
   549	    ) {
   550	      console.log(
   551	        "BLOCK_REASON=NO_REGISTERED_CANDIDATES",
   552	      )
   553	    }
   554	
   555	    if (
   556	      !election
   557	        .trusteeTranscriptHash
   558	    ) {
   559	      console.log(
   560	        "BLOCK_REASON=NO_DKG_TRANSCRIPT",
   561	      )
   562	    }
   563	
   564	    process.exitCode =
   565	      20
   566	  }
   567	}
   568	
   569	
   570	async function commandStart() {
   571	  await localAdmin()
   572	
   573	  const election =
   574	    await current()
   575	
   576	
   577	  if (!election) {
   578	    throw new Error(
   579	      "No current election exists",
   580	    )
   581	  }
   582	
   583	
   584	  const result =
   585	    await counts()
   586	
   587	
   588	  if (
   589	    result.eligibleVoters <
   590	      1
   591	  ) {
   592	    throw new Error(
   593	      "Refusing to start: no eligible WP5 voters exist",
   594	    )
   595	  }
   596	
   597	
   598	  if (
   599	    result.registeredCandidates <
   600	      1
   601	  ) {
   602	    throw new Error(
   603	      "Refusing to start: no registered candidates exist",
   604	    )
   605	  }
   606	
   607	
   608	  if (
   609	    !election
   610	      .trusteeTranscriptHash ||
   611	    !election
   612	      .trusteeTranscript
   613	  ) {
   614	    throw new Error(
   615	      "Refusing to start: verified DKG transcript is missing",
   616	    )
   617	  }
   618	
   619	
   620	  const started =
   621	    await startElectionLifecycle({
   622	      electionId:
   623	        election.electionId,
   624	    })
   625	
   626	
   627	  console.log(
   628	    "ELECTION_START=PASS",
   629	  )
   630	
   631	  console.log(
   632	    JSON.stringify(
   633	      started,
   634	      null,
   635	      2,
   636	    ),
   637	  )
   638	}
   639	
   640	
   641	const command =
   642	  process.argv[2]
   643	
   644	
   645	try {
   646	  await connectDatabase()
   647	
   648	  switch (
   649	    command
   650	  ) {
   651	    case "create":
   652	      await commandCreate()
   653	      break
   654	
   655	    case "status":
   656	      await commandStatus()
   657	      break
   658	
   659	    case "store-transcript":
   660	      await commandStoreTranscript()
   661	      break
   662	
   663	    case "preflight":
   664	      await commandPreflight()
   665	      break
   666	
   667	    case "start":
   668	      await commandStart()
   669	      break
   670	
   671	    default:
   672	      throw new Error(
   673	        "Usage: node scripts/local-election-admin.mjs create|status|store-transcript|preflight|start",
   674	      )
   675	  }
   676	} catch (error) {
   677	  console.error(
   678	    "LOCAL_ELECTION_ADMIN_ERROR=" +
   679	    (
   680	      error?.message ||
   681	      String(error)
   682	    ),
   683	  )
   684	
   685	  process.exitCode =
   686	    process.exitCode ||
   687	    1
   688	} finally {
   689	  await mongoose
   690	    .disconnect()
   691	    .catch(
   692	      () => {},
   693	    )
   694	}

============================== END FILE ==============================

     1	import dotenv
     2	  from "dotenv"
     3	
     4	import mongoose
     5	  from "mongoose"
     6	
     7	import Election
     8	  from "../models/Election.js"
     9	
    10	import {
    11	  getActiveWp5Contract,
    12	} from "../utils/wp5Contract.js"
    13	
    14	
    15	dotenv.config()
    16	
    17	
    18	const mongoEntry =
    19	  Object
    20	    .entries(
    21	      process.env,
    22	    )
    23	    .find(
    24	      (
    25	        [
    26	          _key,
    27	          value,
    28	        ],
    29	      ) =>
    30	        /^mongodb(?:\+srv)?:\/\//i
    31	          .test(
    32	            String(
    33	              value ||
    34	              "",
    35	            ),
    36	          ),
    37	    )
    38	
    39	
    40	if (!mongoEntry) {
    41	  throw new Error(
    42	    "MongoDB URI environment variable not found",
    43	  )
    44	}
    45	
    46	
    47	await mongoose.connect(
    48	  mongoEntry[1],
    49	)
    50	
    51	
    52	try {
    53	  const election =
    54	    await Election
    55	      .findOne({
    56	        lifecycleSlot:
    57	          "current",
    58	
    59	        status:
    60	          "active",
    61	      })
    62	
    63	
    64	  if (!election) {
    65	    throw new Error(
    66	      "No active election found",
    67	    )
    68	  }
    69	
    70	
    71	  const {
    72	    contract,
    73	  } =
    74	    await getActiveWp5Contract()
    75	
    76	
    77	  const [
    78	    phase,
    79	    configured,
    80	    electionId,
    81	    eligibleRoot,
    82	    manifest,
    83	    candidateCount,
    84	    transcriptHash,
    85	    acceptedBallotCount,
    86	  ] =
    87	    await Promise.all([
    88	      contract.phase(),
    89	      contract.configured(),
    90	      contract.electionId(),
    91	      contract.eligibleRoot(),
    92	      contract.candidateManifestHash(),
    93	      contract.candidateCount(),
    94	      contract.trusteeTranscriptHash(),
    95	      contract.acceptedBallotCount(),
    96	    ])
    97	
    98	
    99	  const checks = {
   100	    phaseActive:
   101	      Number(
   102	        phase,
   103	      ) === 1,
   104	
   105	    configured:
   106	      Boolean(
   107	        configured,
   108	      ),
   109	
   110	    electionId:
   111	      BigInt(
   112	        electionId,
   113	      ) ===
   114	      BigInt(
   115	        election.electionId,
   116	      ),
   117	
   118	    eligibleRoot:
   119	      BigInt(
   120	        eligibleRoot,
   121	      ) ===
   122	      BigInt(
   123	        election.eligibleRoot,
   124	      ),
   125	
   126	    candidateManifest:
   127	      BigInt(
   128	        manifest,
   129	      ) ===
   130	      BigInt(
   131	        election
   132	          .candidateManifestHash,
   133	      ),
   134	
   135	    candidateCount:
   136	      BigInt(
   137	        candidateCount,
   138	      ) ===
   139	      BigInt(
   140	        election
   141	          .candidateCount,
   142	      ),
   143	
   144	    trusteeTranscript:
   145	      String(
   146	        transcriptHash,
   147	      )
   148	        .toLowerCase() ===
   149	      String(
   150	        election
   151	          .trusteeTranscriptHash,
   152	      )
   153	        .toLowerCase(),
   154	  }
   155	
   156	
   157	  for (
   158	    const [
   159	      name,
   160	      passed,
   161	    ]
   162	    of Object.entries(
   163	      checks,
   164	    )
   165	  ) {
   166	    console.log(
   167	      `${name.toUpperCase()}=${passed ? "PASS" : "FAIL"}`,
   168	    )
   169	  }
   170	
   171	
   172	  console.log(
   173	    `DIRECT_CONTRACT=${election.directContractAddress}`,
   174	  )
   175	
   176	  console.log(
   177	    `ACCEPTED_BALLOTS=${acceptedBallotCount.toString()}`,
   178	  )
   179	
   180	
   181	  if (
   182	    Object
   183	      .values(
   184	        checks,
   185	      )
   186	      .some(
   187	        passed =>
   188	          !passed,
   189	      )
   190	  ) {
   191	    process.exitCode =
   192	      1
   193	  } else {
   194	    console.log(
   195	      "ACTIVE_ELECTION_CHAIN_CHECK=PASS",
   196	    )
   197	  }
   198	} finally {
   199	  await mongoose.disconnect()
   200	}

FILE: votechain-frontend-ts/src/services/adminAuth.ts

     1	import {
     2	  BrowserProvider,
     3	  getAddress,
     4	} from "ethers"
     5	
     6	import {
     7	  apiUrl,
     8	} from "@/config/api"
     9	
    10	import {
    11	  contractAddress,
    12	} from "@/config/contract"
    13	
    14	const ADMIN_AUTH_SCHEME =
    15	  "VoteChainAdmin"
    16	
    17	const ADMIN_AUTH_VERSION =
    18	  "VoteChain-ZK Admin Request v1"
    19	
    20	function normalizeJson(
    21	  value: unknown,
    22	): unknown {
    23	  if (
    24	    Array.isArray(value)
    25	  ) {
    26	    return value.map(
    27	      normalizeJson,
    28	    )
    29	  }
    30	
    31	  if (
    32	    value &&
    33	    typeof value === "object"
    34	  ) {
    35	    const record =
    36	      value as Record<
    37	        string,
    38	        unknown
    39	      >
    40	
    41	    return Object.fromEntries(
    42	      Object
    43	        .keys(record)
    44	        .sort()
    45	        .map(
    46	          key => [
    47	            key,
    48	            normalizeJson(
    49	              record[key],
    50	            ),
    51	          ],
    52	        ),
    53	    )
    54	  }
    55	
    56	  return value
    57	}
    58	
    59	function canonicalJson(
    60	  value: unknown,
    61	) {
    62	  return JSON.stringify(
    63	    normalizeJson(
    64	      value === undefined
    65	        ? null
    66	        : value,
    67	    ),
    68	  )
    69	}
    70	
    71	async function sha256Hex(
    72	  text: string,
    73	) {
    74	  const bytes =
    75	    new TextEncoder()
    76	      .encode(text)
    77	
    78	  const digest =
    79	    await crypto.subtle.digest(
    80	      "SHA-256",
    81	      bytes,
    82	    )
    83	
    84	  return Array.from(
    85	    new Uint8Array(
    86	      digest,
    87	    ),
    88	    byte =>
    89	      byte
    90	        .toString(16)
    91	        .padStart(
    92	          2,
    93	          "0",
    94	        ),
    95	  ).join("")
    96	}
    97	
    98	function randomNonce() {
    99	  const bytes =
   100	    new Uint8Array(16)
   101	
   102	  crypto.getRandomValues(
   103	    bytes,
   104	  )
   105	
   106	  return Array.from(
   107	    bytes,
   108	    byte =>
   109	      byte
   110	        .toString(16)
   111	        .padStart(
   112	          2,
   113	          "0",
   114	        ),
   115	  ).join("")
   116	}
   117	
   118	function bodyValue(
   119	  body:
   120	    | BodyInit
   121	    | null
   122	    | undefined,
   123	) {
   124	  if (
   125	    body === undefined ||
   126	    body === null
   127	  ) {
   128	    return null
   129	  }
   130	
   131	  if (
   132	    typeof body !== "string"
   133	  ) {
   134	    throw new Error(
   135	      "Administrator API mutations must use a JSON string body",
   136	    )
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  if (
   140	    body.length === 0
   141	  ) {
   142	    return null
   143	  }
   144	
   145	  try {
   146	    return JSON.parse(
   147	      body,
   148	    )
   149	  } catch {
   150	    throw new Error(
   151	      "Administrator API mutation body must be valid JSON",
   152	    )
   153	  }
   154	}
   155	
   156	function buildMessage({
   157	  audience,
   158	  address,
   159	  method,
   160	  path,
   161	  timestamp,
   162	  nonce,
   163	  bodyHash,
   164	}: {
   165	  audience: string
   166	  address: string
   167	  method: string
   168	  path: string
   169	  timestamp: string
   170	  nonce: string
   171	  bodyHash: string
   172	}) {
   173	  return [
   174	    ADMIN_AUTH_VERSION,
   175	    `audience:${audience.toLowerCase()}`,
   176	    `address:${address.toLowerCase()}`,
   177	    `method:${method.toUpperCase()}`,
   178	    `path:${path}`,
   179	    `timestamp:${timestamp}`,
   180	    `nonce:${nonce.toLowerCase()}`,
   181	    `body-sha256:${bodyHash.toLowerCase()}`,
   182	  ].join("\n")
   183	}
   184	
   185	/*
   186	 * Sign one privileged REST mutation with the currently connected wallet.
   187	 *
   188	 * The backend independently reads electionCommission() from the stable legacy authority
   189	 * contract, so possessing an arbitrary wallet is insufficient.
   190	 */
   191	export async function adminFetch(
   192	  path: string,
   193	  init: RequestInit = {},
   194	) {
   195	  const injected =
   196	    (
   197	      window as unknown as {
   198	        ethereum?: unknown
   199	      }
   200	    ).ethereum
   201	
   202	  if (!injected) {
   203	    throw new Error(
   204	      "An injected wallet is required for administrator API mutations",
   205	    )
   206	  }
   207	
   208	  if (
   209	    !contractAddress
   210	  ) {
   211	    throw new Error(
   212	      "VITE_CONTRACT_ADDRESS is required for administrator API mutations",
   213	    )
   214	  }
   215	
   216	  const provider =
   217	    new BrowserProvider(
   218	      injected as never,
   219	    )
   220	
   221	  const signer =
   222	    await provider
   223	      .getSigner()
   224	
   225	  const address =
   226	    getAddress(
   227	      await signer
   228	        .getAddress(),
   229	    )
   230	
   231	  const audience =
   232	    getAddress(
   233	      contractAddress,
   234	    )
   235	
   236	  const method =
   237	    (
   238	      init.method ||
   239	      "GET"
   240	    ).toUpperCase()
   241	
   242	  const timestamp =
   243	    String(
   244	      Date.now(),
   245	    )
   246	
   247	  const nonce =
   248	    randomNonce()
   249	
   250	  const bodyHash =
   251	    await sha256Hex(
   252	      canonicalJson(
   253	        bodyValue(
   254	          init.body,
   255	        ),
   256	      ),
   257	    )
   258	
   259	  const message =
   260	    buildMessage({
   261	      audience,
   262	      address,
   263	      method,
   264	      path,
   265	      timestamp,
   266	      nonce,
   267	      bodyHash,
   268	    })
   269	
   270	  const signature =
   271	    await signer
   272	      .signMessage(
   273	        message,
   274	      )
   275	
   276	  const headers =
   277	    new Headers(
   278	      init.headers,
   279	    )
   280	
   281	  headers.set(
   282	    "Authorization",
   283	    `${ADMIN_AUTH_SCHEME} ${address}.${timestamp}.${nonce}.${signature}`,
   284	  )
   285	
   286	  return fetch(
   287	    apiUrl(path),
   288	    {
   289	      ...init,
   290	      method,
   291	      headers,
   292	    },
   293	  )
   294	}

============================== END FILE ==============================

     1	import { apiUrl } from "@/config/api"
     2	import {
     3	  adminFetch,
     4	} from "@/services/adminAuth"
     5	
     6	export type ElectionState = "Registration" | "Active" | "Ended"
     7	
     8	export interface PublicCandidate {
     9	  candidateId: number
    10	  name: string
    11	  party: string
    12	  location: string
    13	  voteCount: number
    14	}
    15	
    16	export interface PublicElectionData {
    17	  electionState: ElectionState
    18	  candidateCount: number
    19	  candidates: PublicCandidate[]
    20	}
    21	
    22	interface ApiEnvelope<T> {
    23	  success: boolean
    24	  data?: T
    25	  message?: string
    26	  errors?: string[]
    27	}
    28	
    29	async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    30	  const method =
    31	    (init?.method || "GET").toUpperCase()
    32	
    33	  const privileged =
    34	    ["POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE"].includes(method) &&
    35	    (
    36	      path.startsWith("/api/candidates") ||
    37	      path.startsWith("/api/voters") ||
    38	      path.startsWith("/api/biometric") ||
    39	      path.startsWith("/api/elections")
    40	    )
    41	
    42	  const response =
    43	    privileged
    44	      ? await adminFetch(path, init)
    45	      : await fetch(apiUrl(path), init)
    46	  const payload = (await response.json()) as ApiEnvelope<T>
    47	
    48	  if (!response.ok || payload.success === false) {
    49	    const detail = payload.errors?.join(", ") || payload.message || `Request failed with ${response.status}`
    50	    throw new Error(detail)
    51	  }
    52	
    53	  if (payload.data === undefined) {
    54	    throw new Error("API response did not contain data")
    55	  }
    56	
    57	  return payload.data
    58	}
    59	
    60	export async function getPublicElectionData() {
    61	  return request<PublicElectionData>("/api/elections/public/election-data")
    62	}

   117	  merkleTreeRoot: string

   130	export async function createVoter(input: CreateVoterInput) {
   131	  const response = await adminFetch("/api/voters", {
   132	    method: "POST",
   133	    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   134	    body: JSON.stringify(input),
   135	  })
   136	  const payload = await response.json()
   137	
   138	  if (!response.ok || !payload.success) throw new Error(payload.message || "Failed to create voter ZK enrollment")
   139	  const identityCommitment = payload.data?.identityCommitment || payload.identityCommitment
   140	  if (!identityCommitment) throw new Error("Identity commitment was not returned from backend")
   141	  return { identityCommitment: String(identityCommitment) } as VoterCreationResult
   142	}
   143	
   144	export async function updateVoterZkRegistrationStatus(
   145	  identityCommitment: string,
   146	  registrationStatus: "registered" | "failed",
   147	  transactionHash?: string,
   148	) {
   149	  const response = await adminFetch("/api/voters/zk-registration-status", {
   150	    method: "PATCH",
   151	    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   152	    body: JSON.stringify({ identityCommitment, registrationStatus, transactionHash }),
   153	  })
   154	  const payload = await response.json()
   155	  if (!response.ok || !payload.success) throw new Error(payload.message || "Failed to reconcile voter commitment registration")
   156	  return payload.data as { identityCommitment: string; registrationStatus: string; groupIndex: number | null }
   157	}
   158	
   159	export async function prepareZkVote(path: string, candidateId: number) {
   160	  const response = await fetch(apiUrl(path), {
   161	    method: "POST",
   162	    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   163	    body: JSON.stringify({ candidateId }),
   164	  })
   165	  const payload = await response.json()
   166	  if (!response.ok || !payload.success) throw new Error(payload.message || "Biometric verification or proof generation failed")
   167	  const data = payload.data || payload
   168	  if (!data?.proof) throw new Error("Anonymous eligibility proof was not returned")
   169	  return data as ZkVotePreparation
   170	}

   241	export async function authorizeWp5(
   242	  path: "/api/wp5/authorize/face" |
   243	        "/api/wp5/authorize/fingerprint",
   244	  credentialPublicKey: JsonWebKey,
   245	) {
   246	  return request<Wp5Authorization>(
   247	    path,
   248	    {
   249	      method: "POST",
   250	      headers: {
   251	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
   252	      },
   253	      body: JSON.stringify({
   254	        credentialPublicKey,
   255	      }),
   256	    },
   257	  )
   258	}

     1	import {
     2	  BrowserProvider,
     3	  Contract,
     4	  getAddress,
     5	} from "ethers"
     6	
     7	import VoteChainDirectAbi
     8	  from "@/abi/VoteChainDirect.json"

    10	import {
    11	  getWp5PublicConfig,
    12	  type Wp5PublicConfig,
    13	} from "@/services/api"
    14	
    15	
    16	function sameBigInt(
    17	  actual: unknown,
    18	  expected: string,
    19	  label: string,
    20	) {
    21	  if (
    22	    BigInt(
    23	      actual as bigint,
    24	    ) !==
    25	    BigInt(
    26	      expected,
    27	    )
    28	  ) {
    29	    throw new Error(
    30	      `Current VoteChainDirect ${label} does not match the frozen election configuration`,
    31	    )
    32	  }
    33	}
    34	
    35	
    36	export async function getCurrentWp5DirectContract(): Promise<{
    37	  contract: Contract
    38	  config: Wp5PublicConfig
    39	}> {
    40	  const injected =
    41	    (
    42	      window as unknown as {
    43	        ethereum?: unknown
    44	      }
    45	    ).ethereum
    46	
    47	
    48	  if (!injected) {
    49	    throw new Error(
    50	      "A government-funded vote-submission wallet is required",
    51	    )
    52	  }
    53	
    54	
    55	  /*
    56	   * The backend current-election record is the authoritative
    57	   * resolver for the per-election Direct deployment.
    58	   */
    59	  const config =
    60	    await getWp5PublicConfig()
    61	
    62	
    63	  if (
    64	    !config ||
    65	    !config.directContractAddress
    66	  ) {
    67	    throw new Error(
    68	      "The active election does not expose a VoteChainDirect address",
    69	    )
    70	  }
    71	
    72	
    73	  if (
    74	    !config.configured ||
    75	    Number(
    76	      config.phase,
    77	    ) !== 1
    78	  ) {
    79	    throw new Error(
    80	      "The current election is not active for private voting",
    81	    )
    82	  }
    83	
    84	
    85	  let directAddress: string
    86	
    87	  try {
    88	    directAddress =
    89	      getAddress(
    90	        config
    91	          .directContractAddress,
    92	      )
    93	  } catch {
    94	    throw new Error(
    95	      "The active election returned an invalid VoteChainDirect address",
    96	    )
    97	  }
    98	
    99	
   100	  const provider =
   101	    new BrowserProvider(
   102	      injected as never,
   103	    )
   104	
   105	
   106	  const code =
   107	    await provider.getCode(
   108	      directAddress,
   109	    )
   110	
   111	
   112	  if (
   113	    !code ||
   114	    code === "0x"
   115	  ) {
   116	    throw new Error(
   117	      "VoteChainDirect is not deployed on the wallet's current network. Check the selected blockchain network.",
   118	    )
   119	  }
   120	
   121	
   122	  const signer =
   123	    await provider.getSigner()
   124	
   125	
   126	  const contract =
   127	    new Contract(
   128	      directAddress,
   129	      VoteChainDirectAbi,
   130	      signer,
   131	    )
   132	
   133	
   134	  const [
   135	    configured,
   136	    phase,
   137	    electionId,
   138	    eligibleRoot,
   139	    candidateManifestHash,
   140	    candidateCount,
   141	    contractDomain,
   142	    Yx,
   143	    Yy,
   144	    electionContextHash,
   145	    admin,
   146	  ] =
   147	    await Promise.all([
   148	      contract.configured(),
   149	      contract.phase(),
   150	      contract.electionId(),
   151	      contract.eligibleRoot(),
   152	      contract.candidateManifestHash(),
   153	      contract.candidateCount(),
   154	      contract.contractDomain(),
   155	      contract.Yx(),
   156	      contract.Yy(),
   157	      contract.electionContextHash(),
   158	      contract.admin(),
   159	    ])
   160	
   161	
   162	  if (
   163	    !configured ||
   164	    Number(
   165	      phase,
   166	    ) !== 1
   167	  ) {
   168	    throw new Error(
   169	      "The resolved VoteChainDirect contract is not active",
   170	    )
   171	  }
   172	
   173	
   174	  sameBigInt(
   175	    electionId,
   176	    config.config.electionId,
   177	    "electionId",
   178	  )
   179	
   180	  sameBigInt(
   181	    eligibleRoot,
   182	    config.config.eligibleRoot,
   183	    "eligible root",
   184	  )
   185	
   186	  sameBigInt(
   187	    candidateManifestHash,
   188	    config
   189	      .config
   190	      .candidateManifestHash,
   191	    "candidate manifest",
   192	  )
   193	
   194	  sameBigInt(
   195	    candidateCount,
   196	    config.config.candidateCount,
   197	    "candidate count",
   198	  )
   199	
   200	  sameBigInt(
   201	    contractDomain,
   202	    config.config.contractDomain,
   203	    "contract domain",
   204	  )
   205	
   206	  sameBigInt(
   207	    Yx,
   208	    config.config.Yx,
   209	    "election public-key X",
   210	  )
   211	
   212	  sameBigInt(
   213	    Yy,
   214	    config.config.Yy,
   215	    "election public-key Y",
   216	  )
   217	
   218	  sameBigInt(
   219	    electionContextHash,
   220	    config
   221	      .config
   222	      .electionContextHash,
   223	    "election context",
   224	  )
   225	
   226	
   227	  /*
   228	   * The Election Commission/admin account is not the ordinary
   229	   * government-funded vote-submission account.
   230	   */
   231	  const signerAddress =
   232	    getAddress(
   233	      await signer.getAddress(),
   234	    )
   235	
   236	
   237	  const adminAddress =
   238	    getAddress(
   239	      String(
   240	        admin,
   241	      ),
   242	    )
   243	
   244	
   245	  if (
   246	    signerAddress ===
   247	      adminAddress
   248	  ) {
   249	    throw new Error(
   250	      "Connect a non-admin government-funded vote-submission wallet before casting a vote",
   251	    )
   252	  }
   253	
   254	
   255	  return {
   256	    contract,
   257	    config,
   258	  }
   259	}

     1	import {

    11	  ethers,
    12	} from "ethers"

    14	  createContract,

    18	  useWallet,

    27	const ELECTION_STATES:
    28	  Record<
    29	    number,
    30	    ElectionState
    31	  > = {
    32	    0: "Registration",
    33	    1: "Active",
    34	    2: "Ended",
    35	  }
    36	
    37	function toElectionState(
    38	  value:
    39	    bigint |
    40	    number,
    41	): ElectionState {
    42	  const state =
    43	    ELECTION_STATES[
    44	      Number(value)
    45	    ]
    46	
    47	  if (!state) {
    48	    throw new Error(
    49	      `Unknown election state: ${value.toString()}`,
    50	    )
    51	  }
    52	
    53	  return state
    54	}

    56	export function useContract() {
    57	  /*
    58	   * Inherited contract stays available to existing
    59	   * registration/admin screens during migration.
    60	   */
    61	  const [
    62	    contract,
    63	    setContract,
    64	  ] =
    65	    useState<ethers.Contract | null>(
    66	      null,
    67	    )
    68	
    69	  const [
    70	    isLoading,
    71	    setIsLoading,
    72	  ] =
    73	    useState(false)
    74	
    75	  const [
    76	    error,
    77	    setError,
    78	  ] =
    79	    useState<Error | null>(
    80	      null,
    81	    )
    82	
    83	  const [
    84	    isInitialized,
    85	    setIsInitialized,
    86	  ] =
    87	    useState(false)
    88	
    89	  const [
    90	    isAdmin,
    91	    setIsAdmin,
    92	  ] =
    93	    useState(false)
    94	
    95	  /*
    96	   * WP5 DIRECT contract is a separate active voter path.
    97	   */
    98	  const [
    99	    wp5Contract,
   100	    setWp5Contract,
   101	  ] =
   102	    useState<ethers.Contract | null>(
   103	      null,
   104	    )
   105	
   106	  const [
   107	    wp5Loading,
   108	    setWp5Loading,
   109	  ] =
   110	    useState(false)
   111	
   112	  const [
   113	    wp5Error,
   114	    setWp5Error,
   115	  ] =
   116	    useState<Error | null>(
   117	      null,
   118	    )
   119	
   120	  const [
   121	    isWp5Initialized,
   122	    setIsWp5Initialized,
   123	  ] =
   124	    useState(false)
   125	
   126	  const [
   127	    isWp5Admin,
   128	    setIsWp5Admin,
   129	  ] =
   130	    useState(false)
   131	
   132	  const {
   133	    isConnected,
   134	    signer,
   135	    address,
   136	  } =
   137	    useWallet()
   138	
   139	  const lastAddress =
   140	    useRef<string | null>(
   141	      null,
   142	    )
   143	
   144	  const lastWp5Address =
   145	    useRef<string | null>(
   146	      null,
   147	    )
   148	
   149	  useEffect(() => {
   150	    if (
   151	      !isConnected ||
   152	      !address ||
   153	      !signer
   154	    ) {
   155	      setContract(null)
   156	      setIsInitialized(false)
   157	      setIsAdmin(false)
   158	      setIsLoading(false)
   159	      lastAddress.current =
   160	        null
   161	      return
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    if (
   165	      lastAddress.current ===
   166	      address
   167	    ) {
   168	      return
   169	    }
   170	
   171	    lastAddress.current =
   172	      address
   173	
   174	    setIsLoading(true)
   175	    setError(null)
   176	
   177	    const initializeContract =
   178	      async () => {
   179	        try {
   180	          const instance =
   181	            createContract(
   182	              signer,
   183	            )
   184	
   185	          const commission =
   186	            await instance
   187	              .electionCommission()
   188	
   189	          setContract(
   190	            instance,
   191	          )
   192	
   193	          setIsInitialized(
   194	            commission !==
   195	              ethers.ZeroAddress,
   196	          )
   197	
   198	          setIsAdmin(
   199	            address.toLowerCase() ===
   200	              String(
   201	                commission,
   202	              ).toLowerCase(),
   203	          )
   204	        } catch (
   205	          initializationError
   206	        ) {
   207	          setError(
   208	            
   209	        initializationError as Error,
   210	      
   211	          )
   212	
   213	          setContract(null)
   214	          setIsInitialized(false)
   215	          setIsAdmin(false)
   216	        } finally {
   217	          setIsLoading(false)
   218	        }
   219	      }
   220	
   221	    void initializeContract()
   222	  }, [
   223	    isConnected,
   224	    signer,
   225	    address,
   226	  ])
   227	
   228	  useEffect(() => {
   229	    if (
   230	      !isConnected ||
   231	      !address ||
   232	      !signer
   233	    ) {
   234	      setWp5Contract(null)
   235	      setIsWp5Initialized(false)
   236	      setIsWp5Admin(false)
   237	      setWp5Loading(false)
   238	      lastWp5Address.current =
   239	        null
   240	      return
   241	    }
   242	
   243	    if (
   244	      lastWp5Address.current ===
   245	      address
   246	    ) {
   247	      return
   248	    }
   249	
   250	    lastWp5Address.current =
   251	      address
   252	
   253	    setWp5Loading(true)
   254	    setWp5Error(null)
   255	
   256	    const initializeWp5 =
   257	      async () => {
   258	        try {
   259	          const instance =
   260	            createWp5Contract(
   261	              signer,
   262	            )
   263	
   264	          const [
   265	            admin,
   266	            configured,
   267	          ] =
   268	            await Promise.all([
   269	              instance.admin(),
   270	              instance.configured(),
   271	            ])
   272	
   273	          setWp5Contract(
   274	            instance,
   275	          )
   276	
   277	          setIsWp5Initialized(
   278	            Boolean(
   279	              configured,
   280	            ),
   281	          )
   282	
   283	          setIsWp5Admin(
   284	            address.toLowerCase() ===
   285	              String(
   286	                admin,
   287	              ).toLowerCase(),
   288	          )
   289	        } catch (
   290	          initializationError
   291	        ) {
   292	          setWp5Error(
   293	            
   294	        initializationError as Error,
   295	      
   296	          )
   297	
   298	          setWp5Contract(null)
   299	          setIsWp5Initialized(false)
   300	          setIsWp5Admin(false)
   301	        } finally {
   302	          setWp5Loading(false)
   303	        }
   304	      }
   305	
   306	    void initializeWp5()
   307	  }, [
   308	    isConnected,
   309	    signer,
   310	    address,
   311	  ])
   312	
   313	  const requireContract =
   314	    useCallback(
   315	      () => {
   316	        if (!contract) {
   317	          throw new Error(
   318	            "Legacy contract not initialized",
   319	          )
   320	        }
   321	
   322	        return contract
   323	      },
   324	      [contract],
   325	    )
   326	
   327	  const registerCandidate =
   328	    useCallback(
   329	      async (
   330	        candidateId:
   331	          number,
   332	      ) => {
   333	        const instance =
   334	          requireContract()
   335	
   336	        const tx =
   337	          await instance
   338	            .registerCandidate(
   339	              candidateId,
   340	            )
   341	
   342	        const receipt =
   343	          await tx.wait()
   344	
   345	        if (
   346	          receipt?.status ===
   347	          0
   348	        ) {
   349	          throw new Error(
   350	            "Candidate registration transaction failed",
   351	          )
   352	        }
   353	
   354	        return receipt
   355	      },
   356	      [
   357	        requireContract,
   358	      ],
   359	    )
   360	
   361	  const registerVoterCommitment =
   362	    useCallback(
   363	      async (
   364	        identityCommitment:
   365	          string,
   366	      ) => {
   367	        if (
   368	          !/^\d+$/.test(
   369	            identityCommitment,
   370	          )
   371	        ) {
   372	          throw new Error(
   373	            "Invalid Semaphore identity commitment",
   374	          )
   375	        }
   376	
   377	        const instance =
   378	          requireContract()
   379	
   380	        const tx =
   381	          await instance
   382	            .registerVoterCommitment(
   383	              BigInt(
   384	                identityCommitment,
   385	              ),
   386	            )
   387	
   388	        const receipt =
   389	          await tx.wait()
   390	
   391	        if (
   392	          receipt?.status ===
   393	          0
   394	        ) {
   395	          throw new Error(
   396	            "Voter commitment registration transaction failed",
   397	          )
   398	        }
   399	
   400	        return receipt
   401	      },
   402	      [
   403	        requireContract,
   404	      ],
   405	    )
   406	
   407	  const castEncryptedVote =
   408	    useCallback(
   409	      async (
   410	        ballot:
   411	          Wp5EncryptedBallot,
   412	      ) => {
   413	        if (
   414	          ballot.rx.length !==
   415	            16 ||
   416	          ballot.ry.length !==
   417	            16 ||
   418	          ballot.sx.length !==
   419	            16 ||
   420	          ballot.sy.length !==
   421	            16
   422	        ) {
   423	          throw new Error(
   424	            "Invalid encrypted ballot shape",
   425	          )
   426	        }
   427	
   428	        if (
   429	          !/^\d+$/.test(
   430	            ballot.nullifier,
   431	          )
   432	        ) {
   433	          throw new Error(
   434	            "Invalid WP5 nullifier",
   435	          )
   436	        }
   437	
   438	        const {
   439	          contract:
   440	            instance,
   441	        } =
   442	          await getCurrentWp5DirectContract()
   443	
   444	        const nullifier =
   445	          BigInt(ballot.nullifier)
   446	
   447	        /*
   448	         * Read the contract's current spent-nullifier state first.
   449	         * This gives the voter a clean response without waiting for
   450	         * MetaMask/estimateGas to surface the raw Solidity revert.
   451	         *
   452	         * The contract check below is still authoritative, so this
   453	         * preflight does not weaken double-vote protection.
   454	         */
   455	        const alreadyUsed =
   456	          await instance.usedNullifier(
   457	            nullifier,
   458	          )
   459	
   460	        if (alreadyUsed) {
   461	          throw new Error(
   462	            "Vote already recorded. This voter has already voted in the current election.",
   463	          )
   464	        }
   465	
   466	        try {
   467	          const tx =
   468	            await instance.castVote(
   469	              ballot.a,
   470	              ballot.b,
   471	              ballot.c,
   472	              nullifier,
   473	              ballot.rx,
   474	              ballot.ry,
   475	              ballot.sx,
   476	              ballot.sy,
   477	            )
   478	
   479	          const receipt =
   480	            await tx.wait()
   481	
   482	          if (
   483	            receipt?.status ===
   484	            0
   485	          ) {
   486	            throw new Error(
   487	              "Encrypted vote transaction failed",
   488	            )
   489	          }
   490	
   491	          return receipt
   492	        } catch (transactionError) {
   493	          /*
   494	           * Keep the contract authoritative. A nullifier could become
   495	           * spent between the preflight read and transaction execution,
   496	           * so normalize the Solidity revert here as well.
   497	           */
   498	          const message =
   499	            transactionError instanceof Error
   500	              ? transactionError.message
   501	              : String(transactionError)
   502	
   503	          if (
   504	            message.includes(
   505	              "NULLIFIER_USED",
   506	            )
   507	          ) {
   508	            throw new Error(
   509	              "Vote already recorded. This voter has already voted in the current election.",
   510	            )
   511	          }
   512	
   513	          if (
   514	            message.includes(
   515	              "NOT_ACTIVE",
   516	            )
   517	          ) {
   518	            throw new Error(
   519	              "Voting is no longer active for this election.",
   520	            )
   521	          }
   522	
   523	          throw transactionError
   524	        }
   525	      },
   526	      [
   527	      ],
   528	    )
   529	
   530	  const startElection =
   531	    useCallback(
   532	      async () => {
   533	        const instance =
   534	          requireContract()
   535	
   536	        const tx =
   537	          await instance
   538	            .startElection()
   539	
   540	        return tx.wait()
   541	      },
   542	      [
   543	        requireContract,
   544	      ],
   545	    )
   546	
   547	  const endElection =
   548	    useCallback(
   549	      async () => {
   550	        const instance =
   551	          requireContract()
   552	
   553	        const tx =
   554	          await instance
   555	            .endElection()
   556	
   557	        return tx.wait()
   558	      },
   559	      [
   560	        requireContract,
   561	      ],
   562	    )
   563	
   564	  const getElectionState =
   565	    useCallback(
   566	      async () => {
   567	        const instance =
   568	          requireContract()
   569	
   570	        return toElectionState(
   571	          await instance
   572	            .getElectionState(),
   573	        )
   574	      },
   575	      [
   576	        requireContract,
   577	      ],
   578	    )
   579	
   580	  const getVoteCount =
   581	    useCallback(
   582	      async (
   583	        candidateId:
   584	          number,
   585	      ) => {
   586	        const instance =
   587	          requireContract()
   588	
   589	        return Number(
   590	          await instance
   591	            .getVoteCount(
   592	              candidateId,
   593	            ),
   594	        )
   595	      },
   596	      [
   597	        requireContract,
   598	      ],
   599	    )
   600	
   601	  return {
   602	    /*
   603	     * Legacy/admin surface.
   604	     */
   605	    contract,
   606	    isLoading,
   607	    error,
   608	    isInitialized,
   609	    isAdmin,
   610	    registerCandidate,
   611	    registerVoterCommitment,
   612	    startElection,
   613	    endElection,
   614	    getElectionState,
   615	    getVoteCount,
   616	
   617	    /*
   618	     * WP5 private-voting surface.
   619	     */
   620	    wp5Contract,
   621	    wp5Loading,
   622	    wp5Error,
   623	    isWp5Initialized,
   624	    isWp5Admin,
   625	    castEncryptedVote,
   626	  }
   627	}

FILE: votechain-frontend-ts/src/config/contract.ts

     1	import {
     2	  Contract,
     3	  JsonRpcProvider,
     4	  type ContractRunner,
     5	  type Signer,
     6	} from "ethers"
     7	import zkLocalVotingAbi from "@/abi/ZKLocalVoting.json"
     8	import voteChainDirectAbi from "@/abi/VoteChainDirect.json"
     9	
    10	export const contractAddress =
    11	  (import.meta.env.VITE_CONTRACT_ADDRESS || "").trim()
    12	
    13	export const wp5ContractAddress =
    14	  (import.meta.env.VITE_WP5_CONTRACT_ADDRESS || "").trim()
    15	
    16	export const rpcUrl =
    17	  (import.meta.env.VITE_RPC_URL || "").trim()
    18	
    19	export const contractABI =
    20	  zkLocalVotingAbi
    21	
    22	export const wp5ContractABI =
    23	  voteChainDirectAbi
    24	
    25	function requireContractAddress() {
    26	  if (!contractAddress) {
    27	    throw new Error(
    28	      "VITE_CONTRACT_ADDRESS is not configured",
    29	    )
    30	  }
    31	
    32	  return contractAddress
    33	}
    34	
    35	function requireWp5ContractAddress() {
    36	  if (!wp5ContractAddress) {
    37	    throw new Error(
    38	      "VITE_WP5_CONTRACT_ADDRESS is not configured",
    39	    )
    40	  }
    41	
    42	  return wp5ContractAddress
    43	}
    44	
    45	export function createContract(
    46	  runner: ContractRunner | Signer,
    47	) {
    48	  return new Contract(
    49	    requireContractAddress(),
    50	    contractABI,
    51	    runner,
    52	  )
    53	}
    54	
    55	export function createWp5Contract(
    56	  runner: ContractRunner | Signer,
    57	) {
    58	  return new Contract(
    59	    requireWp5ContractAddress(),
    60	    wp5ContractABI,
    61	    runner,
    62	  )
    63	}
    64	
    65	export function getProvider() {
    66	  if (!rpcUrl) {
    67	    throw new Error(
    68	      "VITE_RPC_URL is not configured",
    69	    )
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  return new JsonRpcProvider(
    73	    rpcUrl,
    74	  )
    75	}

============================== END FILE ==============================
