FILE: votechain-frontend-ts/src/components/Admin/ElectionStatus.tsx
======================================================================
     1	import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react"
     2	import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card"
     3	import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
     4	import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
     5	import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast"
     6	import { useContract } from "@/hooks/useContract"
     7	import { Loader2, AlertCircle, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react"
     8	import { CandidateList } from "@/components/Dashboard/CandidateList"
     9	import { getPublicElectionData, type ElectionState, type PublicElectionData } from "@/services/api"
    10	
    11	export default function ElectionStatus() {
    12	  const { toast } = useToast()
    13	  const { getElectionState, startElection, endElection, isAdmin, isInitialized, isLoading: contractLoading } = useContract()
    14	  const [state, setState] = useState<ElectionState | null>(null)
    15	  const [publicData, setPublicData] = useState<PublicElectionData | null>(null)
    16	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    17	  const [actionLoading, setActionLoading] = useState(false)
    18	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
    19	
    20	  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    21	    if (!isInitialized) return
    22	    setLoading(true)
    23	    setError(null)
    24	    try {
    25	      const [contractState, electionData] = await Promise.all([getElectionState(), getPublicElectionData()])
    26	      setState(contractState)
    27	      setPublicData(electionData)
    28	    } catch (refreshError) {
    29	      setError(refreshError instanceof Error ? refreshError.message : "Failed to fetch election status")
    30	    } finally {
    31	      setLoading(false)
    32	    }
    33	  }, [isInitialized, getElectionState])
    34	
    35	  useEffect(() => {
    36	    void refresh()
    37	  }, [refresh])
    38	
    39	  const runLifecycleAction = async (action: "start" | "end") => {
    40	    setActionLoading(true)
    41	    try {
    42	      if (action === "start") await startElection()
    43	      else await endElection()
    44	      toast({ title: "Success", description: action === "start" ? "Election is now Active." : "Election has ended." })
    45	      await refresh()
    46	    } catch (actionError) {
    47	      toast({ title: "Transaction failed", description: actionError instanceof Error ? actionError.message : "Lifecycle transaction failed", variant: "destructive" })
    48	    } finally {
    49	      setActionLoading(false)
    50	    }
    51	  }
    52	
    53	  if (!isInitialized || loading || contractLoading) {
    54	    return <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto py-8"><Card><CardHeader><CardTitle>Election Status</CardTitle></CardHeader><CardContent className="flex items-center justify-center py-12"><Loader2 className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin" /></CardContent></Card></div>
    55	  }
    56	
    57	  if (error || !state || !publicData) {
    58	    return <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto py-8"><Card><CardHeader><CardTitle>Election Status</CardTitle></CardHeader><CardContent className="text-center py-12"><AlertCircle className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto text-destructive" /><p className="text-destructive mt-3">{error || "Election status unavailable"}</p><Button onClick={refresh} variant="outline" className="mt-4"><RefreshCw className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />Try Again</Button></CardContent></Card></div>
    59	  }
    60	
    61	  return (
    62	    <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto py-8 space-y-6">
    63	      <Card>
    64	        <CardHeader><CardTitle>Election Lifecycle</CardTitle></CardHeader>
    65	        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
    66	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><span className="font-medium">Current state:</span><Badge variant={state === "Active" ? "default" : "secondary"}>{state}</Badge></div>
    67	          {isAdmin && state === "Registration" && <Button className="w-full" onClick={() => runLifecycleAction("start")} disabled={actionLoading}>{actionLoading ? "Starting..." : "Start Election"}</Button>}
    68	          {isAdmin && state === "Active" && <Button className="w-full" variant="destructive" onClick={() => runLifecycleAction("end")} disabled={actionLoading}>{actionLoading ? "Ending..." : "End Election"}</Button>}
    69	          {state === "Ended" && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">The election is final. Registration, start, end, and voting actions are disabled.</p>}
    70	        </CardContent>
    71	      </Card>
    72	      <CandidateList candidates={publicData.candidates} isLoading={false} />
    73	    </div>
    74	  )
    75	}

FILE: votechain-frontend-ts/src/components/Admin/RegisterCandidate.tsx
======================================================================
     1	import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
     2	import { useContract } from "@/hooks/useContract"
     3	import { useWallet } from "@/components/ui/wallet-provider"
     4	import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card"
     5	import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
     6	import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom"
     7	import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
     8	import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label"
     9	import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast"
    10	import {
    11	  createCandidateMetadata,
    12	  updateCandidateRegistrationStatus,
    13	  type ElectionState,
    14	} from "@/services/api"
    15	import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert"
    16	
    17	interface FormData {
    18	  name: string
    19	  party: string
    20	  nationalId: string
    21	  age: number
    22	  location: string
    23	}
    24	
    25	export function RegisterCandidate() {
    26	  const navigate = useNavigate()
    27	  const { registerCandidate, getElectionState, isAdmin, isInitialized } = useContract()
    28	  const { address } = useWallet()
    29	  const { toast } = useToast()
    30	  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<FormData>({ name: "", party: "", nationalId: "", age: 18, location: "" })
    31	  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false)
    32	  const [electionState, setElectionState] = useState<ElectionState | null>(null)
    33	
    34	  useEffect(() => {
    35	    if (isInitialized) {
    36	      void getElectionState().then(setElectionState).catch(() => setElectionState(null))
    37	    }
    38	  }, [isInitialized, getElectionState])
    39	
    40	  const handleSubmit = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    41	    event.preventDefault()
    42	    if (!address || !isAdmin) {
    43	      toast({ title: "Error", description: "Only the Election Commission wallet can register candidates", variant: "destructive" })
    44	      return
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    const currentState = await getElectionState()
    48	    setElectionState(currentState)
    49	    if (currentState !== "Registration") {
    50	      toast({ title: "Registration closed", description: `Candidate registration is unavailable while the election is ${currentState}.`, variant: "destructive" })
    51	      return
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    setIsLoading(true)
    55	    let candidateId: number | null = null
    56	    let chainRegistered = false
    57	
    58	    try {
    59	      const candidate = await createCandidateMetadata(formData)
    60	      candidateId = candidate.candidateId
    61	
    62	      try {
    63	        await registerCandidate(candidate.candidateId)
    64	        chainRegistered = true
    65	      } catch (chainError) {
    66	        try {
    67	          await updateCandidateRegistrationStatus(candidate.candidateId, "failed")
    68	        } catch (statusError) {
    69	          console.error("Failed to mark candidate registration as failed", statusError)
    70	        }
    71	        throw chainError
    72	      }
    73	
    74	      await updateCandidateRegistrationStatus(candidate.candidateId, "registered")
    75	      toast({ title: "Success", description: `Candidate #${candidate.candidateId} registered on-chain successfully.` })
    76	      navigate("/admin")
    77	    } catch (registrationError) {
    78	      console.error("Candidate registration error", registrationError)
    79	      const message = chainRegistered && candidateId
    80	        ? `Candidate #${candidateId} exists on-chain, but the database status could not be synchronized. Reconciliation is required.`
    81	        : registrationError instanceof Error ? registrationError.message : "Failed to register candidate"
    82	      toast({ title: "Registration failed", description: message, variant: "destructive" })
    83	    } finally {
    84	      setIsLoading(false)
    85	    }
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  const registrationOpen = electionState === null || electionState === "Registration"
    89	
    90	  return (
    91	    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
    92	      <Card>
    93	        <CardHeader><CardTitle>Register Candidate</CardTitle></CardHeader>
    94	        <CardContent>
    95	          {electionState && electionState !== "Registration" && (
    96	            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mb-6"><AlertDescription>Registration is closed. Current state: {electionState}.</AlertDescription></Alert>
    97	          )}
    98	          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
    99	            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
   100	              <div className="space-y-2"><Label htmlFor="name">Full Name</Label><Input id="name" value={formData.name} onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, name: event.target.value })} required pattern="^[A-Za-z ]{3,50}$" /></div>
   101	              <div className="space-y-2"><Label htmlFor="party">Party</Label><Input id="party" value={formData.party} onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, party: event.target.value })} required pattern="^[A-Za-z ]{2,40}$" /></div>
   102	              <div className="space-y-2"><Label htmlFor="nationalId">National ID</Label><Input id="nationalId" value={formData.nationalId} onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, nationalId: event.target.value.trim() })} required pattern="^\d{10}$" title="National ID must be exactly 10 digits" /></div>
   103	              <div className="space-y-2"><Label htmlFor="age">Age</Label><Input id="age" type="number" value={formData.age} onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, age: Number(event.target.value) })} required min={18} max={120} /></div>
   104	              <div className="space-y-2"><Label htmlFor="location">Location</Label><Input id="location" value={formData.location} onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, location: event.target.value })} required pattern="^[A-Za-z ,.-]{2,100}$" /></div>
   105	            </div>
   106	            <Button type="submit" disabled={isLoading || !registrationOpen || !isAdmin} className="w-full">
   107	              {isLoading ? "Registering..." : "Register Candidate"}
   108	            </Button>
   109	          </form>
   110	        </CardContent>
   111	      </Card>
   112	    </div>
   113	  )
   114	}

FILE: votechain-frontend-ts/src/components/Admin/RegisterVoter.tsx
======================================================================
     1	import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
     2	import { useContract } from "@/hooks/useContract"
     3	import { useWallet } from "@/components/ui/wallet-provider"
     4	import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card"
     5	import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
     6	import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom"
     7	import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
     8	import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label"
     9	import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast"
    10	import { FaceCapture } from "@/components/Biometric/face-recognition/FaceCapture"
    11	import { FingerprintCapture } from "@/components/Biometric/fingerprint/FingerprintCapture"
    12	import { Calendar } from "@/components/ui/calendar"
    13	import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from "@/components/ui/popover"
    14	import { format } from "date-fns"
    15	import { CalendarIcon } from "lucide-react"
    16	import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
    17	import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert"
    18	import { createVoter, updateVoterZkRegistrationStatus, type ElectionState } from "@/services/api"
    19	import { apiUrl } from "@/config/api"
    20	
    21	interface FormData {
    22	  name: string
    23	  nationalId: string
    24	  dateOfBirth: Date
    25	  location: string
    26	}
    27	
    28	export function RegisterVoter() {
    29	  const navigate = useNavigate()
    30	  const { registerVoterCommitment, getElectionState, isAdmin, isInitialized } = useContract()
    31	  const { address } = useWallet()
    32	  const { toast } = useToast()
    33	  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<FormData>({
    34	    name: "",
    35	    nationalId: "",
    36	    dateOfBirth: new Date(2000, 0, 1),
    37	    location: "",
    38	  })
    39	  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false)
    40	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
    41	  const [electionState, setElectionState] = useState<ElectionState | null>(null)
    42	  const [biometricStatus, setBiometricStatus] = useState({
    43	    faceCaptured: false,
    44	    fingerprintCaptured: false,
    45	    faceTrainingProgress: 0,
    46	    isTraining: false,
    47	  })
    48	
    49	  useEffect(() => {
    50	    if (isInitialized) {
    51	      void getElectionState().then(setElectionState).catch(() => setElectionState(null))
    52	    }
    53	  }, [isInitialized, getElectionState])
    54	
    55	  const handleTrainFace = async () => {
    56	    if (!formData.nationalId.trim() || !biometricStatus.faceCaptured) {
    57	      toast({ title: "Error", description: "Enter the National ID and capture face images first", variant: "destructive" })
    58	      return
    59	    }
    60	
    61	    setBiometricStatus((previous) => ({ ...previous, isTraining: true, faceTrainingProgress: 0 }))
    62	    try {
    63	      const response = await fetch(apiUrl(`/api/voters/train-face/${formData.nationalId}`), {
    64	        method: "POST",
    65	        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    66	      })
    67	      const payload = await response.json()
    68	      if (!response.ok || !payload.success) throw new Error(payload.message || "Failed to train face model")
    69	      setBiometricStatus((previous) => ({ ...previous, faceTrainingProgress: 100, isTraining: false }))
    70	      toast({ title: "Success", description: "Face training completed successfully" })
    71	    } catch (trainingError) {
    72	      setBiometricStatus((previous) => ({ ...previous, isTraining: false, faceTrainingProgress: 0 }))
    73	      toast({ title: "Error", description: trainingError instanceof Error ? trainingError.message : "Failed to train face model", variant: "destructive" })
    74	    }
    75	  }
    76	
    77	  const handleSubmit = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    78	    event.preventDefault()
    79	    setError(null)
    80	
    81	    if (!address || !isAdmin) {
    82	      toast({ title: "Error", description: "Only the Election Commission wallet can register voters", variant: "destructive" })
    83	      return
    84	    }
    85	    if (!biometricStatus.faceCaptured) {
    86	      toast({ title: "Error", description: "Please capture face biometric data", variant: "destructive" })
    87	      return
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    const currentState = await getElectionState()
    91	    setElectionState(currentState)
    92	    if (currentState !== "Registration") {
    93	      toast({ title: "Registration closed", description: `Voter registration is unavailable while the election is ${currentState}.`, variant: "destructive" })
    94	      return
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    const today = new Date()
    98	    let age = today.getFullYear() - formData.dateOfBirth.getFullYear()
    99	    const monthDifference = today.getMonth() - formData.dateOfBirth.getMonth()
   100	    if (monthDifference < 0 || (monthDifference === 0 && today.getDate() < formData.dateOfBirth.getDate())) age -= 1
   101	    if (age < 18 || age > 120) {
   102	      toast({ title: "Error", description: "Voter must be between 18 and 120 years old", variant: "destructive" })
   103	      return
   104	    }
   105	
   106	    setIsLoading(true)
   107	    try {
   108	      const { identityCommitment } = await createVoter({
   109	        name: formData.name,
   110	        nationalId: formData.nationalId,
   111	        dateOfBirth: formData.dateOfBirth.toISOString(),
   112	        location: formData.location,
   113	      })
   114	
   115	      try {
   116	        const receipt = await registerVoterCommitment(identityCommitment)
   117	        await updateVoterZkRegistrationStatus(identityCommitment, "registered", receipt?.hash)
   118	      } catch (chainError) {
   119	        await updateVoterZkRegistrationStatus(identityCommitment, "failed").catch(() => undefined)
   120	        throw chainError
   121	      }
   122	
   123	      toast({ title: "Success", description: "Voter Semaphore commitment registered on-chain successfully" })
   124	      navigate("/admin")
   125	    } catch (registrationError) {
   126	      const message = registrationError instanceof Error ? registrationError.message : "Failed to register voter"
   127	      setError(message)
   128	      toast({ title: "Error", description: message, variant: "destructive" })
   129	    } finally {
   130	      setIsLoading(false)
   131	    }
   132	  }
   133	
   134	  const registrationOpen = electionState === null || electionState === "Registration"
   135	
   136	  return (
   137	    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
   138	      <Card>
   139	        <CardHeader><CardTitle>Register Voter</CardTitle></CardHeader>
   140	        <CardContent>
   141	          {electionState && electionState !== "Registration" && (
   142	            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mb-6"><AlertDescription>Registration is closed. Current state: {electionState}.</AlertDescription></Alert>
   143	          )}
   144	          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
   145	            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
   146	              <div className="space-y-2"><Label htmlFor="name">Full Name</Label><Input id="name" value={formData.name} onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, name: event.target.value })} required pattern="^[A-Za-z ]{3,50}$" /></div>
   147	              <div className="space-y-2"><Label htmlFor="nationalId">National ID</Label><Input id="nationalId" value={formData.nationalId} onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, nationalId: event.target.value.trim() })} required pattern="^\d{10}$" /></div>
   148	              <div className="space-y-2">
   149	                <Label>Date of Birth</Label>
   150	                <Popover>
   151	                  <PopoverTrigger asChild>
   152	                    <Button variant="outline" className={cn("w-full justify-start text-left font-normal")} type="button">
   153	                      <CalendarIcon className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />{format(formData.dateOfBirth, "PPP")}
   154	                    </Button>
   155	                  </PopoverTrigger>
   156	                  <PopoverContent className="w-auto p-0">
   157	                    <Calendar mode="single" selected={formData.dateOfBirth} onSelect={(date) => date && setFormData({ ...formData, dateOfBirth: date })} initialFocus />
   158	                  </PopoverContent>
   159	                </Popover>
   160	              </div>
   161	              <div className="space-y-2"><Label htmlFor="location">Location</Label><Input id="location" value={formData.location} onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, location: event.target.value })} required pattern="^[A-Za-z ,.-]{2,100}$" /></div>
   162	            </div>
   163	
   164	            <FaceCapture nid={formData.nationalId} onCaptureComplete={(success) => setBiometricStatus((previous) => ({ ...previous, faceCaptured: success }))} />
   165	            {biometricStatus.faceCaptured && (
   166	              <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={handleTrainFace} disabled={biometricStatus.isTraining}>
   167	                {biometricStatus.isTraining ? "Training Face..." : biometricStatus.faceTrainingProgress === 100 ? "Face Trained" : "Train Face"}
   168	              </Button>
   169	            )}
   170	            <FingerprintCapture
   171	              nid={formData.nationalId}
   172	              isCaptured={biometricStatus.fingerprintCaptured}
   173	              onCapture={() => setBiometricStatus((previous) => ({ ...previous, fingerprintCaptured: true }))}
   174	              onRetake={() => setBiometricStatus((previous) => ({ ...previous, fingerprintCaptured: false }))}
   175	            />
   176	
   177	            {error && <Alert variant="destructive"><AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription></Alert>}
   178	            <Button type="submit" disabled={isLoading || !registrationOpen || !isAdmin} className="w-full">
   179	              {isLoading ? "Registering..." : "Register Voter"}
   180	            </Button>
   181	          </form>
   182	        </CardContent>
   183	      </Card>
   184	    </div>
   185	  )
   186	}

FILE: votechain-frontend-ts/src/components/Voter/VoteCasting.tsx

     1	import {
     2	  useCallback,
     3	  useEffect,
     4	  useState,
     5	} from "react"
     6	import {
     7	  useContract,
     8	} from "@/hooks/useContract"
     9	import {
    10	  useWallet,
    11	} from "@/components/ui/wallet-provider"
    12	import {
    13	  Card,
    14	  CardContent,
    15	  CardHeader,
    16	  CardTitle,
    17	} from "@/components/ui/card"
    18	import {
    19	  Button,
    20	} from "@/components/ui/button"
    21	import {
    22	  Alert,
    23	  AlertDescription,
    24	} from "@/components/ui/alert"
    25	import {
    26	  Badge,
    27	} from "@/components/ui/badge"
    28	import {
    29	  useToast,
    30	} from "@/hooks/use-toast"
    31	import {
    32	  AlertCircle,
    33	  CheckCircle2,

    35	  MapPin,
    36	  RefreshCw,
    37	  ShieldCheck,
    38	  User,
    39	  Vote,
    40	} from "lucide-react"
    41	import {
    42	  VerificationModal,
    43	} from "@/components/Biometric/VerificationModal"
    44	import {
    45	  getWp5PublicConfig,
    46	  type Wp5Authorization,
    47	  type Wp5Candidate,
    48	  type Wp5PublicConfig,
    49	} from "@/services/api"
    50	import {
    51	  prepareWp5EncryptedBallot,
    52	  resetWp5PrivacySession,
    53	} from "@/privacy/wp5PrivacyClient"
    54	
    55	const PHASE_LABELS:
    56	  Record<number, string> = {
    57	    0: "Registration",
    58	    1: "Active",
    59	    2: "Ended",
    60	    3: "Finalized",
    61	  }
    62	
    63	export function VoteCasting() {
    64	  const {
    65	    isWalletChecked,
    66	    isConnected,
    67	    address,
    68	  } =
    69	    useWallet()
    70	
    71	  const {
    72	    isAdmin,
    73	    castEncryptedVote,
    74	  } =
    75	    useContract()
    76	
    77	  const {
    78	    toast,
    79	  } =
    80	    useToast()
    81	
    82	  const [
    83	    data,
    84	    setData,
    85	  ] =
    86	    useState<Wp5PublicConfig | null>(
    87	      null,
    88	    )
    89	
    90	  const [
    91	    loading,
    92	    setLoading,
    93	  ] =
    94	    useState(true)
    95	
    96	  const [
    97	    error,
    98	    setError,
    99	  ] =
   100	    useState<string | null>(
   101	      null,
   102	    )
   103	
   104	  const [
   105	    selectedCandidate,
   106	    setSelectedCandidate,
   107	  ] =
   108	    useState<number | null>(
   109	      null,
   110	    )
   111	
   112	  const [
   113	    pendingCandidate,
   114	    setPendingCandidate,
   115	  ] =
   116	    useState<number | null>(
   117	      null,
   118	    )
   119	
   120	  const [
   121	    votedCandidate,
   122	    setVotedCandidate,
   123	  ] =
   124	    useState<Wp5Candidate | null>(
   125	      null,
   126	    )
   127	
   128	  const [
   129	    transactionHash,
   130	    setTransactionHash,
   131	  ] =
   132	    useState<string | null>(
   133	      null,
   134	    )
   135	
   136	  const [
   137	    isVoting,
   138	    setIsVoting,
   139	  ] =
   140	    useState(false)
   141	
   142	  const [
   143	    isModalOpen,
   144	    setIsModalOpen,
   145	  ] =
   146	    useState(false)
   147	
   148	  const refresh =
   149	    useCallback(
   150	      async () => {
   151	        setLoading(true)
   152	        setError(null)
   153	
   154	        try {
   155	          setData(
   156	            await getWp5PublicConfig(),
   157	          )
   158	        } catch (
   159	          requestError
   160	        ) {
   161	          setError(
   162	            requestError
   163	              instanceof Error
   164	              ? requestError.message
   165	              : "Failed to fetch VoteChain-ZK election configuration",
   166	          )
   167	        } finally {
   168	          setLoading(false)
   169	        }
   170	      },
   171	      [],
   172	    )
   173	
   174	  useEffect(() => {
   175	    void refresh()
   176	  }, [refresh])
   177	
   178	  useEffect(
   179	    () => {
   180	      return () => {
   181	        resetWp5PrivacySession()
   182	      }
   183	    },
   184	    [],
   185	  )
   186	
   187	  const closeModal =
   188	    () => {
   189	      if (isVoting) {
   190	        return
   191	      }
   192	
   193	      resetWp5PrivacySession()
   194	      setIsModalOpen(false)
   195	      setPendingCandidate(null)
   196	      setSelectedCandidate(null)
   197	    }
   198	
   199	  const handleVote =
   200	    (
   201	      candidateId:
   202	        number,
   203	    ) => {
   204	      if (!isConnected) {
   205	        toast({
   206	          title:
   207	            "Wallet Not Connected",
   208	          description:
   209	            "Connect the government-funded vote-submission wallet before voting.",
   210	          variant:
   211	            "destructive",
   212	        })
   213	
   214	        return
   215	      }
   216	
   217	      if (isAdmin) {
   218	        toast({
   219	          title:
   220	            "Election admin wallet cannot use voter UI",
   221	          description:
   222	            "Use the designated non-admin vote-submission wallet.",
   223	          variant:
   224	            "destructive",
   225	        })
   226	
   227	        return
   228	      }
   229	
   230	      if (
   231	        !data ||
   232	        !data.configured ||
   233	        data.phase !== 1
   234	      ) {
   235	        toast({
   236	          title:
   237	            "Voting unavailable",
   238	          description:
   239	            `Voting requires an active configured VoteChain-ZK election. Current phase: ${
   240	              PHASE_LABELS[
   241	                data?.phase ??
   242	                -1
   243	              ] ??
   244	              "unknown"
   245	            }.`,
   246	          variant:
   247	            "destructive",
   248	        })
   249	
   250	        return
   251	      }
   252	
   253	      /*
   254	       * The selected candidate remains local.
   255	       * VerificationModal never receives it.
   256	       */
   257	      setPendingCandidate(
   258	        candidateId,
   259	      )
   260	
   261	      setIsModalOpen(
   262	        true,
   263	      )
   264	    }
   265	
   266	  const handleVerified =
   267	    async (
   268	      authorization:
   269	        Wp5Authorization,
   270	    ) => {
   271	      if (
   272	        pendingCandidate ===
   273	          null ||
   274	        !data
   275	      ) {
   276	        throw new Error(
   277	          "Local candidate selection is unavailable",
   278	        )
   279	      }
   280	
   281	      const candidateId =
   282	        pendingCandidate
   283	
   284	      const candidate =
   285	        data.candidates.find(
   286	          item =>
   287	            item.candidateId ===
   288	            candidateId,
   289	        ) ?? null
   290	
   291	      try {
   292	        setIsVoting(true)
   293	        setSelectedCandidate(
   294	          candidateId,
   295	        )
   296	
   297	        /*
   298	         * Candidate plaintext enters only the isolated
   299	         * local privacy worker here.
   300	         */
   301	        const ballot =
   302	          await prepareWp5EncryptedBallot(
   303	            candidateId,
   304	            authorization,
   305	            data,
   306	          )
   307	
   308	        /*
   309	         * The wallet receives only proof, nullifier and
   310	         * ciphertext coordinates.
   311	         */
   312	        const receipt =
   313	          await castEncryptedVote(
   314	            ballot,
   315	          )
   316	
   317	        setVotedCandidate(
   318	          candidate,
   319	        )
   320	
   321	        setTransactionHash(
   322	          receipt?.hash ??
   323	          null,
   324	        )
   325	
   326	        toast({
   327	          title:
   328	            "Encrypted Vote Accepted",
   329	          description:
   330	            "The privacy-preserving ballot was recorded on-chain.",
   331	        })
   332	
   333	        setIsModalOpen(false)
   334	        setPendingCandidate(null)
   335	
   336	        await refresh()
   337	      } catch (
   338	        voteError
   339	      ) {
   340	        console.error(
   341	          "Private vote transaction failed",
   342	          voteError,
   343	        )
   344	
   345	        toast({
   346	          title:
   347	            "Vote Failed",
   348	          description:
   349	            voteError
   350	              instanceof Error
   351	              ? voteError.message
   352	              : "There was an error preparing or submitting the encrypted ballot.",
   353	          variant:
   354	            "destructive",
   355	        })
   356	
   357	        throw voteError
   358	      } finally {
   359	        setIsVoting(false)
   360	        setSelectedCandidate(null)
   361	
   362	        /*
   363	         * Terminates the worker and destroys the
   364	         * ephemeral credential-release key reference.
   365	         */
   366	        resetWp5PrivacySession()
   367	      }
   368	    }
   369	
   370	  const modal = (
   371	    <VerificationModal
   372	      open={
   373	        isModalOpen
   374	      }
   375	      onClose={
   376	        closeModal
   377	      }
   378	      onVerified={
   379	        handleVerified
   380	      }
   381	    />
   382	  )
   383	
   384	  if (
   385	    !isWalletChecked ||
   386	    !isConnected ||
   387	    loading
   388	  ) {
   389	    return (
   390	      <>
   391	        {modal}
   392	
   393	        <div className="container mx-auto p-4 max-w-4xl">
   394	          <Card>
   395	            <CardHeader>
   396	              <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
   397	                <Vote className="h-6 w-6" />
   398	                Cast Your Vote
   399	              </CardTitle>
   400	            </CardHeader>
   401	
   402	            <CardContent className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   403	              <Loader2 className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin" />
   404	            </CardContent>
   405	          </Card>
   406	        </div>
   407	      </>
   408	    )
   409	  }
   410	
   411	  if (
   412	    error ||
   413	    !data
   414	  ) {
   415	    return (
   416	      <>
   417	        {modal}
   418	
   419	        <div className="container mx-auto p-4 max-w-4xl">
   420	          <Card>
   421	            <CardHeader>
   422	              <CardTitle>
   423	                Cast Your Vote
   424	              </CardTitle>
   425	            </CardHeader>
   426	
   427	            <CardContent className="text-center py-12">
   428	              <AlertCircle className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto text-destructive" />
   429	
   430	              <p className="text-destructive mt-3">
   431	                {error ||
   432	                  "VoteChain-ZK election data unavailable"}
   433	              </p>
   434	
   435	              <Button
   436	                onClick={
   437	                  refresh
   438	                }
   439	                variant="outline"
   440	                className="mt-4"
   441	              >
   442	                <RefreshCw className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
   443	                Try Again
   444	              </Button>
   445	            </CardContent>
   446	          </Card>
   447	        </div>
   448	      </>
   449	    )
   450	  }
   451	
   452	  if (
   453	    votedCandidate
   454	  ) {
   455	    return (
   456	      <>
   457	        {modal}
   458	
   459	        <div className="container mx-auto p-4 max-w-2xl">
   460	          <Card>
   461	            <CardHeader className="text-center">
   462	              <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-green-600 mb-2">
   463	                <CheckCircle2 className="h-8 w-8" />
   464	
   465	                <CardTitle className="text-2xl">
   466	                  Encrypted Vote Recorded
   467	                </CardTitle>
   468	              </div>
   469	            </CardHeader>
   470	
   471	            <CardContent className="text-center space-y-4">
   472	              <p>
   473	                Your locally selected vote for{" "}
   474	                <strong>
   475	                  {votedCandidate.name}
   476	                </strong>{" "}
   477	                was submitted as an encrypted ballot.
   478	              </p>
   479	
   480	              {transactionHash && (
   481	                <Badge
   482	                  variant="secondary"
   483	                  className="break-all"
   484	                >
   485	                  Transaction:{" "}
   486	                  {transactionHash}
   487	                </Badge>
   488	              )}
   489	            </CardContent>
   490	          </Card>
   491	        </div>
   492	      </>
   493	    )
   494	  }
   495	
   496	  const phaseLabel =
   497	    PHASE_LABELS[
   498	      data.phase
   499	    ] ??
   500	    "Unknown"
   501	
   502	  const votingEnabled =
   503	    data.configured &&
   504	    data.phase === 1 &&
   505	    !isAdmin
   506	
   507	  return (
   508	    <>
   509	      {modal}
   510	
   511	      <div className="container mx-auto p-4 max-w-4xl">
   512	        <Card className="mb-6">
   513	          <CardHeader>
   514	            <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
   515	              <Vote className="h-6 w-6" />
   516	
   517	              Cast Your Vote
   518	
   519	              <Badge
   520	                variant={
   521	                  data.phase === 1
   522	                    ? "default"
   523	                    : "secondary"
   524	                }
   525	              >
   526	                {phaseLabel}
   527	              </Badge>
   528	            </CardTitle>
   529	          </CardHeader>
   530	
   531	          <CardContent>
   532	            <Alert>
   533	              <ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4" />
   534	
   535	              <AlertDescription>
   536	                Candidate selection remains on this terminal.
   537	                Biometric services receive no selected candidate.
   538	                The local privacy worker creates the one-hot ballot,
   539	                BabyJub ciphertexts and DIRECT Groth16 proof before
   540	                the wallet submits the encrypted transaction.
   541	              </AlertDescription>
   542	            </Alert>
   543	          </CardContent>
   544	        </Card>
   545	
   546	        {data.candidates.length === 0
   547	          ? (
   548	            <Card>
   549	              <CardContent className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   550	                <div className="text-center space-y-4">
   551	                  <User className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto text-muted-foreground" />
   552	
   553	                  <p className="text-muted-foreground">
   554	                    No registered candidates were found in the
   555	                    frozen election manifest.
   556	                  </p>
   557	                </div>
   558	              </CardContent>
   559	            </Card>
   560	          )
   561	          : (
   562	            <div className="grid gap-6">
   563	              {data.candidates.map(
   564	                candidate => {
   565	                  const isCurrentlyVoting =
   566	                    isVoting &&
   567	                    selectedCandidate ===
   568	                      candidate.candidateId
   569	
   570	                  return (
   571	                    <Card
   572	                      key={
   573	                        candidate.candidateId
   574	                      }
   575	                      className="transition-all hover:shadow-md"
   576	                    >
   577	                      <CardHeader>
   578	                        <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
   579	                          <div className="space-y-2">
   580	                            <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
   581	                              <User className="h-5 w-5" />
   582	                              {candidate.name}
   583	                            </CardTitle>
   584	
   585	                            <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
   586	                              Candidate #
   587	                              {candidate.candidateId}
   588	                              {" · "}
   589	                              {candidate.party}
   590	                            </div>
   591	
   592	                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground">
   593	                              <MapPin className="h-4 w-4" />
   594	                              <span>
   595	                                {candidate.location}
   596	                              </span>
   597	                            </div>
   598	                          </div>
   599	
   600	                          <Badge variant="outline">
   601	                            Encrypted tally
   602	                          </Badge>
   603	                        </div>
   604	                      </CardHeader>
   605	
   606	                      <CardContent>
   607	                        <Button
   608	                          onClick={() =>
   609	                            handleVote(
   610	                              candidate.candidateId,
   611	                            )
   612	                          }
   613	                          disabled={
   614	                            !votingEnabled ||
   615	                            isVoting
   616	                          }
   617	                          className="w-full"
   618	                          size="lg"
   619	                        >
   620	                          {isCurrentlyVoting
   621	                            ? (
   622	                              <>
   623	                                <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
   624	                                Preparing Private Vote...
   625	                              </>
   626	                            )
   627	                            : (
   628	                              <>
   629	                                <Vote className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
   630	                                Vote for{" "}
   631	                                {candidate.name}
   632	                              </>
   633	                            )}
   634	                        </Button>
   635	                      </CardContent>
   636	                    </Card>
   637	                  )
   638	                },
   639	              )}
   640	            </div>
   641	          )}
   642	
   643	        <Card className="mt-6">
   644	          <CardContent className="pt-6">
   645	            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-muted-foreground">
   646	              <span>
   647	                Submission Wallet:{" "}
   648	                {address?.slice(0, 6)}
   649	                ...
   650	                {address?.slice(-4)}
   651	              </span>
   652	
   653	              <span>
   654	                Accepted encrypted ballots:{" "}
   655	                {data.acceptedBallotCount}
   656	              </span>
   657	            </div>
   658	          </CardContent>
   659	        </Card>
   660	      </div>
   661	    </>
   662	  )
   663	}

============================== END FILE ==============================

     1	/// <reference lib="webworker" />

     3	import {
     4	  Buffer,
     5	} from "buffer"
     6	
     7	import appProfile from "./wp5-application-profile.json"
     8	
     9	import type {
    10	  Wp5Authorization,
    11	  Wp5PublicConfig,
    12	} from "../services/api"

    14	/*
    15	 * circomlibjs/ffjavascript still exercise Buffer-backed code
    16	 * in the browser bundle.
    17	 *
    18	 * This assignment MUST happen before circomlibjs/snarkjs are
    19	 * evaluated, so those packages are loaded dynamically below.
    20	 */
    21	;(globalThis as any).Buffer = Buffer
    22	
    23	/*
    24	 * Lazy browser crypto loading.
    25	 *
    26	 * Buffer is assigned above before either dependency is evaluated.
    27	 * Keeping the dynamic imports inside async functions avoids
    28	 * top-level await while preserving browser-first initialization.
    29	 */
    30	async function buildPoseidon() {
    31	  const circomlib: any =
    32	    await import(
    33	      "circomlibjs"
    34	    )
    35	
    36	  return circomlib.buildPoseidon()
    37	}
    38	
    39	async function buildBabyjub() {
    40	  const circomlib: any =
    41	    await import(
    42	      "circomlibjs"
    43	    )
    44	
    45	  return circomlib.buildBabyjub()
    46	}
    47	
    48	async function loadGroth16() {
    49	  const snarkjs: any =
    50	    await import(
    51	      "snarkjs"
    52	    )
    53	
    54	  return snarkjs.groth16
    55	}
    56	
    57	const groth16: any = {
    58	  async fullProve(
    59	    ...args: any[]
    60	  ) {
    61	    const api =
    62	      await loadGroth16()
    63	
    64	    return api.fullProve(
    65	      ...args
    66	    )
    67	  },
    68	
    69	  async verify(
    70	    ...args: any[]
    71	  ) {
    72	    const api =
    73	      await loadGroth16()
    74	
    75	    return api.verify(
    76	      ...args
    77	    )
    78	  },
    79	
    80	  async exportSolidityCallData(
    81	    ...args: any[]
    82	  ) {
    83	    const api =
    84	      await loadGroth16()
    85	
    86	    return api.exportSolidityCallData(
    87	      ...args
    88	    )
    89	  },
    90	}

    92	const MAX =
    93	  Number(appProfile.maxCandidates)
    94	
    95	const DEPTH =
    96	  Number(appProfile.merkleDepth)
    97	
    98	const FIELD =
    99	  BigInt(appProfile.fieldModulus)

   102	  BigInt(
   103	    appProfile.babyjub.subOrder,

   126	let sessionKeyPair:
   127	  CryptoKeyPair | null =
   128	    null

   130	let authorizationSessionState:
   131	  "idle" |
   132	  "initializing" |
   133	  "ready" |

   135	    "idle"

   145	function getPoseidon() {
   146	  if (!poseidonPromise) {
   147	    poseidonPromise =
   148	      buildPoseidon()
   149	  }
   150	
   151	  return poseidonPromise
   152	}
   153	
   154	function getBabyjub() {
   155	  if (!babyjubPromise) {
   156	    babyjubPromise =
   157	      buildBabyjub()
   158	  }
   159	
   160	  return babyjubPromise
   161	}
   162	
   163	function fromBase64(
   164	  value: string,
   165	) {
   166	  const raw =
   167	    atob(value)
   168	
   169	  const out =
   170	    new Uint8Array(
   171	      raw.length,
   172	    )
   173	
   174	  for (
   175	    let i = 0;
   176	    i < raw.length;
   177	    i += 1
   178	  ) {
   179	    out[i] =
   180	      raw.charCodeAt(i)
   181	  }
   182	
   183	  return out
   184	}
   185	
   186	function randomScalar() {
   187	  const bytes =
   188	    new Uint8Array(32)
   189	
   190	  crypto.getRandomValues(
   191	    bytes,
   192	  )
   193	
   194	  let value = 0n
   195	
   196	  for (const byte of bytes) {
   197	    value =
   198	      (value << 8n) |
   199	      BigInt(byte)
   200	  }
   201	
   202	  return (
   203	    value %
   204	    (SUBORDER - 1n)
   205	  ) + 1n
   206	}
   207	
   208	async function H(
   209	  values:
   210	    Array<
   211	      string |
   212	      number |
   213	      bigint
   214	    >,
   215	) {
   216	  const poseidon =
   217	    await getPoseidon()
   218	
   219	  return BigInt(
   220	    poseidon.F.toObject(
   221	      poseidon(
   222	        values.map(
   223	          value =>
   224	            BigInt(value),
   225	        ),
   226	      ),
   227	    ),
   228	  )
   229	}
   230	
   231	async function computeManifest(
   232	  candidateIds: number[],
   233	) {
   234	  if (
   235	    candidateIds.length < 1 ||
   236	    candidateIds.length > MAX
   237	  ) {
   238	    throw new Error(
   239	      "Candidate count is outside the frozen WP5 range",
   240	    )
   241	  }
   242	
   243	  if (
   244	    appProfile
   245	      .candidateManifest
   246	      .algorithm !==
   247	    "POSEIDON_INDEXED_CHAIN_V1"
   248	  ) {
   249	    throw new Error(
   250	      "Unexpected WP5 candidate-manifest algorithm",
   251	    )
   252	  }
   253	
   254	  let state =
   255	    await H([
   256	      D_MANIFEST,
   257	      BigInt(
   258	        candidateIds.length,
   259	      ),
   260	    ])
   261	
   262	  for (
   263	    let index = 0;
   264	    index < candidateIds.length;
   265	    index += 1
   266	  ) {
   267	    state =
   268	      await H([
   269	        state,
   270	        BigInt(index),
   271	        BigInt(
   272	          candidateIds[index],
   273	        ),
   274	      ])
   275	  }
   276	
   277	  return state
   278	}
   279	
   280	async function reconstructRoot(
   281	  commitment: bigint,
   282	  siblings: string[],
   283	  pathBits: string[],
   284	) {
   285	  if (
   286	    siblings.length !== DEPTH ||
   287	    pathBits.length !== DEPTH
   288	  ) {
   289	    throw new Error(
   290	      "Invalid WP5 Merkle witness depth",
   291	    )
   292	  }
   293	
   294	  let current =
   295	    commitment
   296	
   297	  for (
   298	    let i = 0;
   299	    i < DEPTH;
   300	    i += 1
   301	  ) {
   302	    const bit =
   303	      BigInt(pathBits[i])
   304	
   305	    const sibling =
   306	      BigInt(siblings[i])
   307	
   308	    if (
   309	      bit !== 0n &&
   310	      bit !== 1n
   311	    ) {
   312	      throw new Error(
   313	        "Invalid Merkle path bit",
   314	      )
   315	    }
   316	
   317	    current =
   318	      bit === 0n
   319	        ? await H([
   320	            current,
   321	            sibling,
   322	          ])
   323	        : await H([
   324	            sibling,
   325	            current,
   326	          ])
   327	  }
   328	
   329	  return current
   330	}
   331	
   332	async function beginAuthorization() {
   333	  if (
   334	    authorizationSessionState !==
   335	      "idle" ||
   336	    sessionKeyPair
   337	  ) {
   338	    throw new Error(
   339	      "A WP5 authorization session is already active",
   340	    )
   341	  }
   342	
   343	  authorizationSessionState =
   344	    "initializing"
   345	
   346	  try {
   347	    sessionKeyPair =
   348	      await crypto.subtle.generateKey(
   349	        {
   350	          name: "ECDH",
   351	          namedCurve: "P-256",
   352	        },
   353	        true,
   354	        ["deriveBits"],
   355	      )
   356	
   357	    const publicKey =
   358	      await crypto.subtle.exportKey(
   359	        "jwk",
   360	        sessionKeyPair.publicKey,
   361	      )
   362	
   363	    authorizationSessionState =
   364	      "ready"
   365	
   366	    return {
   367	      credentialPublicKey:
   368	        publicKey,
   369	    }
   370	  } catch (error) {
   371	    sessionKeyPair =
   372	      null
   373	
   374	    authorizationSessionState =
   375	      "idle"
   376	
   377	    throw error
   378	  }
   379	}
   380	
   381	async function unwrapCredential(
   382	  authorization:
   383	    Wp5Authorization,
   384	) {
   385	  if (!sessionKeyPair) {
   386	    throw new Error(
   387	      "Credential-release session is missing",
   388	    )
   389	  }
   390	
   391	  const envelope =
   392	    authorization
   393	      .credentialEnvelope
   394	
   395	  if (
   396	    envelope.version !== 1 ||
   397	    envelope.keyAgreement !==
   398	      "ECDH-P256-HKDF-SHA256" ||
   399	    envelope.contentEncryption !==
   400	      "AES-256-GCM"
   401	  ) {
   402	    throw new Error(
   403	      "Unsupported credential envelope",
   404	    )
   405	  }
   406	
   407	  if (
   408	    Date.now() >
   409	    authorization.expiresAt ||
   410	    Date.now() >
   411	    envelope.expiresAt
   412	  ) {
   413	    throw new Error(
   414	      "Biometric authorization expired",
   415	    )
   416	  }
   417	
   418	  const expectedAad =
   419	    [
   420	      "VoteChain-ZK/WP5/v1",
   421	      authorization
   422	        .membership
   423	        .identityCommitment,
   424	      authorization.electionId,
   425	      String(
   426	        envelope.expiresAt,
   427	      ),
   428	    ].join("|")
   429	
   430	  const aad =
   431	    fromBase64(
   432	      envelope.aad,
   433	    )
   434	
   435	  const decodedAad =
   436	    new TextDecoder()
   437	      .decode(aad)
   438	
   439	  if (
   440	    decodedAad !==
   441	    expectedAad
   442	  ) {
   443	    throw new Error(
   444	      "Credential-envelope context mismatch",
   445	    )
   446	  }
   447	
   448	  const serverPublicKey =
   449	    await crypto.subtle.importKey(
   450	      "jwk",
   451	      envelope.serverPublicKey,
   452	      {
   453	        name: "ECDH",
   454	        namedCurve: "P-256",
   455	      },
   456	      false,
   457	      [],
   458	    )
   459	
   460	  const shared =
   461	    await crypto.subtle.deriveBits(
   462	      {
   463	        name: "ECDH",
   464	        public:
   465	          serverPublicKey,
   466	      },
   467	      sessionKeyPair.privateKey,
   468	      256,
   469	    )
   470	
   471	  const ikm =
   472	    await crypto.subtle.importKey(
   473	      "raw",
   474	      shared,
   475	      "HKDF",
   476	      false,
   477	      ["deriveKey"],
   478	    )
   479	
   480	  const aesKey =
   481	    await crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
   482	      {
   483	        name: "HKDF",
   484	        hash: "SHA-256",
   485	        salt:
   486	          fromBase64(
   487	            envelope.salt,
   488	          ),
   489	        info:
   490	          new TextEncoder()
   491	            .encode(
   492	              "VoteChain-ZK/WP5/credential-release/v1",
   493	            ),
   494	      },
   495	      ikm,
   496	      {
   497	        name: "AES-GCM",
   498	        length: 256,
   499	      },
   500	      false,
   501	      ["decrypt"],
   502	    )
   503	
   504	  const plaintext =
   505	    await crypto.subtle.decrypt(
   506	      {
   507	        name: "AES-GCM",
   508	        iv:
   509	          fromBase64(
   510	            envelope.iv,
   511	          ),
   512	        additionalData:
   513	          aad,
   514	      },
   515	      aesKey,
   516	      fromBase64(
   517	        envelope.ciphertext,
   518	      ),
   519	    )
   520	
   521	  const secretText =
   522	    new TextDecoder()
   523	      .decode(plaintext)
   524	
   525	  if (
   526	    !/^\d+$/.test(
   527	      secretText,
   528	    )
   529	  ) {
   530	    throw new Error(
   531	      "Released WP5 credential is malformed",
   532	    )
   533	  }
   534	
   535	  const secret =
   536	    BigInt(secretText)
   537	
   538	  if (
   539	    secret <= 0n ||
   540	    secret >= FIELD
   541	  ) {
   542	    throw new Error(
   543	      "Released WP5 credential is outside the field",
   544	    )
   545	  }
   546	
   547	  return secret
   548	}
   549	
   550	function pointBig(
   551	  babyjub: any,
   552	  point: any[],
   553	) {
   554	  return [
   555	    BigInt(
   556	      babyjub.F.toObject(
   557	        point[0],
   558	      ),
   559	    ),
   560	    BigInt(
   561	      babyjub.F.toObject(
   562	        point[1],
   563	      ),
   564	    ),
   565	  ]
   566	}
   567	
   568	function normalizeScalar(
   569	  value: unknown,
   570	) {
   571	  return BigInt(
   572	    String(value),
   573	  ).toString()
   574	}
   575	
   576	async function prepareBallot(
   577	  candidateId: number,
   578	  authorization:
   579	    Wp5Authorization,
   580	  publicConfig:
   581	    Wp5PublicConfig,
   582	) {
   583	  if (
   584	    authorizationSessionState !==
   585	      "ready" ||
   586	    !sessionKeyPair
   587	  ) {
   588	    throw new Error(
   589	      "WP5 authorization session is not available for ballot preparation",
   590	    )
   591	  }
   592	
   593	  /*
   594	   * Transition synchronously before the first await so a second
   595	   * PREPARE_BALLOT message cannot consume the same credential session.
   596	   */
   597	  authorizationSessionState =
   598	    "consuming"
   599	
   600	  let secret = 0n
   601	
   602	  const randomness:
   603	    bigint[] =
   604	    Array.from(
   605	      { length: MAX },
   606	      () => 0n,
   607	    )
   608	
   609	  try {
   610	    if (
   611	      !Number.isSafeInteger(
   612	        candidateId,
   613	      ) ||
   614	      candidateId <= 0
   615	    ) {
   616	      throw new Error(
   617	        "Invalid locally selected candidate",
   618	      )
   619	    }
   620	
   621	    if (
   622	      !publicConfig.configured ||
   623	      Number(
   624	        publicConfig.phase,
   625	      ) !== 1
   626	    ) {
   627	      throw new Error(
   628	        "Election is not active",
   629	      )
   630	    }
   631	
   632	    const candidateCount =
   633	      Number(
   634	        publicConfig
   635	          .config
   636	          .candidateCount,
   637	      )
   638	
   639	    if (
   640	      !Number.isSafeInteger(
   641	        candidateCount,
   642	      ) ||
   643	      candidateCount < 1 ||
   644	      candidateCount > MAX ||
   645	      candidateCount !==
   646	        publicConfig
   647	          .candidates
   648	          .length
   649	    ) {
   650	      throw new Error(
   651	        "Public candidate count is inconsistent",
   652	      )
   653	    }
   654	
   655	    const candidateIndex =
   656	      publicConfig
   657	        .candidates
   658	        .findIndex(
   659	          candidate =>
   660	            candidate
   661	              .candidateId ===
   662	            candidateId,
   663	        )
   664	
   665	    if (
   666	      candidateIndex < 0 ||
   667	      candidateIndex >=
   668	        candidateCount
   669	    ) {
   670	      throw new Error(
   671	        "Selected candidate is not in the frozen manifest",
   672	      )
   673	    }
   674	
   675	    const candidateIds =
   676	      publicConfig
   677	        .candidates
   678	        .map(
   679	          candidate =>
   680	            candidate
   681	              .candidateId,
   682	        )
   683	
   684	    const manifest =
   685	      await computeManifest(
   686	        candidateIds,
   687	      )
   688	
   689	    if (
   690	      manifest !==
   691	      BigInt(
   692	        publicConfig
   693	          .config
   694	          .candidateManifestHash,
   695	      )
   696	    ) {
   697	      throw new Error(
   698	        "Candidate manifest does not match the contract",
   699	      )
   700	    }
   701	
   702	    const electionId =
   703	      BigInt(
   704	        publicConfig
   705	          .config
   706	          .electionId,
   707	      )
   708	
   709	    if (
   710	      authorization.electionId !==
   711	      electionId.toString()
   712	    ) {
   713	      throw new Error(
   714	        "Authorization belongs to another election",
   715	      )
   716	    }
   717	
   718	    if (
   719	      authorization
   720	        .membership
   721	        .eligibleRoot !==
   722	      publicConfig
   723	        .config
   724	        .eligibleRoot
   725	    ) {
   726	      throw new Error(
   727	        "Authorization Merkle root does not match election configuration",
   728	      )
   729	    }
   730	
   731	    secret =
   732	      await unwrapCredential(
   733	        authorization,
   734	      )
   735	
   736	    const commitment =
   737	      await H([
   738	        D_ID,
   739	        secret,
   740	      ])
   741	
   742	    if (
   743	      commitment.toString() !==
   744	      authorization
   745	        .membership
   746	        .identityCommitment
   747	    ) {
   748	      throw new Error(
   749	        "Released credential does not match voter commitment",
   750	      )
   751	    }
   752	
   753	    const reconstructedRoot =
   754	      await reconstructRoot(
   755	        commitment,
   756	        authorization
   757	          .membership
   758	          .siblings,
   759	        authorization
   760	          .membership
   761	          .pathBits,
   762	      )
   763	
   764	    if (
   765	      reconstructedRoot.toString() !==
   766	      publicConfig
   767	        .config
   768	        .eligibleRoot
   769	    ) {
   770	      throw new Error(
   771	        "Merkle witness does not reconstruct the configured root",
   772	      )
   773	    }
   774	
   775	    const context =
   776	      await H([
   777	        D_CTX,
   778	        electionId,
   779	        manifest,
   780	        BigInt(candidateCount),
   781	        BigInt(
   782	          publicConfig
   783	            .config
   784	            .contractDomain,
   785	        ),
   786	        BigInt(
   787	          publicConfig
   788	            .config
   789	            .Yx,
   790	        ),
   791	        BigInt(
   792	          publicConfig
   793	            .config
   794	            .Yy,
   795	        ),
   796	      ])
   797	
   798	    if (
   799	      context.toString() !==
   800	      publicConfig
   801	        .config
   802	        .electionContextHash
   803	    ) {
   804	      throw new Error(
   805	        "Election context hash mismatch",
   806	      )
   807	    }
   808	
   809	    const nullifier =
   810	      await H([
   811	        D_NULL,
   812	        secret,
   813	        electionId,
   814	      ])
   815	
   816	    const babyjub =
   817	      await getBabyjub()
   818	
   819	    const Y = [
   820	      babyjub.F.e(
   821	        BigInt(
   822	          publicConfig
   823	            .config
   824	            .Yx,
   825	        ),
   826	      ),
   827	      babyjub.F.e(
   828	        BigInt(
   829	          publicConfig
   830	            .config
   831	            .Yy,
   832	        ),
   833	      ),
   834	    ]
   835	
   836	    if (
   837	      typeof babyjub.inCurve ===
   838	        "function" &&
   839	      !babyjub.inCurve(Y)
   840	    ) {
   841	      throw new Error(
   842	        "Election BabyJub public key is not on curve",
   843	      )
   844	    }
   845	
   846	    if (
   847	      typeof babyjub.inSubgroup ===
   848	        "function" &&
   849	      !babyjub.inSubgroup(Y)
   850	    ) {
   851	      throw new Error(
   852	        "Election BabyJub public key is outside the prime subgroup",
   853	      )
   854	    }
   855	
   856	    const bits =
   857	      Array.from(
   858	        { length: MAX },
   859	        (_unused, index) =>
   860	          index ===
   861	          candidateIndex
   862	            ? 1n
   863	            : 0n,
   864	      )
   865	
   866	    const identityPoint = [
   867	      babyjub.F.e(0),
   868	      babyjub.F.e(1),
   869	    ]
   870	
   871	    const Rx: bigint[] = []
   872	    const Ry: bigint[] = []
   873	    const Sx: bigint[] = []
   874	    const Sy: bigint[] = []
   875	
   876	    for (
   877	      let slot = 0;
   878	      slot < MAX;
   879	      slot += 1
   880	    ) {
   881	      const r =
   882	        randomScalar()
   883	
   884	      randomness[slot] =
   885	        r
   886	
   887	      const R =
   888	        babyjub
   889	          .mulPointEscalar(
   890	            babyjub.Base8,
   891	            r,
   892	          )
   893	
   894	      const rY =
   895	        babyjub
   896	          .mulPointEscalar(
   897	            Y,
   898	            r,
   899	          )
   900	
   901	      const message =
   902	        bits[slot] === 1n
   903	          ? babyjub.Base8
   904	          : identityPoint
   905	
   906	      const S =
   907	        babyjub.addPoint(
   908	          rY,
   909	          message,
   910	        )
   911	
   912	      const rPoint =
   913	        pointBig(
   914	          babyjub,
   915	          R,
   916	        )
   917	
   918	      const sPoint =
   919	        pointBig(
   920	          babyjub,
   921	          S,
   922	        )
   923	
   924	      Rx.push(
   925	        rPoint[0],
   926	      )
   927	
   928	      Ry.push(
   929	        rPoint[1],
   930	      )
   931	
   932	      Sx.push(
   933	        sPoint[0],
   934	      )
   935	
   936	      Sy.push(
   937	        sPoint[1],
   938	      )
   939	    }
   940	
   941	    const input = {
   942	      secret:
   943	        secret.toString(),
   944	
   945	      siblings:
   946	        authorization
   947	          .membership
   948	          .siblings,
   949	
   950	      pathBits:
   951	        authorization
   952	          .membership
   953	          .pathBits,
   954	
   955	      bit:
   956	        bits.map(
   957	          value =>
   958	            value.toString(),
   959	        ),
   960	
   961	      randomness:
   962	        randomness.map(
   963	          value =>
   964	            value.toString(),
   965	        ),
   966	
   967	      eligibleRoot:
   968	        publicConfig
   969	          .config
   970	          .eligibleRoot,
   971	
   972	      electionId:
   973	        electionId.toString(),
   974	
   975	      candidateManifestHash:
   976	        manifest.toString(),
   977	
   978	      candidateCount:
   979	        String(candidateCount),
   980	
   981	      contractDomain:
   982	        publicConfig
   983	          .config
   984	          .contractDomain,
   985	
   986	      Yx:
   987	        publicConfig
   988	          .config
   989	          .Yx,
   990	
   991	      Yy:
   992	        publicConfig
   993	          .config
   994	          .Yy,
   995	
   996	      electionContextHash:
   997	        context.toString(),
   998	
   999	      nullifier:
  1000	        nullifier.toString(),
  1001	
  1002	      Rx:
  1003	        Rx.map(
  1004	          value =>
  1005	            value.toString(),
  1006	        ),
  1007	
  1008	      Ry:
  1009	        Ry.map(
  1010	          value =>
  1011	            value.toString(),
  1012	        ),
  1013	
  1014	      Sx:
  1015	        Sx.map(
  1016	          value =>
  1017	            value.toString(),
  1018	        ),
  1019	
  1020	      Sy:
  1021	        Sy.map(
  1022	          value =>
  1023	            value.toString(),
  1024	        ),
  1025	    }
  1026	
  1027	    const {
  1028	      proof,
  1029	      publicSignals,
  1030	    } =
  1031	      await groth16.fullProve(
  1032	        input,
  1033	        appProfile
  1034	          .directArtifacts
  1035	          .wasmUrl,
  1036	        appProfile
  1037	          .directArtifacts
  1038	          .zkeyUrl,
  1039	      )
  1040	
  1041	    if (
  1042	      !Array.isArray(
  1043	        publicSignals,
  1044	      ) ||
  1045	      publicSignals.length !== 73
  1046	    ) {
  1047	      throw new Error(
  1048	        "DIRECT prover did not return 73 public signals",
  1049	      )
  1050	    }
  1051	
  1052	    const expectedPublic = [
  1053	      publicConfig
  1054	        .config
  1055	        .eligibleRoot,
  1056	      electionId.toString(),
  1057	      manifest.toString(),
  1058	      String(candidateCount),
  1059	      publicConfig
  1060	        .config
  1061	        .contractDomain,
  1062	      publicConfig
  1063	        .config
  1064	        .Yx,
  1065	      publicConfig
  1066	        .config
  1067	        .Yy,
  1068	      context.toString(),
  1069	      nullifier.toString(),
  1070	      ...Rx.map(
  1071	        value =>
  1072	          value.toString(),
  1073	      ),
  1074	      ...Ry.map(
  1075	        value =>
  1076	          value.toString(),
  1077	      ),
  1078	      ...Sx.map(
  1079	        value =>
  1080	          value.toString(),
  1081	      ),
  1082	      ...Sy.map(
  1083	        value =>
  1084	          value.toString(),
  1085	      ),
  1086	    ]
  1087	
  1088	    if (
  1089	      expectedPublic.length !==
  1090	      73
  1091	    ) {
  1092	      throw new Error(
  1093	        "Internal DIRECT public-signal layout error",
  1094	      )
  1095	    }
  1096	
  1097	    for (
  1098	      let i = 0;
  1099	      i < 73;
  1100	      i += 1
  1101	    ) {
  1102	      if (
  1103	        BigInt(
  1104	          publicSignals[i],
  1105	        ) !==
  1106	        BigInt(
  1107	          expectedPublic[i],
  1108	        )
  1109	      ) {
  1110	        throw new Error(
  1111	          `DIRECT public signal mismatch at index ${i}`,
  1112	        )
  1113	      }
  1114	    }
  1115	
  1116	    const verificationKeyResponse =
  1117	      await fetch(
  1118	        appProfile
  1119	          .directArtifacts
  1120	          .verificationKeyUrl,
  1121	        {
  1122	          cache: "no-store",
  1123	        },
  1124	      )
  1125	
  1126	    if (
  1127	      !verificationKeyResponse.ok
  1128	    ) {
  1129	      throw new Error(
  1130	        "Unable to load DIRECT verification key",
  1131	      )
  1132	    }
  1133	
  1134	    const verificationKey =
  1135	      await verificationKeyResponse.json()
  1136	
  1137	    const locallyVerified =
  1138	      await groth16.verify(
  1139	        verificationKey,
  1140	        publicSignals,
  1141	        proof,
  1142	      )
  1143	
  1144	    if (!locallyVerified) {
  1145	      throw new Error(
  1146	        "Locally generated DIRECT proof did not verify",
  1147	      )
  1148	    }
  1149	
  1150	    const calldataText =
  1151	      await groth16
  1152	        .exportSolidityCallData(
  1153	          proof,
  1154	          publicSignals,
  1155	        )
  1156	
  1157	    const [
  1158	      rawA,
  1159	      rawB,
  1160	      rawC,
  1161	      rawPublic,
  1162	    ] =
  1163	      JSON.parse(
  1164	        `[${calldataText}]`,
  1165	      )
  1166	
  1167	    if (
  1168	      !Array.isArray(rawA) ||
  1169	      rawA.length !== 2 ||
  1170	      !Array.isArray(rawB) ||
  1171	      rawB.length !== 2 ||
  1172	      !Array.isArray(rawC) ||
  1173	      rawC.length !== 2 ||
  1174	      !Array.isArray(rawPublic) ||
  1175	      rawPublic.length !== 73
  1176	    ) {
  1177	      throw new Error(
  1178	        "Unexpected Groth16 Solidity calldata shape",
  1179	      )
  1180	    }
  1181	
  1182	    for (
  1183	      let i = 0;
  1184	      i < 73;
  1185	      i += 1
  1186	    ) {
  1187	      if (
  1188	        BigInt(rawPublic[i]) !==
  1189	        BigInt(
  1190	          publicSignals[i],
  1191	        )
  1192	      ) {
  1193	        throw new Error(
  1194	          "Groth16 calldata/public-signal mismatch",
  1195	        )
  1196	      }
  1197	    }
  1198	
  1199	    return {
  1200	      a:
  1201	        rawA.map(
  1202	          normalizeScalar,
  1203	        ),
  1204	
  1205	      b:
  1206	        rawB.map(
  1207	          (row: unknown[]) =>
  1208	            row.map(
  1209	              normalizeScalar,
  1210	            ),
  1211	        ),
  1212	
  1213	      c:
  1214	        rawC.map(
  1215	          normalizeScalar,
  1216	        ),
  1217	
  1218	      nullifier:
  1219	        nullifier.toString(),
  1220	
  1221	      rx:
  1222	        Rx.map(
  1223	          value =>
  1224	            value.toString(),
  1225	        ),
  1226	
  1227	      ry:
  1228	        Ry.map(
  1229	          value =>
  1230	            value.toString(),
  1231	        ),
  1232	
  1233	      sx:
  1234	        Sx.map(
  1235	          value =>
  1236	            value.toString(),
  1237	        ),
  1238	
  1239	      sy:
  1240	        Sy.map(
  1241	          value =>
  1242	            value.toString(),
  1243	        ),
  1244	    }
  1245	  } finally {
  1246	    secret = 0n
  1247	
  1248	    randomness.fill(
  1249	      0n,
  1250	    )
  1251	
  1252	    sessionKeyPair =
  1253	      null
  1254	
  1255	    authorizationSessionState =
  1256	      "idle"
  1257	  }
  1258	}
  1259	
  1260	type RequestMessage =
  1261	  {
  1262	    id: number
  1263	    type: "BEGIN_AUTH"
  1264	  } |
  1265	  {
  1266	    id: number
  1267	    type: "PREPARE_BALLOT"
  1268	    candidateId: number
  1269	    authorization:
  1270	      Wp5Authorization
  1271	    publicConfig:
  1272	      Wp5PublicConfig
  1273	  } |
  1274	  {
  1275	    id: number
  1276	    type: "CLEAR"
  1277	  }
  1278	
  1279	self.onmessage =
  1280	  async (
  1281	    event:
  1282	      MessageEvent<RequestMessage>,
  1283	  ) => {
  1284	    const message =
  1285	      event.data
  1286	
  1287	    try {
  1288	      let result:
  1289	        unknown
  1290	
  1291	      if (
  1292	        message.type ===
  1293	        "BEGIN_AUTH"
  1294	      ) {
  1295	        result =
  1296	          await beginAuthorization()
  1297	      } else if (
  1298	        message.type ===
  1299	        "PREPARE_BALLOT"
  1300	      ) {
  1301	        result =
  1302	          await prepareBallot(
  1303	            message.candidateId,
  1304	            message.authorization,
  1305	            message.publicConfig,
  1306	          )
  1307	      } else if (
  1308	        message.type ===
  1309	        "CLEAR"
  1310	      ) {
  1311	        sessionKeyPair =
  1312	          null
  1313	
  1314	        authorizationSessionState =
  1315	          "idle"
  1316	
  1317	        result = {
  1318	          cleared: true,
  1319	        }
  1320	      } else {
  1321	        throw new Error(
  1322	          "Unknown WP5 privacy-worker request",
  1323	        )
  1324	      }
  1325	
  1326	      self.postMessage({
  1327	        id:
  1328	          message.id,
  1329	        ok: true,
  1330	        result,
  1331	      })
  1332	    } catch (error) {
  1333	      self.postMessage({
  1334	        id:
  1335	          message.id,
  1336	        ok: false,
  1337	        error:
  1338	          error instanceof Error
  1339	            ? error.message
  1340	            : "WP5 privacy worker failed",
  1341	      })
  1342	    }
  1343	  }
  1344	
  1345	export {}
